一、文档范围与状态

本文件汇编当前项目工作目录的C/C++源码、头文件、Make配置、构建脚本、EOS系数/元数据，以及最新独立MFC黏性迁移原型。
共138个文件，原文件总字节393095。每项附原路径、SHA256、字节数及全文；源码正文按原内容整理，未重构或修改求解器。
不包含历史source_before/封存目录、checkpoint、编译对象/二进制、lnInclude链接、外部OpenFOAM/CoolProp完整源码、旧测试输出集合及案例网格。
文档是阅读/审查用源码快照，不是重新编译、部署或可直接解包的发行包。

当前项目与2C erratum_release_source存在同路径的118个纳入文件逐项SHA一致；此项只证明源码快照一致，不是一次新运行资格。
2C已完成限定Representation-V2运行资格；整体M6B/生产求解器仍PARTIAL，production restart NOT CERTIFIED。
独立MFC模块尚未接入活动求解器：PASS_VARIABLE_COEFFICIENT_WORK_KERNEL_ONLY；原生EOS/entropy/两层authority适配未完成。
原正在运行的CRV3 mu3/N4096控制器与冻结source/executable/case未停止、覆盖或重编译。

二、结构导航

1. 根目录：rhosprayVoFDPMFoam.C主入口、VOF/气相旧路径、场初始化、方程头文件。
2. serialFiveEq：一维串行五方程实际入口与恢复观测。
3. fiveEqThermo/liquidEOS：气相热力学、公压恢复依赖、正癸烷EOS v1/v2/v3与工厂。
4. fiveEqPrecision/fiveEqHighOrderPrecision：保守状态、P2/ScaledDD/稀疏authority与高阶精度工具。
5. fiveEqFlux/fiveEqReconstruction：KT波速/面通量、非保守alpha、重构、热力学时间界与可接受性。
6. fiveEqIntegration：时间推进、TD1、CRV3、species映射、runtime桥接、authority ledger与restart数据格式。
7. dpmFiveEq：原生parcel/cloud包装、支持判定、源账本及事务工具。限定测试通过不等于活动全耦合生产资格。
8. Make/Allwmake/Allwclean：项目编译链接配置；依赖OF7及项目热力学库，未在本整理中执行。
9. fitting/integration/mfc_viscosity_adoption_v1：独立牛顿应力、空气/正癸烷输运代理、变系数黏性功通量与验证。

三、MFC模块说明

固定MFC提交3dc5b2f2a1b1b9c25672aa09d4fbfabee21a91e8。
采用剪切/体积黏性张量与体积分数混合；一维纵向系数4eta/3+zeta；保留原EOS。
输运代理范围：空气600–1200K/0.1–10MPa；正癸烷280–450K/1–10MPa。
bulk=0为Stokes假设；干空气不是燃料反应混合物黏度认证；超范围拒绝，不静默外推。
PeriodicViscousWork.H为小规模周期均匀网格验证核：三速度分量、正变系数、anchor冻结系数、密集线性求解，仅N<=256；不是生产大网格求解器。
100变系数work/守恒fixture、3解析Fourier模式、1000应力耗散恒等式及4008输运参考点通过。原生两层authority/EOS恢复尚待接入。

Global
    centralCourantNo

Description
    Calculates the mean and maximum wave speed based Courant Numbers.

if (mesh.nInternalFaces())
{
    scalarField sumAmaxSf(fvc::surfaceSum(amaxSf)().internalField());

    CoNum = 0.5*gMax(sumAmaxSf/mesh.V().field())*runTime.deltaTValue();

    meanCoNum =
        0.5*(gSum(sumAmaxSf)/gSum(mesh.V().field()))*runTime.deltaTValue();

    acousticCoNum = 0.5*gMax
    (
        fvc::surfaceSum
        (
            fvc::interpolate(scalar(1)/sqrt(psi))*mesh.magSf()
        )().primitiveField()/mesh.V().field()
    )*runTime.deltaTValue();
}

Info<< "Central Courant Number mean: " << meanCoNum
    << " max: " << CoNum
    << " acoustic max: " << acousticCoNum
    << endl;

// Foundation OF7 reads alpha controls from mesh.solverDict(alpha.name()).
// M2 keeps the explicit MULES branch and Euler time discretisation only.
const dictionary& alphaControls = mesh.solverDict(alphaL.name());

const label nAlphaCorr(readLabel(alphaControls.lookup("nAlphaCorr")));
const label nAlphaSubCycles
(
    readLabel(alphaControls.lookup("nAlphaSubCycles"))
);
const scalar cAlpha(readScalar(alphaControls.lookup("cAlpha")));
const scalar maxAlphaCo
(
    runTime.controlDict().lookupOrDefault<scalar>("maxAlphaCo", 0.1)
);
const scalar alphaBoundTolerance
(
    alphaControls.lookupOrDefault<scalar>("alphaBoundTolerance", 1e-8)
);

if (nAlphaCorr < 1 || nAlphaSubCycles < 1 || cAlpha < 0)
{
    FatalErrorInFunction
        << "Require nAlphaCorr>=1, nAlphaSubCycles>=1 and cAlpha>=0"
        << exit(FatalError);
}
if (alphaBoundTolerance < 0)
{
    FatalErrorInFunction
        << "alphaBoundTolerance must be non-negative"
        << exit(FatalError);
}
if (maxAlphaCo <= 0)
{
    FatalErrorInFunction << "maxAlphaCo must be positive"
        << exit(FatalError);
}

// OF7 VoF/alphaCourantNo.H masks cells with mixture.nearInterface().
// M2 has no mixture/interface model, so use all cells: this is a
// conservative diagnostic bound for the VOF volume flux.
scalar alphaCoNum = 0;
scalar meanAlphaCoNum = 0;

if (mesh.nInternalFaces())
{
    const scalarField sumPhi
    (
        fvc::surfaceSum(mag(phiV))().primitiveField()
    );
    alphaCoNum =
        0.5*gMax(sumPhi/mesh.V().field())*runTime.deltaTValue();
    meanAlphaCoNum =
        0.5*gSum(sumPhi)/gSum(mesh.V().field())*runTime.deltaTValue();
}

Info<< "Alpha Courant Number mean: " << meanAlphaCoNum
    << " max: " << alphaCoNum
    << " configured maxAlphaCo: " << maxAlphaCo
    << " (diagnostic only)" << endl;

文件 006/138：alphaEqn.H
类别：当前项目源码
绝对路径：D:\DATA\Solver\rhosprayVoFDPMFoam\alphaEqn.H
SHA256：e80ee131e8a4f55e070b6f066d81bfe5bc4ff35f852dab7064e73a2ac32b5a61
原文件字节数：1933
----- BEGIN SOURCE 006 -----
// M2 explicit VOF transport, adapted from Foundation OF7 VoF/alphaEqn.H.
// The carrier equations remain gas-only. For this one-way marker stage,
// D(alphaL)/Dt=0 is written as ddt(alphaL)+div(alphaPhi)
// = alphaL*div(phiV), following OF7's explicit divU source structure.
for (label alphaCorr=0; alphaCorr<nAlphaCorr; ++alphaCorr)
{
    // This local normal is used only for M2 interface compression. Keep its
    // registry name distinct from OF7 interfaceProperties::nHatf_ (M3).
    volVectorField gradAlpha(fvc::grad(alphaL, "nHat"));
    surfaceVectorField gradAlphaf(fvc::interpolate(gradAlpha));
    const dimensionedScalar deltaN
    (
        "deltaN", 1e-8/pow(average(mesh.V()), 1.0/3.0)
    );
    surfaceScalarField alphaCompressionNHatf
    (
        "alphaCompressionNHatf",
        (gradAlphaf/(mag(gradAlphaf) + deltaN)) & mesh.Sf()
    );
    surfaceScalarField phic
    (
        "phic", cAlpha*mag(phiV/mesh.magSf())
    );
    forAll(phic.boundaryField(), patchi)
    {
        if (!phic.boundaryField()[patchi].coupled())
        {
            phic.boundaryFieldRef()[patchi] == 0;
        }
    }
    surfaceScalarField phir("phir", phic*alphaCompressionNHatf);

    alphaPhi =
        fvc::flux(phiV, alphaL, "div(phiV,alphaL)")
      + fvc::flux
        (
            -fvc::flux(-phir, alphaG, "div(phirb,alphaL)"),
            alphaL,
            "div(phirb,alphaL)"
        );

    volScalarField::Internal Sp
    (
        IOobject("SpAlpha", runTime.timeName(), mesh),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar(dimless/dimTime, 0)
    );
    volScalarField::Internal Su
    (
        IOobject("SuAlpha", runTime.timeName(), mesh),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar(dimless/dimTime, 0)
    );
    Su = fvc::div(phiV)()*min(alphaL(), scalar(1));

    MULES::explicitSolve
    (
        geometricOneField(), alphaL, phiV, alphaPhi,
        Sp, Su, oneField(), zeroField()
    );
    alphaG = scalar(1) - alphaL;
}

----- END SOURCE 006 -----

// Foundation OF7 VoF/alphaEqnSubCycle.H accumulates a dt-weighted face flux.
// This solver accumulates alphaPhi rather than rhoPhi: mixture mass flux
// ownership is deferred to M5.
if (nAlphaSubCycles > 1)
{
    const dimensionedScalar totalDeltaT = runTime.deltaT();
    surfaceScalarField alphaPhiSum
    (
        IOobject("alphaPhiSum", runTime.timeName(), mesh),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar(alphaPhi.dimensions(), 0)
    );

    tmp<volScalarField> trSubDeltaT;
    if (LTS)
    {
        trSubDeltaT =
            fv::localEulerDdt::localRSubDeltaT(mesh, nAlphaSubCycles);
    }

    for
    (
        subCycle<volScalarField> alphaSubCycle(alphaL, nAlphaSubCycles);
        !(++alphaSubCycle).end();
    )
    {
        #include "alphaEqn.H"
        alphaPhiSum += (runTime.deltaT()/totalDeltaT)*alphaPhi;
    }
    alphaPhi = alphaPhiSum;
}
else
{
    #include "alphaEqn.H"
}

alphaG = scalar(1) - alphaL;

const scalar alphaLMin = min(alphaL).value();
const scalar alphaLMax = max(alphaL).value();
if
(
    alphaLMin < -alphaBoundTolerance
 || alphaLMax > 1 + alphaBoundTolerance
)
{
    FatalErrorInFunction
        << "alphaL outside [0,1] beyond tolerance "
        << alphaBoundTolerance << ": min=" << alphaLMin
        << " max=" << alphaLMax << exit(FatalError);
}
Info<< "alphaL min/max = " << alphaLMin << ", " << alphaLMax
    << "; volume = " << fvc::domainIntegrate(alphaL).value()
    << endl;

// Transition view: current gas density is the sole legacy KT rho field.
// The future five-equation state will recover intrinsic rhoG from mG/alphaG.
// This alias adds no second density equation and keeps DPM gas-carried.
const volScalarField& rhoG = rho;

// M2 field ownership: resolved Eulerian liquid is independent of the
// Lagrangian parcel population. Neither fraction changes the legacy gas PDEs.
volScalarField alphaL
(
    IOobject
    (
        "alphaL", runTime.timeName(), mesh,
        IOobject::MUST_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE
    ),
    mesh
);

(
    IOobject
    (

        IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE
    ),
    scalar(1) - alphaL
);

    IOobject
    (
        "rhoU",
        runTime.timeName(),
        mesh,
        IOobject::NO_READ,
        IOobject::NO_WRITE
    ),
    rho*U
);

(
    IOobject
    (
        "rhoE",
        runTime.timeName(),
        mesh,
        IOobject::NO_READ,
        IOobject::NO_WRITE

    ),

    dimensionedScalar("neg", dimless, -1.0)
);

    (
        "phiV", runTime.timeName(), mesh,

// replaces this initial value with the limited MULES liquid-fraction flux.

    IOobject
    (

        IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE

//- Interpolate field vf according to direction dir
template<class Type>
tmp<GeometricField<Type, fvsPatchField, surfaceMesh> > interpolate
(
    const GeometricField<Type, fvPatchField, volMesh>& vf,
    const surfaceScalarField& dir,
    const word& reconFieldName = word::null
)
{
    tmp<GeometricField<Type, fvsPatchField, surfaceMesh> > tsf
    (
        fvc::interpolate
        (
            vf,
            dir,
            "reconstruct("
          + (reconFieldName != word::null ? reconFieldName : vf.name())
          + ')'
        )
    );

    GeometricField<Type, fvsPatchField, surfaceMesh>& sf = tsf.ref();

    sf.rename(vf.name() + '_' + dir.name());

    return tsf;
}

}

绝对路径：D:\DATA\Solver\rhosprayVoFDPMFoam\rhoYEqn.H

----- BEGIN SOURCE 021 -----
tmp<fv::convectionScheme<scalar>> mvConvection
(
    fv::convectionScheme<scalar>::New
    (
        mesh,
        fields,
        phi,
        mesh.divScheme("div(phi,Yi_h)")
    )
);

{
    reaction->correct();
    Qdot = reaction->Qdot();
    volScalarField Yt(0*Y[0]);

    forAll(Y, i)
    {
        if (i != inertIndex && composition.active(i))
        {
	    volScalarField& Yi = Y[i];

            fvScalarMatrix YiEqn
            (
                fvm::ddt(rho, Yi)
              + mvConvection->fvmDiv(phi, Yi)
	      - parcels.SYi(i, Yi)
              - reaction->R(Yi)
            );

            if (!inviscid)
	    {
                YiEqn -= fvm::laplacian(turbulence->muEff(), Yi);
            }

	    YiEqn.solve("Yi");
            Yi.max(0.0);
	    Yi.correctBoundaryConditions();
            Yt += Yi;
        }
    }
    
    Y[inertIndex] = scalar(1) - Yt;
    Y[inertIndex].max(0.0);
}

原文件字节数：2972

#include "rhosprayLiquidThermo.H"
#include "error.H"
#include "dimensionSets.H"
#include <cmath>

Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::rhosprayLiquidThermo(const dictionary& dict)
:
    component_(dict.lookup("component")),
    gamma_(readScalar(dict.lookup("gamma"))),
    pInf_
    (
        dimensionedScalar("pInf", dimPressure, dict).value()
    ),
    Cv_
    (
        dimensionedScalar("Cv", dimSpecificHeatCapacity, dict).value()
    ),
    q_
    (
        dimensionedScalar("q", dimEnergy/dimMass, dict).value()
    )
{
    if
    (
        !std::isfinite(gamma_) || gamma_ <= 1
     || !std::isfinite(pInf_) || pInf_ < 0
     || !std::isfinite(Cv_) || Cv_ <= 0
     || !std::isfinite(q_)
    )
    {
        FatalErrorInFunction
            << "Invalid stiffened-gas Eulerian liquid parameters"
            << exit(FatalError);
    }
}


void Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::checkPT
(
    const scalar p,
    const scalar T
) const
{
    if
    (
        !std::isfinite(p) || !std::isfinite(T)
     || p + pInf_ <= 0 || T <= 0
    )
    {
        FatalErrorInFunction
            << "Invalid Eulerian liquid state: p=" << p << ", T=" << T
            << exit(FatalError);
    }
}


void Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::checkRhoP
(
    const scalar rho,
    const scalar p
) const
{
    if
    (
        !std::isfinite(rho) || !std::isfinite(p)
     || rho <= 0 || p + pInf_ <= 0
    )
    {
        FatalErrorInFunction
            << "Invalid Eulerian liquid state: rho=" << rho << ", p=" << p
            << exit(FatalError);
    }
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::rho
(
    const scalar p,
    const scalar T
) const
{
    checkPT(p, T);
    return (p + pInf_)/((gamma_ - 1)*Cv_*T);
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::e
(
    const scalar p,
    const scalar T
) const
{
    return q_ + Cv_*T + pInf_/rho(p, T);
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::h
(
    const scalar p,
    const scalar T
) const
{
    return e(p, T) + p/rho(p, T);
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::T
(
    const scalar rho,
    const scalar p
) const
{
    checkRhoP(rho, p);
    return (p + pInf_)/((gamma_ - 1)*rho*Cv_);
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::eFromRhoP
(
    const scalar rho,
    const scalar p
) const
{
    checkRhoP(rho, p);
    return q_ + (p + gamma_*pInf_)/((gamma_ - 1)*rho);
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::c
(
    const scalar rho,
    const scalar p
) const
{
    checkRhoP(rho, p);
    return std::sqrt(gamma_*(p + pInf_)/rho);
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::deDpConstRho
(
    const scalar rho,
    const scalar p
) const
{
    checkRhoP(rho, p);
    return 1/((gamma_ - 1)*rho);
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::drhoDpConstT
(
    const scalar p,
    const scalar T
) const
{
    checkPT(p, T);
    return 1/((gamma_ - 1)*Cv_*T);
}


Foam::scalar Foam::rhosprayLiquidThermo::xi
(
    const scalar rho,
    const scalar p
) const
{
    checkRhoP(rho, p);
    return 1/(gamma_ - 1);
}

#ifndef rhosprayLiquidThermo_H
#define rhosprayLiquidThermo_H

#include "dictionary.H"
#include "dimensionedScalar.H"
#include "word.H"

namespace Foam
{

// Eulerian liquid EOS candidate for the future two-material conservative
// solver. This class owns no mesh field and does not affect the active PDEs.
class rhosprayLiquidThermo
{
    word component_;
    scalar gamma_;
    scalar pInf_;
    scalar Cv_;
    scalar q_;

    void checkPT(const scalar p, const scalar T) const;
    void checkRhoP(const scalar rho, const scalar p) const;

public:
    explicit rhosprayLiquidThermo(const dictionary& dict);

    const word& component() const { return component_; }
    scalar gamma() const { return gamma_; }
    scalar pInf() const { return pInf_; }
    scalar Cv() const { return Cv_; }
    scalar q() const { return q_; }

    scalar rho(const scalar p, const scalar T) const;
    scalar e(const scalar p, const scalar T) const;
    scalar h(const scalar p, const scalar T) const;
    scalar T(const scalar rho, const scalar p) const;
    scalar eFromRhoP(const scalar rho, const scalar p) const;
    scalar c(const scalar rho, const scalar p) const;

    // Required by the future isobaric pressure residual and acoustic closure.
    scalar deDpConstRho(const scalar rho, const scalar p) const;
    scalar drhoDpConstT(const scalar p, const scalar T) const;
    scalar xi(const scalar rho, const scalar p) const;
};

}

#endif

/*---------------------------------------------------------------------------*\

Application
    rhoReactingCentralFoam

Description
    Density-based compressible flow solver based on central-upwind schemes of
    Kurganov and Tadmor with reactions

    Ported to OF7 by CL, based on original code from CFD Online thread :
    https://www.cfd-online.com/Forums/openfoam-community-contributions/82851-new-solver-supersonic-combustion.html#post286897

#include "fvCFD.H"
#include "serialFiveEq/runSerialFiveEq.H"
#include "dynamicFvMesh.H"
#include "turbulentFluidThermoModel.H"
#include "psiReactionThermo.H"
#include "CombustionModel.H"
#include "multivariateScheme.H"
#include "fixedRhoFvPatchScalarField.H"
#include "coupledFvsPatchFields.H"

#include "fvOptions.H"
#include "gaussConvectionScheme.H"
#include "CMULES.H"
#include "interfaceProperties.H"
#include "rhosprayVoFMixture.H"
#include "subCycle.H"
#include "slicedSurfaceFields.H"
#include "basicSprayCloud.H"//1
#include "radiationModel.H" //2
#include "SLGThermo.H" //3
#include "CorrectPhi.H"//
#include "fvOptions.H"//

// * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * //

int main(int argc, char *argv[])
{
    argList::addBoolOption("serialFiveEq", "M6B.0 serial Eulerian verification bootstrap");
    #include "postProcess.H"

    #include "setRootCase.H"

    #include "createTime.H"
    #include "createDynamicFvMesh.H"
    if (args.optionFound("serialFiveEq"))
    {
        return runSerialFiveEq(runTime, mesh);
    }
    // Frozen legacy reference path; never entered by serialFiveEq. 
    #include "createControls.H"
    //#include "createClouds.H"
    #include "createFields.H"
    #include "createFieldRefs.H"
    #include "alphaControls.H"
    #include "createInterfaceProperties.H"
    rhosprayVoFMixture vofMixture
    (
        mesh, alphaL, alphaG, rhoG, thermo, slgThermo
    );
    #include "createFvOptions.H"

    //#include "compressibleCourantNo.H"//
    //#include "setInitialDeltaT.H"//
   // #include "initContinuityErrs.H"//
    //#include "createRhoUfIfPresent.H"//
    //#include "createDyMControls.H"//

    // * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * * //

    #include "readFluxScheme.H"

    dimensionedScalar v_zero("v_zero", dimVolume/dimTime, 0.0);

    // Courant numbers used to adjust the time-step
    scalar CoNum = 0.0;
    scalar meanCoNum = 0.0;
    scalar acousticCoNum = 0.0;
    scalar startTimeIndex = runTime.timeIndex();

    Info<< "\nStarting time loop\n" << endl;

    while (runTime.run())
    {
        #include "readControls.H"


		// Indicators for refinement. Note: before runTime++
        // only for postprocessing reasons. Grabbed from 
        // PDRFoamAutoRefine.C.
        tmp<volScalarField> tmagGradP = mag(fvc::grad(p));
        volScalarField normalisedGradP
        (
            "normalisedGradP",
            tmagGradP()/max(tmagGradP())
        );
        normalisedGradP.writeOpt() = IOobject::AUTO_WRITE;
        tmagGradP.clear();

	volScalarField cc
        (
            "cc",
            sqrt((thermo.Cp()/thermo.Cv())/thermo.psi())
        );
        cc.writeOpt() = IOobject::AUTO_WRITE;

        // Store the particle positions
        parcels.storeGlobalPositions();

        // Do any mesh changes
        if (runTime.timeIndex() - startTimeIndex > 0)
	{
            mesh.update();
        }

        if (!LTS)
        {
            #include "setDeltaT.H"
            runTime++;
        }

        // --- Directed interpolation of primitive fields onto faces

        surfaceScalarField rho_pos(interpolate(rho, pos));
        surfaceScalarField rho_neg(interpolate(rho, neg));

        surfaceVectorField rhoU_pos(interpolate(rhoU, pos, U.name()));
        surfaceVectorField rhoU_neg(interpolate(rhoU, neg, U.name()));

        volScalarField rPsi("rPsi", 1.0/psi);
        surfaceScalarField rPsi_pos(interpolate(rPsi, pos, T.name()));
        surfaceScalarField rPsi_neg(interpolate(rPsi, neg, T.name()));

        surfaceScalarField e_pos(interpolate(e, pos, T.name()));
        surfaceScalarField e_neg(interpolate(e, neg, T.name()));

        surfaceVectorField U_pos("U_pos", rhoU_pos/rho_pos);
        surfaceVectorField U_neg("U_neg", rhoU_neg/rho_neg);

        surfaceScalarField p_pos("p_pos", rho_pos*rPsi_pos);
        surfaceScalarField p_neg("p_neg", rho_neg*rPsi_neg);

        surfaceScalarField phiv_pos("phiv_pos", U_pos & mesh.Sf());
        surfaceScalarField phiv_neg("phiv_neg", U_neg & mesh.Sf());

        // Make fluxes relative to mesh-motion
        if (mesh.moving())
        {
            phiv_pos -= mesh.phi();
            phiv_neg -= mesh.phi();
        }

        volScalarField c("c", sqrt(thermo.Cp()/thermo.Cv()*rPsi));
        
        surfaceScalarField cSf_pos("cSf_pos",interpolate(c, pos, T.name())*mesh.magSf());
        surfaceScalarField cSf_neg("cSf_neg",interpolate(c, neg, T.name())*mesh.magSf());

        surfaceScalarField ap("ap",max(max(phiv_pos + cSf_pos, phiv_neg + cSf_neg), v_zero));
        surfaceScalarField am("am",min(min(phiv_pos - cSf_pos, phiv_neg - cSf_neg), v_zero));

        surfaceScalarField a_pos("a_pos", ap/(ap - am));

        surfaceScalarField amaxSf("amaxSf", max(mag(am), mag(ap)));

        surfaceScalarField aSf("aSf", am*a_pos);

        if (fluxScheme == "Tadmor")
        {
            aSf = -0.5*amaxSf;
            a_pos = 0.5;
        }

        surfaceScalarField a_neg("a_neg", 1.0 - a_pos);

        phiv_pos *= a_pos;
        phiv_neg *= a_neg;

        surfaceScalarField aphiv_pos("aphiv_pos", phiv_pos - aSf);
        surfaceScalarField aphiv_neg("aphiv_neg", phiv_neg + aSf);

        // Reuse amaxSf for the maximum positive and negative fluxes
        // estimated by the central scheme
        amaxSf = max(mag(aphiv_pos), mag(aphiv_neg));

        #include "CourantNo.H"

        if (LTS)
        {
            #include "setRDeltaT.H"
            runTime++;
        }

        Info<< "Time = " << runTime.timeName() << nl << endl;

        phi = aphiv_pos*rho_pos + aphiv_neg*rho_neg;

        // M2 one-way VOF: KT gas mass flux remains owned by phi.
        phiV = aphiv_pos + aphiv_neg;
        #include "alphaCourantNo.H"
        #include "alphaSubCycle.H"
        #include "interfaceGeometry.H"
        vofMixture.correct();
        // M5A flux diagnostics remain an active design reference, not part
        // of the current legacy gas-KT run path.

        surfaceVectorField phiUp
        (
            (aphiv_pos*rhoU_pos + aphiv_neg*rhoU_neg)
          + (a_pos*p_pos + a_neg*p_neg)*mesh.Sf()
        );

        surfaceScalarField phiEp
        (
            "phiEp",
            aphiv_pos*(rho_pos*(e_pos + 0.5*magSqr(U_pos)) + p_pos)
          + aphiv_neg*(rho_neg*(e_neg + 0.5*magSqr(U_neg)) + p_neg)
          + aSf*p_pos - aSf*p_neg
        );

        /*surfaceScalarField phiEp
        (
            "phiEp",
            aphiv_pos*(rho_pos*(e_pos) + p_pos)
          + aphiv_neg*(rho_neg*(e_neg) + p_neg)
          + aSf*p_pos - aSf*p_neg
        );*/

        // Make flux for pressure-work absolute
        if (mesh.moving())
        {
            phiEp += mesh.phi()*(a_pos*p_pos + a_neg*p_neg);
        }

        volScalarField muEff("muEff", turbulence->muEff());
        volTensorField tauMC("tauMC", muEff*dev2(Foam::T(fvc::grad(U))));
	parcels.evolve();
        // --- Solve density
        #include "rhoEqn.H"

        // --- Solve momentum
        #include "rhoUEqn.H"
        
        //CL --- Solve species
        #include "rhoYEqn.H"

        // --- Solve energy
        #include "rhoEEqn.H"
        // Refresh diagnostic properties after the legacy gas rho/T update.
        vofMixture.correct();

       /* forAll(maxp, celli)    
          {
	    if(maxp[celli] < p[celli])
               maxp[celli] = p[celli];
           }*/
        turbulence->correct();

        runTime.write();

        Info<< "ExecutionTime = " << runTime.elapsedCpuTime() << " s"
            << "  ClockTime = " << runTime.elapsedClockTime() << " s"
            << nl << endl;
    }

    Info<< "End\n" << endl;

    return 0;
}

#include "rhosprayVoFMixture.H"

Foam::rhosprayVoFMixture::rhosprayVoFMixture
(
    const fvMesh& mesh,
    const volScalarField& alphaL,
    const volScalarField& alphaG,
    const volScalarField& rhoGas,
    const psiReactionThermo& gasThermo,
    const SLGThermo& slgThermo
)
:
    alphaL_(alphaL),
    alphaG_(alphaG),
    rhoG_(rhoGas),
    gasThermo_(gasThermo),
    liquids_(slgThermo.liquids()),
    liquidX_(1, scalar(1)),
    rhoL_
    (
        IOobject("rhoL", mesh.time().timeName(), mesh,
            IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar("rhoL", dimDensity, 0)
    ),
    rhoMix_
    (
        IOobject("rhoMix", mesh.time().timeName(), mesh,
            IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar("rhoMix", dimDensity, 0)
    ),
    muL_
    (
        IOobject("muL", mesh.time().timeName(), mesh,
            IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar("muL", dimDynamicViscosity, 0)
    ),
    muMix_
    (
        IOobject("muMix", mesh.time().timeName(), mesh,
            IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar("muMix", dimDynamicViscosity, 0)
    ),
    CpL_
    (
        IOobject("CpL", mesh.time().timeName(), mesh,
            IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar("CpL", dimSpecificHeatCapacity, 0)
    ),
    CpMix_
    (
        IOobject("CpMix", mesh.time().timeName(), mesh,
            IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar("CpMix", dimSpecificHeatCapacity, 0)
    ),
    kappaL_
    (
        IOobject("kappaL", mesh.time().timeName(), mesh,
            IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar
        (
            "kappaL", dimPower/dimLength/dimTemperature, 0
        )
    ),
    kappaMix_
    (
        IOobject("kappaMix", mesh.time().timeName(), mesh,
            IOobject::NO_READ, IOobject::AUTO_WRITE),
        mesh, dimensionedScalar
        (
            "kappaMix", dimPower/dimLength/dimTemperature, 0
        )
    )
{
    if (liquids_.size() != 1)
    {
        FatalErrorInFunction
            << "M4 single-liquid closure requires exactly one SLGThermo "
            << "liquid; found " << liquids_.size()
            << exit(FatalError);
    }

    Info<< "M4 diagnostic VOF liquid uses the DPM SLGThermo component "
        << liquids_.components()[0] << endl;

    correct();
}


void Foam::rhosprayVoFMixture::updateLiquid
(
    volScalarField& field,
    liquidPropertyFunction property
)
{
    // Diagnostic property estimate only: gas p/T are query coordinates,
    // not the future five-equation liquid pressure/temperature ownership.
    const volScalarField& p = gasThermo_.p();
    const volScalarField& T = gasThermo_.T();

    forAll(field, celli)
    {
        field[celli] = (liquids_.*property)(p[celli], T[celli], liquidX_);
    }

    forAll(field.boundaryField(), patchi)
    {
        forAll(field.boundaryField()[patchi], facei)
        {
            field.boundaryFieldRef()[patchi][facei] =
                (liquids_.*property)
                (
                    p.boundaryField()[patchi][facei],
                    T.boundaryField()[patchi][facei],
                    liquidX_
                );
        }
    }
}


void Foam::rhosprayVoFMixture::correct()
{
    updateLiquid(rhoL_, &liquidMixtureProperties::rho);
    rhoMix_ = alphaL_*rhoL_ + alphaG_*rhoG_;
    updateLiquid(muL_, &liquidMixtureProperties::mu);
    muMix_ = alphaL_*muL_ + alphaG_*gasThermo_.mu();
    updateLiquid(CpL_, &liquidMixtureProperties::Cp);
    CpMix_ =
        (alphaL_*rhoL_*CpL_ + alphaG_*rhoG_*gasThermo_.Cp())
       /max(rhoMix_, dimensionedScalar("rhoMixFloor", dimDensity, SMALL));
    updateLiquid(kappaL_, &liquidMixtureProperties::kappa);
    kappaMix_ = alphaL_*kappaL_ + alphaG_*gasThermo_.kappa();

    if
    (
        rhoG_.dimensions() != dimDensity
     || rhoL_.dimensions() != dimDensity
     || rhoMix_.dimensions() != dimDensity
     || gasThermo_.mu().ref().dimensions() != dimDynamicViscosity
     || muL_.dimensions() != dimDynamicViscosity
     || muMix_.dimensions() != dimDynamicViscosity
     || gasThermo_.Cp().ref().dimensions() != dimSpecificHeatCapacity
     || CpL_.dimensions() != dimSpecificHeatCapacity
     || CpMix_.dimensions() != dimSpecificHeatCapacity
     || gasThermo_.kappa().ref().dimensions()
        != dimPower/dimLength/dimTemperature
     || kappaL_.dimensions() != dimPower/dimLength/dimTemperature
     || kappaMix_.dimensions() != dimPower/dimLength/dimTemperature
    )
    {
        FatalErrorInFunction << "M4 phase-property dimensions invalid"
            << exit(FatalError);
    }
}

#ifndef rhosprayVoFMixture_H
#define rhosprayVoFMixture_H

#include "fvCFD.H"
#include "psiReactionThermo.H"
#include "SLGThermo.H"

namespace Foam
{

// M4 property closure only. No conservative field is owned or updated here.
class rhosprayVoFMixture
{
    const volScalarField& alphaL_;
    const volScalarField& alphaG_;
    const volScalarField& rhoG_;
    const psiReactionThermo& gasThermo_;
    const liquidMixtureProperties& liquids_;
    const scalarField liquidX_;

    typedef scalar (liquidMixtureProperties::*liquidPropertyFunction)
    (
        const scalar,
        const scalar,
        const scalarField&
    ) const;

    void updateLiquid(volScalarField& field, liquidPropertyFunction property);

    volScalarField rhoL_;
    volScalarField rhoMix_;
    volScalarField muL_;
    volScalarField muMix_;
    volScalarField CpL_;
    volScalarField CpMix_;
    volScalarField kappaL_;
    volScalarField kappaMix_;

public:
    rhosprayVoFMixture
    (
        const fvMesh& mesh,
        const volScalarField& alphaL,
        const volScalarField& alphaG,
        const volScalarField& rhoGas,
        const psiReactionThermo& gasThermo,
        const SLGThermo& slgThermo
    );

    const volScalarField& rhoGas() const { return rhoG_; }
    const volScalarField& rhoLiquid() const { return rhoL_; }
    const volScalarField& rhoMix() const { return rhoMix_; }
    tmp<volScalarField> muGas() const { return gasThermo_.mu(); }
    const volScalarField& muLiquid() const { return muL_; }
    const volScalarField& muMix() const { return muMix_; }
    tmp<volScalarField> CpGas() const { return gasThermo_.Cp(); }
    const volScalarField& CpLiquid() const { return CpL_; }
    const volScalarField& CpMix() const { return CpMix_; }
    tmp<volScalarField> kappaGas() const { return gasThermo_.kappa(); }
    const volScalarField& kappaLiquid() const { return kappaL_; }
    const volScalarField& kappaMix() const { return kappaMix_; }

    void correct();
};

}

{
    scalar maxDeltaTFact =
        min(maxCo/(CoNum + small), maxAcousticCo/(acousticCoNum + small));

    scalar deltaTFact = min(min(maxDeltaTFact, 1.0 + 0.1*maxDeltaTFact), 1.2);

    runTime.setDeltaT

        min
        (
            deltaTFact*runTime.deltaTValue(),
            maxDeltaT
        )
    );

    Info<< "deltaT = " <<  runTime.deltaTValue() << endl;

{

    scalar deltaTFact = min(min(maxDeltaTFact, 1.0 + 0.1*maxDeltaTFact), 1.2);

    runTime.setDeltaT
    (
        min
        (
            deltaTFact*runTime.deltaTValue(),
            maxDeltaT
        )
    );
}

文件 028/138：setRDeltaT.H
类别：当前项目源码
绝对路径：D:\DATA\Solver\rhosprayVoFDPMFoam\setRDeltaT.H
SHA256：969556c57da516695f6528ff72561e0f20d9103a5a47dca04239dd782f015edb
原文件字节数：2225
----- BEGIN SOURCE 028 -----
{
    volScalarField& rDeltaT = trDeltaT.ref();

    // Cache old reciprocal time scale field
    volScalarField rDeltaT0("rDeltaT0", rDeltaT);

    // Maximum flow Courant number
    scalar maxCo(runTime.controlDict().lookupOrDefault<scalar>("maxCo", 0.5));

    // Maximum time scale
    scalar maxDeltaT(runTime.controlDict().lookupOrDefault<scalar>("maxDeltaT", GREAT));

    // Smoothing parameter (0-1) when smoothing iterations > 0
    scalar rDeltaTSmoothingCoeff(runTime.controlDict().lookupOrDefault<scalar>("rDeltaTSmoothingCoeff",0.02));

    // Damping coefficient (1-0)
    scalar rDeltaTDampingCoeff(runTime.controlDict().lookupOrDefault<scalar>("rDeltaTDampingCoeff",0.9));

    // Maximum change in cell temperature per iteration
    // (relative to previous value)
    scalar alphaTemp(runTime.controlDict().lookupOrDefault("alphaTemp",1));

    {
        // Set the reciprocal time-step from the local Courant number
        rDeltaT.ref() =
        (
            fvc::surfaceSum(amaxSf)()
           /((2*maxCo)*mesh.V())
        );
        
        // Limit the largest time scale
        rDeltaT.max(1/maxDeltaT);
    }

    // Reaction source time scale
    if (alphaTemp < 1.0)
    {
        volScalarField::Internal rDeltaTT
        (
            mag(Qdot)/(alphaTemp*rho*thermo.Cp()*T)
        );

        rDeltaT.ref() = max
        (
            rDeltaT(),
            rDeltaTT
        );
    }

    // Update tho boundary values of the reciprocal time-step
    rDeltaT.correctBoundaryConditions();

    // Spatially smooth the time scale field
    if (rDeltaTSmoothingCoeff < 1.0)
    {
        fvc::smooth(rDeltaT, rDeltaTSmoothingCoeff);
    }

    // Limit rate of change of time scale
    // - reduce as much as required
    // - only increase at a fraction of old time scale
    if
    (
        rDeltaTDampingCoeff < 1.0
     && runTime.timeIndex() > runTime.startTimeIndex() + 1
    )
    {
        rDeltaT = max
        (
            rDeltaT,
            (scalar(1.0) - rDeltaTDampingCoeff)*rDeltaT0
        );
    }

    Info<< "Flow time scale min/max = "
        << gMin(1/(rDeltaT.primitiveField() + 1.0e-12))
        << ", " << gMax(1/(rDeltaT.primitiveField() + 1.0e-12)) << endl;
}

#ifndef twoPhaseKTState_H
#define twoPhaseKTState_H

#include "scalar.H"
#include "vector.H"

namespace Foam
{

// M5B ACTIVE DESIGN contract only. These records have no field ownership,
// EOS evaluation or solver side effects. alphaLiquid is non-conservative in
// the selected isobaric five-equation model.
struct twoPhaseKTConserved
{
    scalar gasMass;
    scalar liquidMass;
    vector totalMomentum;
    scalar totalEnergy;
};

struct twoPhaseKTState
{
    twoPhaseKTConserved conserved;
    scalar alphaLiquid;
};

struct twoPhaseKTPrimitive
{
    scalar alphaLiquid;
    scalar gasDensity;
    scalar liquidDensity;
    vector velocity;
    scalar pressure;
    scalar gasTemperature;
    scalar liquidTemperature;
    scalar gasSoundSpeed;
    scalar liquidSoundSpeed;
    scalar mixtureSoundSpeed;
};

struct twoPhaseKTConservativeFlux
{
    scalar gasMass;
    scalar liquidMass;
    vector totalMomentum;
    scalar totalEnergy;
};

}

文件 032/138：dpmFiveEq/KHNewbornIdentityV1.H

// Only the existing guarded KH physical-birth site may call this adapter.

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqDPMSourceAdapter.H"
namespace fiveEq {
enum class CarrierSupportStatus {VALID_PRESENT,VALID_TRACE,INVALID_GAS_ABSENT,INVALID_INCONSISTENT,INVALID_THERMO,UNSUPPORTED_STENCIL};
struct CarrierSupportResult {CarrierSupportStatus status;std::string reason;CarrierSupportResult(CarrierSupportStatus s=CarrierSupportStatus::INVALID_INCONSISTENT,std::string r={}):status(s),reason(std::move(r)){}bool valid()const{return status==CarrierSupportStatus::VALID_PRESENT||status==CarrierSupportStatus::VALID_TRACE;}};
struct GasStencilContributor {bool boundary=false;unsigned index=0;Foam::tensor weight=Foam::tensor::zero;bool nativeRead=false;};
// Component comparison is intentional: squared norms can underflow to zero.
inline bool hasNonzeroStencilWeight(const Foam::tensor& weight){for(Foam::direction j=0;j<9;++j)if(weight[j]!=0)return true;return false;}
// Owns an immutable snapshot of committed P2/Q, including authoritative patch
// states supplied by the boundary owner. A rejected trial cannot change it.
class fiveEqDPMCarrierSupport {
 std::vector<PrecisionCell> cells_,boundary_;const PrecisionOperator& op_;
 CarrierSupportResult classify(const PrecisionCell& c)const {
  if(c.fraction.mode()!=FractionMode::P2)return {CarrierSupportStatus::INVALID_INCONSISTENT,"P2_REQUIRED"};
  const double a=c.fraction.gasValue(),m=c.q[1];
  if(!std::isfinite(a)||!std::isfinite(m)||a<0||a>1||m<0||(a==0)!=(m==0))return {CarrierSupportStatus::INVALID_INCONSISTENT,"P2_MG_CONFLICT"};
  if(a==0&&m==0)return {CarrierSupportStatus::INVALID_GAS_ABSENT,"EXACT_GAS_ABSENT"};
  try{auto r=op_.recover(c);if(!(r.rhoG>0&&r.TG>0))return {CarrierSupportStatus::INVALID_THERMO,"INVALID_INTRINSIC_GAS"};return {r.gasPresence==PhasePresence::TRACE_PRESENT?CarrierSupportStatus::VALID_TRACE:CarrierSupportStatus::VALID_PRESENT,"OK"};}
  catch(const std::exception& e){return {CarrierSupportStatus::INVALID_THERMO,e.what()};}
 }
public:
 fiveEqDPMCarrierSupport(std::vector<PrecisionCell> c,std::vector<PrecisionCell> patch,const PrecisionOperator& op):cells_(std::move(c)),boundary_(std::move(patch)),op_(op){}
 CarrierSupportResult classifyCell(unsigned c)const {if(c>=cells_.size())return {CarrierSupportStatus::INVALID_INCONSISTENT,"CELL_RANGE"};return classify(cells_[c]);}
 CarrierSupportResult validateInterpolationStencil(const std::vector<GasStencilContributor>& stencil)const {
  if(stencil.empty()){return {CarrierSupportStatus::UNSUPPORTED_STENCIL,"EMPTY_STENCIL"};}bool trace=false;
  for(const auto& s:stencil){for(Foam::direction j=0;j<9;++j)if(!std::isfinite(s.weight[j]))return {CarrierSupportStatus::UNSUPPORTED_STENCIL,"NONFINITE_WEIGHT"};
   if(!s.nativeRead&&!hasNonzeroStencilWeight(s.weight))continue;
   CarrierSupportResult r;if(s.boundary){if(s.index>=boundary_.size())return {CarrierSupportStatus::INVALID_INCONSISTENT,"PATCH_STATE_REQUIRED"};r=classify(boundary_[s.index]);}else r=classifyCell(s.index);
   if(!r.valid()){return r;}trace=trace||r.status==CarrierSupportStatus::VALID_TRACE;
  }return {trace?CarrierSupportStatus::VALID_TRACE:CarrierSupportStatus::VALID_PRESENT,"OK"};
 }
 CarrierSupportResult validateInjectionLocation(unsigned c,const std::vector<GasStencilContributor>& s)const {auto r=classifyCell(c);return r.valid()?validateInterpolationStencil(s):r;}
 CarrierSupportResult validateTrackLocation(unsigned c,const std::vector<GasStencilContributor>& s)const {return validateInjectionLocation(c,s);}
 CarrierSupportResult validateSourceDeposit(unsigned c)const{return classifyCell(c);}
 PrecisionCell sourceTrial(unsigned c,double V,const fiveEqDPMSourceIncrement& source,const fiveEqDPMSourceAdapter& adapter)const {
  auto r=validateSourceDeposit(c);if(!r.valid())throw RecoveryError("DPM_SUPPORT_SOURCE:"+r.reason);return adapter.trial(cells_.at(c),V,source,op_);
 }
};
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqDPMCarrierSupport.H"
#include "interpolationCellPoint.H"
#include "cellPointWeight.H"
namespace fiveEq {
// Diagnostic exact linear basis expansion of the REAL OF7 vector operator.
// Includes component constraints via tensor weights. Never queries gas EOS.
// This deliberately bounded serial probe is NOT a scalable production hook.
inline std::vector<GasStencilContributor> nativeCellPointStencil(const Foam::volVectorField& U,const Foam::vector& position,Foam::label cell,const Foam::barycentric* coordinates=nullptr,const Foam::tetIndices* tet=nullptr){
 const auto& mesh=U.mesh();if(Foam::Pstream::parRun()||mesh.nCells()+mesh.nFaces()-mesh.nInternalFaces()>256)throw RecoveryError("UNSUPPORTED_PRODUCTION_OR_MPI_STENCIL_PROBE");
 std::vector<GasStencilContributor> out;
 for(Foam::label entity=0;entity<mesh.nCells()+mesh.nFaces()-mesh.nInternalFaces();++entity){
  GasStencilContributor c;c.boundary=entity>=mesh.nCells();c.index=c.boundary?entity-mesh.nCells():entity;
  for(Foam::direction axis=0;axis<3;++axis){
   Foam::volVectorField basis(Foam::IOobject("dpmStencilBasis",U.time().timeName(),mesh,Foam::IOobject::NO_READ,Foam::IOobject::NO_WRITE,false),U);
   basis.primitiveFieldRef()=Foam::vector::zero;forAll(basis.boundaryField(),patchi)basis.boundaryFieldRef()[patchi]==Foam::vector::zero;
   if(!c.boundary)basis[entity][axis]=1;else{Foam::label face=mesh.nInternalFaces()+c.index;Foam::label patch=mesh.boundaryMesh().whichPatch(face);basis.boundaryFieldRef()[patch][face-mesh.boundaryMesh()[patch].start()][axis]=1;}
   Foam::interpolationCellPoint<Foam::vector> interpolator(basis);auto w=coordinates&&tet?interpolator.interpolate(*coordinates,*tet):interpolator.interpolate(position,cell);for(Foam::direction row=0;row<3;++row)c.weight[3*row+axis]=w[row];
  }
  if(hasNonzeroStencilWeight(c.weight))out.push_back(c);
 }
 // Cell property interpolators always read containing cell independently of
 // the U barycentric coefficient, even at zero cell-centre U weight.
 GasStencilContributor own;own.index=cell;own.nativeRead=true;out.push_back(own);return out;
}
inline std::vector<GasStencilContributor> nativeParticleStencil(const Foam::volVectorField& U,const Foam::particle& p){auto tet=p.currentTetIndices();return nativeCellPointStencil(U,p.position(),p.cell(),&p.coordinates(),&tet);}
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqP2IO.H"
#include <numeric>
namespace fiveEq {
struct NativeDPMTransfer {double massKg=0,sensibleEnthalpyJ=0;Foam::vector momentumKgMS{0,0,0};std::vector<double> speciesKg;};
// Complete aggregate of all parcels participating in ONE local source event.
// Enthalpy reference is actual OF7 CompositionModel::H (raw liquid h), not C_L.
struct ParcelEnergyState {double massKg=0,enthalpyJ=0,volumeM3=0,kineticJ=0;Foam::vector momentumKgMS{0,0,0};std::vector<double> carrierSpeciesKg;};
struct DPMExchangeContext {double heldPressurePa=0,externalHeatJ=0,externalWorkJ=0;Foam::vector externalImpulseKgMS{0,0,0};bool clipped=false;};
struct DPMGasCarrier {double rhoG=0,TG=0,p=0;Foam::vector U{0,0,0};std::vector<double> runtimeYG;PhasePresence presence=PhasePresence::ABSENT;};
struct fiveEqDPMSourceIncrement {double dmG=0,dEt=0;Foam::vector dM{0,0,0};std::vector<double> dmYi;double nativeEnthalpyJ=0,caloricCorrectionJ=0,kineticCorrectionJ=0,chemicalReferenceJ=0,pressureWorkJ=0;Foam::vector momentumCorrection{0,0,0};};
inline bool ledgerClose(double a,double b,double absolute){return std::abs(a-b)<=absolute+1e-12*(std::abs(a)+std::abs(b));}
inline bool endpointClose(double transferred,double before,double after,double absolute){return std::abs(transferred-(before-after))<=absolute+128*std::numeric_limits<double>::epsilon()*(std::abs(before)+std::abs(after)+std::abs(transferred));}
class fiveEqDPMSourceAdapter {
 std::vector<double> hc_;
public:
 explicit fiveEqDPMSourceAdapter(std::vector<double> runtimeHc):hc_(runtimeHc){}
 DPMGasCarrier gasCarrier(const PrecisionCell& q,const PrecisionOperator& op)const {
  auto r=op.recover(q);if(r.gasPresence==PhasePresence::ABSENT||r.gasPresence==PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT||!(r.rhoG>0&&r.TG>0&&r.mG>0))throw RecoveryError("DPM_TRACKING_GAS_ABSENT_OR_INVALID");
  DPMGasCarrier c;c.rhoG=r.rhoG;c.TG=r.TG;c.p=r.p;c.U=Foam::vector(r.U[0],r.U[1],r.U[2]);c.presence=r.gasPresence;for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i)c.runtimeYG.push_back(q.q[7+i]/q.q[1]);return c;
 }
 fiveEqDPMSourceIncrement convert(const NativeDPMTransfer& n,const ParcelEnergyState& before,const ParcelEnergyState& after,const DPMExchangeContext& ctx)const {
  if(ctx.clipped||!std::isfinite(ctx.heldPressurePa)||ctx.heldPressurePa<=0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_DPM_EVENT_CONTEXT");
  for(const auto* p:{&before,&after}){
   if(!std::isfinite(p->massKg)||!std::isfinite(p->volumeM3)||p->massKg<0||p->volumeM3<0||(p->massKg>0&&p->volumeM3==0)||!std::isfinite(p->enthalpyJ)||!std::isfinite(p->kineticJ)||p->kineticJ<0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_PARCEL_ENDPOINT");
   double mass=0;for(double x:p->carrierSpeciesKg){if(!std::isfinite(x)||x<0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_PARCEL_COMPOSITION");mass+=x;}
   if(!ledgerClose(mass,p->massKg,1e-30))throw RecoveryError("PARCEL_ENDPOINT_SPECIES_SUM");
  }
  const unsigned N=hc_.size();if(n.speciesKg.size()!=N||before.carrierSpeciesKg.size()!=N||after.carrierSpeciesKg.size()!=N)throw RecoveryError("DPM_RUNTIME_SPECIES_SIZE");
  fiveEqDPMSourceIncrement s;s.dmG=n.massKg;s.dmYi=n.speciesKg;
  double sum=0;for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){if(!std::isfinite(s.dmYi[i])||!std::isfinite(hc_[i]))throw RecoveryError("NONFINITE_DPM_SPECIES");sum+=s.dmYi[i];if(!endpointClose(s.dmYi[i],before.carrierSpeciesKg[i],after.carrierSpeciesKg[i],1e-30))throw RecoveryError("PARCEL_SPECIES_LEDGER_MISMATCH");s.chemicalReferenceJ+=hc_[i]*s.dmYi[i];}
  if(!ledgerClose(sum,s.dmG,1e-30)||!endpointClose(s.dmG,before.massKg,after.massKg,1e-30))throw RecoveryError("PARCEL_MASS_LEDGER_MISMATCH");
  s.dM=ctx.externalImpulseKgMS-(after.momentumKgMS-before.momentumKgMS);s.momentumCorrection=s.dM-n.momentumKgMS;
  s.nativeEnthalpyJ=n.sensibleEnthalpyJ;
  // Exact isobaric enthalpy remapping, explicitly separate from the native
  // frozen-Cp/latent/deletion approximation. Not an external heat residual.
  const double exactThermal=before.enthalpyJ-after.enthalpyJ-s.chemicalReferenceJ+ctx.externalHeatJ;
  s.caloricCorrectionJ=exactThermal-s.nativeEnthalpyJ;
  s.kineticCorrectionJ=before.kineticJ-after.kineticJ+ctx.externalWorkJ;
  // Convert parcel H to its internal energy H-pV at HELD pressure.
  // Opposite pressure work is an internal exchange, never external residual.
  s.pressureWorkJ=ctx.heldPressurePa*(after.volumeM3-before.volumeM3);
  s.dEt=s.nativeEnthalpyJ+s.caloricCorrectionJ+s.kineticCorrectionJ+s.pressureWorkJ;
  for(double v:{s.dmG,s.dEt,s.caloricCorrectionJ,s.kineticCorrectionJ,s.pressureWorkJ})if(!std::isfinite(v))throw RecoveryError("NONFINITE_DPM_LEDGER");
  for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)if(!std::isfinite(s.dM[j]))throw RecoveryError("NONFINITE_DPM_MOMENTUM");
  return s;
 }
 PrecisionCell trial(const PrecisionCell& anchor,double eventVolume,const fiveEqDPMSourceIncrement& s,const PrecisionOperator& op)const {
  if(!(eventVolume>0&&std::isfinite(eventVolume))||s.dmYi.size()!=3)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_SOURCE_VOLUME_OR_CURRENT_SPECIES_COUNT");
  bool zero=s.dmG==0&&s.dEt==0&&Foam::magSqr(s.dM)==0;for(double x:s.dmYi)zero=zero&&x==0;
  if(zero)return anchor; // signed zeros and every field bit preserved
  auto recovered=op.recover(anchor);if(recovered.gasPresence==PhasePresence::ABSENT)throw RecoveryError("DPM_GAS_ABSENT_NO_ALPHA_CREATION_MODEL");
  PrecisionCell t=anchor;t.q[1]+=s.dmG/eventVolume;for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){t.q[3+j]+=s.dM[j]/eventVolume;t.q[7+j]+=s.dmYi[j]/eventVolume;}t.q[6]+=s.dEt/eventVolume;
  op.recover(t);return t; // all Q coherent; mL/P2 untouched; no clipping
 }
};
}

#pragma once
#include <stdexcept>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
namespace fiveEq {
enum class DPMTransactionState {NEW,TRIAL,COMMITTED,DISCARDED,UNSUPPORTED};
// Capability gate, not a claim that native basicSprayCloud can transact.
// Unknown/unsupported side effects reject BEFORE mutating production state.
class fiveEqDPMTransactionGate {
 DPMTransactionState state_=DPMTransactionState::NEW;bool validated_=false;
public:
 void beginTrial(const std::vector<std::string>& unsupported){if(state_!=DPMTransactionState::NEW)throw std::logic_error("TRANSACTION_ALREADY_STARTED");if(!unsupported.empty()){state_=DPMTransactionState::UNSUPPORTED;throw std::runtime_error("DPM_TRANSACTION_UNSUPPORTED:"+unsupported.front());}state_=DPMTransactionState::TRIAL;}
 void validated(){if(state_!=DPMTransactionState::TRIAL)throw std::logic_error("NO_TRIAL");validated_=true;}
 void commitTrial(){if(state_!=DPMTransactionState::TRIAL||!validated_)throw std::logic_error("COMMIT_NOT_VALIDATED");state_=DPMTransactionState::COMMITTED;}
 void discardTrial(){if(state_!=DPMTransactionState::TRIAL)throw std::logic_error("DISCARD_NOT_LIVE");state_=DPMTransactionState::DISCARDED;}
 DPMTransactionState state()const{return state_;}
};
}

#pragma once
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <stdexcept>
namespace fiveEq {
// Native diagnostic is a residence-time integral, NOT endpoint occupancy.
// Record postMove samples in trial memory. Discard drops them; commit exposes
// the integrated diagnostic once. This helper itself never writes a file.
class fiveEqDeferredVoidFraction {
 std::vector<double> integral_; bool live_=true;
public:
 explicit fiveEqDeferredVoidFraction(unsigned cells):integral_(cells,0){}
 void postMove(unsigned cell,double dt,double nParticle,double volume){
  if(!live_||cell>=integral_.size()||!std::isfinite(dt)||!std::isfinite(nParticle)||!std::isfinite(volume)||dt<0||nParticle<0||volume<0)throw std::logic_error("INVALID_VOID_FRACTION_SAMPLE");
  double next=integral_[cell]+dt*nParticle*volume;if(!std::isfinite(next))throw std::logic_error("VOID_FRACTION_OVERFLOW");integral_[cell]=next;
 }
 std::vector<double> commit(double dt,const std::vector<double>& V){
  if(!live_||!(dt>0)||!std::isfinite(dt)||V.size()!=integral_.size())throw std::logic_error("INVALID_DIAGNOSTIC_COMMIT");
  std::vector<double> result(integral_);for(unsigned i=0;i<V.size();++i){if(!(V[i]>0)||!std::isfinite(V[i])||!std::isfinite(dt*V[i])||dt*V[i]==0)throw std::logic_error("INVALID_CELL_VOLUME");result[i]/=dt*V[i];}
  live_=false;return result;
 }
 void discard(){if(!live_)throw std::logic_error("DIAGNOSTIC_NOT_LIVE");integral_.clear();live_=false;}
};
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqDPMSourceAdapter.H"
namespace fiveEq {
// Template evaluated against actual Foundation7 basicSprayCloud, no fake API.
template<class Cloud> NativeDPMTransfer nativeCellTransfer(Cloud& cloud,unsigned cell){
 NativeDPMTransfer n;for(Foam::label i=0;i<cloud.rhoTrans().size();++i){double x=cloud.rhoTrans(i)[cell];n.speciesKg.push_back(x);n.massKg+=x;}n.momentumKgMS=cloud.UTrans()[cell];n.sensibleEnthalpyJ=cloud.hsTrans()[cell];return n;
}
template<class Cloud> ParcelEnergyState parcelEnergyState(const Cloud& cloud,const typename Cloud::parcelType& p,double pressure){
 ParcelEnergyState s;s.massKg=p.nParticle()*p.mass();s.volumeM3=s.massKg/p.rho();s.momentumKgMS=s.massKg*p.U();s.kineticJ=.5*s.massKg*Foam::magSqr(p.U());s.enthalpyJ=s.massKg*cloud.composition().H(0,p.Y(),pressure,p.T());s.carrierSpeciesKg.assign(cloud.rhoTrans().size(),0);
 for(Foam::label i=0;i<p.Y().size();++i){s.carrierSpeciesKg.at(cloud.composition().localToCarrierId(0,i))+=s.massKg*p.Y()[i];}return s;
}
}

namespace fiveEq {
// Source part of rollback only. Caller separately owns cloud clone, Eulerian
// Q, RNG/model/output metadata, time and mesh; no universal rollback claim.
template<class Cloud> class NativeDPMSourceSnapshot {
 Foam::vectorField momentum_;
 Foam::scalarField momentumCoeff_,heat_,heatCoeff_;
 std::vector<Foam::scalarField> species_;
public:
 explicit NativeDPMSourceSnapshot(Cloud& c):momentum_(c.UTrans().field()),momentumCoeff_(c.UCoeff().field()),heat_(c.hsTrans().field()),heatCoeff_(c.hsCoeff().field()){
  if(c.radiation())throw std::runtime_error("DPM_SNAPSHOT_RADIATION_NOT_SUPPORTED");
  for(Foam::label i=0;i<c.rhoTrans().size();++i)species_.push_back(c.rhoTrans(i).field());
 }
 void restore(Cloud& c)const {
  if(c.rhoTrans().size()!=static_cast<Foam::label>(species_.size()))throw std::runtime_error("DPM_SNAPSHOT_SPECIES_SIZE_CHANGED");
  c.UTrans().field()=momentum_;c.UCoeff().field()=momentumCoeff_;c.hsTrans().field()=heat_;c.hsCoeff().field()=heatCoeff_;
  for(Foam::label i=0;i<c.rhoTrans().size();++i)c.rhoTrans(i).field()=species_[i];
 }
};
}

namespace fiveEq {
// OF7 allocator is a process-local static, shared by ALL clouds. A trial must
// hold exclusive access; nested/interleaved cloud transactions are rejected.
class fiveEqParticleIDTransaction {
 static bool& occupied(){static bool value=false;return value;}
 Foam::label saved_; bool live_=false;
public:
 fiveEqParticleIDTransaction():saved_(Foam::particle::particleCount_){
  if(occupied())throw std::logic_error("PARTICLE_ALLOCATOR_TRANSACTION_BUSY");
  occupied()=true;live_=true;
 }
 fiveEqParticleIDTransaction(const fiveEqParticleIDTransaction&)=delete;
 fiveEqParticleIDTransaction& operator=(const fiveEqParticleIDTransaction&)=delete;
 ~fiveEqParticleIDTransaction(){if(live_)discard();}
 void discard(){if(!live_)throw std::logic_error("ID_TRANSACTION_NOT_LIVE");Foam::particle::particleCount_=saved_;live_=false;occupied()=false;}
 void commit(){if(!live_)throw std::logic_error("ID_TRANSACTION_NOT_LIVE");live_=false;occupied()=false;}

#include "fiveEqAlphaDiscrete.H"
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <cmath>
namespace fiveEq {
fiveEqAlphaStep alphaEulerStep(double a,double V,double dt,double FL,double FR,
    const fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds& L,const fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds& R,double meshL,double meshR){
    if(!std::isfinite(a)||!std::isfinite(V)||!std::isfinite(dt)||V<=0||dt<0)
        throw std::invalid_argument("Invalid alpha step input");
    fiveEqAlphaStep s;s.volume=V+dt*(meshR-meshL);
    if(!std::isfinite(s.volume)||s.volume<=0)throw std::invalid_argument("Nonpositive GCL volume");
    s.sourceIntegral=dt*a*(R.phiPhysStar-L.phiPhysStar);
    s.centerWeight=V+dt*(-L.Aplus+R.Aminus+meshR-meshL);
    // Algebraically equal to (V*a-dt*(FR-FL)+sourceIntegral)/Vnew.
    // Cancelling the GCL term symbolically preserves exact alpha=0/1 plateaus,
    // without clipping or changing any phase mass/energy.
    const double relativeDivergence=R.phiRelStar-L.phiRelStar;
    s.alpha=a+dt*(-(FR-FL)+a*relativeDivergence)/s.volume;
    // Deliberately no clipping; caller must respect the joint timestep bounds.
    return s;
}
double alphaPositiveDtBound(double V,const fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds& L,
    const fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds& R,double meshL,double meshR){
    const double out=L.Aplus-R.Aminus-(meshR-meshL);
    return out>0?V/out:std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
}
double massPositiveDtBound(double V,const fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds& L,const fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds& R){
    const double out=R.Aplus-L.Aminus;
    return out>0?V/out:std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
}
}

#include "fiveEqKTFaceFlux.H"
namespace fiveEq {
fiveEqKTFaceFlux fiveEqKTFaceFluxKernel::evaluate(const fiveEqRuntimeInput& L,
    const fiveEqRuntimeInput& R,const Foam::vector& Sf,double meshPhi,KTScheme scheme) const {
    fiveEqKTFaceFlux f;
    const bool leftOK=bridge_.recoverFaceState(L,f.left,f.leftDiagnostic);
    const bool rightOK=bridge_.recoverFaceState(R,f.right,f.rightDiagnostic);
    if(!leftOK||!rightOK){f.status=!leftOK?(!rightOK?FaceFluxStatus::BOTH_INVALID:FaceFluxStatus::LEFT_INVALID):FaceFluxStatus::RIGHT_INVALID;return f;}
    if(speciesCount_<=0||(L.mG.value()>0&&L.YG.size()!=speciesCount_)
       ||(R.mG.value()>0&&R.YG.size()!=speciesCount_)){
        f.status=FaceFluxStatus::INVALID_SPECIES_LAYOUT;return f;
    }
    try {f.wave=ktWaveSpeeds(f.left.U,f.right.U,f.left.thermo.cMix,
        f.right.thermo.cMix,Sf,meshPhi,scheme);}
    catch(const std::invalid_argument&){f.status=FaceFluxStatus::INVALID_GEOMETRY;return f;}
    const auto& w=f.wave;const double pL=f.left.thermo.p,pR=f.right.thermo.p;
    const double pStar=w.aPos*pL+w.aNeg*pR;
    f.mGPhi=w.Aplus*L.mG.value()+w.Aminus*R.mG.value();
    f.mLPhi=w.Aplus*L.mL.value()+w.Aminus*R.mL.value();
    f.momentumPhi=w.Aplus*L.M.value()+w.Aminus*R.M.value()+pStar*Sf;
    f.energyPhi=w.Aplus*(L.Et.value()+pL)+w.Aminus*(R.Et.value()+pR)
        +w.aSf*pL-w.aSf*pR+meshPhi*pStar;
    f.alphaPhiCandidate=w.Aplus*L.alphaL.value()+w.Aminus*R.alphaL.value();
    f.speciesPhi=Foam::scalarField(speciesCount_,0);
    for(Foam::label i=0;i<speciesCount_;++i){
        const double left=L.mG.value()>0?L.mG.value()*L.YG[i]:0;
        const double right=R.mG.value()>0?R.mG.value()*R.YG[i]:0;
        f.speciesPhi[i]=w.Aplus*left+w.Aminus*right;
    }
    f.status=FaceFluxStatus::OK;return f;
}
}

#include "fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds.H"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <stdexcept>
namespace fiveEq {
fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds ktWaveSpeeds(const Foam::vector& UL,const Foam::vector& UR,
    double cL,double cR,const Foam::vector& Sf,double meshPhi,KTScheme scheme) {
    const double area=Foam::mag(Sf);
    if(!std::isfinite(area)||area<=0||!std::isfinite(meshPhi)
       ||!std::isfinite(cL)||!std::isfinite(cR)||cL<=0||cR<=0)
        throw std::invalid_argument("Invalid KT geometry/acoustics");
    fiveEqKTWaveSpeeds w;
    w.qPlus=(UL & Sf)-meshPhi;w.qMinus=(UR & Sf)-meshPhi;
    if(!std::isfinite(w.qPlus)||!std::isfinite(w.qMinus))
        throw std::invalid_argument("Nonfinite relative velocity");
    w.ap=std::max({w.qPlus+cL*area,w.qMinus+cR*area,0.});
    w.am=std::min({w.qPlus-cL*area,w.qMinus-cR*area,0.});
    w.aPos=w.ap/(w.ap-w.am);w.aSf=w.am*w.aPos;
    if(scheme==KTScheme::Tadmor){w.aPos=.5;w.aSf=-.5*std::max(std::abs(w.ap),std::abs(w.am));}
    w.aNeg=1-w.aPos;
    w.Aplus=w.aPos*w.qPlus-w.aSf;w.Aminus=w.aNeg*w.qMinus+w.aSf;
    w.phiRelStar=w.Aplus+w.Aminus;w.phiPhysStar=w.phiRelStar+meshPhi;
    if(!std::isfinite(w.Aplus)||!std::isfinite(w.Aminus))
        throw std::invalid_argument("Nonfinite KT coefficient");
    return w;
}
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqPrecisionOperator.H"
#include "fiveEqThermodynamicTimeStep.H"
namespace fiveEq {
enum class PrecisionBoundaryDecision {KEEP_HIGH_ORDER,ROLLBACK_R0,EOS_DOMAIN_EXHAUSTION};
// Exact-boundary semantics from frozen M5C.2A. No generic interior fallback.
// Both candidates must be computed from the same unmodified committed state.
inline PrecisionBoundaryDecision certifiedPureLiquidBoundaryDecision(
 const PrecisionCell& c,double V,const PrecisionFace& highL,const PrecisionFace& highR,
 const PrecisionFace& lowL,const PrecisionFace& lowR,double meshL,double meshR,
 const fiveEqRuntimeBridge& bridge,const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& liquid,
 double certifiedP,double certifiedT){
 bool high=pureLiquidOutwardBoundary(c.q,V,highL.flux,highR.flux,meshL,meshR,bridge,liquid,certifiedP,certifiedT);
 bool low=pureLiquidOutwardBoundary(c.q,V,lowL.flux,lowR.flux,meshL,meshR,bridge,liquid,certifiedP,certifiedT);
 if(high&&low){return PrecisionBoundaryDecision::EOS_DOMAIN_EXHAUSTION;}
 return high?PrecisionBoundaryDecision::ROLLBACK_R0:PrecisionBoundaryDecision::KEEP_HIGH_ORDER;
}
}

struct PrecisionReconstructionStats {unsigned cells=0,limited=0,direct=0,fallback=0;std::vector<double> theta;};
class PrecisionReconstruction {
 const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator& gas_;const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& liquid_;const PrecisionOperator& op_;std::array<unsigned,3> index_;
public:
 PrecisionReconstruction(const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator& g,const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& l,const PrecisionOperator& o,std::array<unsigned,3> index):gas_(g),liquid_(l),op_(o),index_(index){}
 PrecisionCell generate(PrecisionFraction f,double p,double TG,double TL,Foam::vector U,std::vector<double> Y)const {
  if(f.mode()==FractionMode::P2Scaled){using namespace scaledV2;auto qv=f.scaledAuthority().q.toDoubleView();if(qv.exact&&!qv.underflow){bool gm=f.scaledAuthority().side==Side::GAS_MINORITY||f.scaledAuthority().side==Side::PURE_LIQUID;auto c=generate(PrecisionFraction::minority(qv.value,gm,FractionMode::P2),p,TG,TL,U,Y);c.migrateV2();return c;}

  }

  PrecisionCell c;c.fraction=f;c.q.fill(0);c.q[0]=f.liquidValue();double internal=0;
  if(f.gasValue()>0){auto s=gas_.evaluatePT(p,TG,Y);c.q[1]=f.gasValue()*s.rho;internal+=c.q[1]*s.e;for(unsigned k=0;k<3;++k)c.q[7+index_[k]]=c.q[1]*Y[k];}
  if(f.liquidValue()>0){auto s=liquid_.evaluatePT(p,TL);c.q[2]=f.liquidValue()*s.rho;internal+=c.q[2]*s.e;}

 }
 std::vector<std::array<PrecisionCell,2>> reconstruct(const std::vector<PrecisionCell>& q,unsigned strategy,PrecisionReconstructionStats* stats=nullptr)const {
  if(usesNonzeroK(op_.model())&&strategy!=0)throw RecoveryError("MC4_PIECEWISE_CONSTANT_ONLY");

  unsigned N=q.size();std::vector<std::array<PrecisionCell,2>> result(N);for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)result[i]={{q[i],q[i]}};if(strategy==0)return result;

   }

   }
  }return result;

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqPrecisionOperator.H"
namespace fiveEq {
inline std::array<scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator,5> authorityLedger(const std::vector<PrecisionCell>& cells,const std::vector<double>& volumes){
 if(cells.size()!=volumes.size())throw RecoveryError("AUTHORITY_LEDGER_VOLUME_MISMATCH");std::array<scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator,5> sums;
 for(unsigned i=0;i<cells.size();++i){scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator V;V.add(volumes[i]);for(unsigned k=0;k<5;++k){unsigned j=k<2?k+1:k+5;sums[k].add(cells[i].mass(j).multiplied(V));}}return sums;
}
inline void guardAuthorityConservation(const std::array<scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator,5>& before,const std::vector<PrecisionCell>& after,const std::vector<double>& volumes){
 using namespace scaledV2;auto sums=authorityLedger(after,volumes);
 // Each local mass/species update rounds only once. The dependent species is
 // a residual: its bound is the sum of the three independent rounding bounds.
 for(unsigned k=0;k<5;++k){auto error=sums[k];error.add(before[k],-1);if(error.sign()<0)error=error.negated();auto bound=sums[k].shifted(-103);if(k==4){bound=sums[0];bound.add(sums[2]);bound.add(sums[3]);bound=bound.shifted(-103);}if(error.compare(bound)>0)throw RecoveryError("EXTENDED_MASS_OR_SPECIES_CONSERVATION_BLOCKER");}
}
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqPrecisionReconstruction.H"
namespace fiveEq {
struct BoundaryPrimitive {PrecisionFraction fraction{FractionMode::P2};double p=0,TG=0,TL=0;Foam::vector U{0,0,0};std::vector<double> canonicalY;};
class fiveEqBoundaryStateBuilder {
 const PrecisionReconstruction& rec_;const PrecisionOperator& op_;
public:
 fiveEqBoundaryStateBuilder(const PrecisionReconstruction& r,const PrecisionOperator& o):rec_(r),op_(o){}
 PrecisionCell build(const BoundaryPrimitive& b)const {
  if(b.fraction.mode()!=FractionMode::P2)throw RecoveryError("BOUNDARY_REQUIRES_P2");
  auto c=rec_.generate(b.fraction,b.p,b.TG,b.TL,b.U,b.canonicalY);auto s=op_.recover(c);
  if(std::abs(s.p-b.p)>std::max(1e-4,1e-8*b.p))throw RecoveryError("BOUNDARY_PRESSURE_ROUNDTRIP");
  for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)if(std::abs(s.U[j]-b.U[j])>1e-7)throw RecoveryError("BOUNDARY_VELOCITY_ROUNDTRIP");
  if(c.q[1]>0&&std::abs(s.TG-b.TG)>1e-8*b.TG)throw RecoveryError("BOUNDARY_GAS_TEMPERATURE_ROUNDTRIP");
  if(c.q[2]>0&&std::abs(s.TL-b.TL)>1e-8*b.TL)throw RecoveryError("BOUNDARY_LIQUID_TEMPERATURE_ROUNDTRIP");
  return c;
 }
 fiveEqRecoveredState recoverBoundaryState(const PrecisionCell& c)const{return op_.recover(c);}
 PrecisionCell reflected(const PrecisionCell& c,const Foam::vector& unitNormal)const {
  if(std::abs(Foam::magSqr(unitNormal)-1)>1e-12)throw RecoveryError("NON_UNIT_WALL_NORMAL");
  auto r=c;Foam::vector M(c.q[3],c.q[4],c.q[5]);M-=2*(M&unitNormal)*unitNormal;for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)r.q[3+j]=M[j];op_.recover(r);return r;
 }
 PrecisionCell transformed(const PrecisionCell& c,const std::array<std::array<double,3>,3>& R)const {
  for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i)for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){double dot=0;for(unsigned k=0;k<3;++k)dot+=R[i][k]*R[j][k];if(!std::isfinite(dot)||std::abs(dot-(i==j?1.:0.))>1e-12)throw RecoveryError("NON_ORTHOGONAL_PATCH_TRANSFORM");}
  auto r=c;for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i){r.q[3+i]=0;for(unsigned k=0;k<3;++k)r.q[3+i]+=R[i][k]*c.q[3+k];}op_.recover(r);return r;
 }
};
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqScaledRatio.H"
#include "fiveEqTemporalContract.H"
#include "fiveEqCRV3Viscosity.H"
namespace fiveEq {
struct CRV3Bounds {double hyperbolic=INFINITY,alphaSource=INFINITY,viscous=INFINITY,joint=INFINITY,spectralUpper=0;};
struct CRV3StepResult:TemporalStepResult {CRV3Bounds bounds;double linearResidual=0,heatingMin=0,heatingIntegral=0,entropyProductionIntegral=0;unsigned capAdjustments=0;std::string firstRejection="NONE";};
// Serial uniform periodic axial stress. This contract does not certify 2D/MPI.
class fiveEqCRV3TemporalDriver {
 const PrecisionOperator& op_;const PrecisionReconstruction& rec_;double dx_,mu_;TemporalPolicyJournal* journal_;
 void guard(const std::vector<PrecisionCell>& q,const std::vector<PrecisionCell>& anchor)const {
  for(unsigned i=0;i<q.size();++i){const auto& c=q[i];
   if(c.representationV2){if(c.fraction.mode()!=FractionMode::P2Scaled||!c.fraction.scaledAuthority().q.canonical())throw RecoveryError("CRV3_SCALED_P2_AUTHORITY");for(unsigned j:{1u,2u})if(anchor[i].mass(j).sign()>0&&c.mass(j).sign()<=0)throw RecoveryError("CRV3_PRESENT_PHASE_LOST");}
   else {if(c.fraction.mode()!=FractionMode::P2||c.fraction.payload().lo!=0||std::abs(c.fraction.payload().hi)>.5||c.q[0]!=c.fraction.liquidValue())throw RecoveryError("CRV3_P2_AUTHORITY");for(unsigned j:{1u,2u})if(anchor[i].q[j]>0&&!(c.q[j]>0))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_PRESENT_PHASE_LOST");}
   op_.recover(c);
  }
 }
 design::KEWorkStep viscous(const std::vector<PrecisionCell>& old,double dt)const {
  if(mu_==0){design::KEWorkStep z;z.cells=old;z.heating.assign(old.size(),0);return z;}
  auto z=design::keWorkMidpoint(old,dx_,dt,mu_);
  for(unsigned i=0;i<old.size();++i){const auto&a=old[i];const auto&b=z.cells[i];
   if(a.representationV2){if(a.fraction.scaledAuthority().side!=b.fraction.scaledAuthority().side||a.fraction.scaledAuthority().q.wire()!=b.fraction.scaledAuthority().q.wire())throw RecoveryError("CRV3_VISCOUS_P2_CHANGED");for(unsigned k=0;k<4;++k)if(a.authority[k].tag()!=b.authority[k].tag()||a.authority[k].scaled().wire()!=b.authority[k].scaled().wire())throw RecoveryError("CRV3_VISCOUS_AUTHORITY_CHANGED");}
   else if(exactBits(a.fraction.payload().hi)!=exactBits(b.fraction.payload().hi)||exactBits(a.fraction.payload().lo)!=exactBits(b.fraction.payload().lo))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_VISCOUS_P2_CHANGED");
   for(unsigned j:{0u,1u,2u,4u,5u,7u,8u,9u})if(exactBits(a.q[j])!=exactBits(b.q[j]))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_VISCOUS_FIXED_COMPONENT_CHANGED");
   long double rho=a.representationV2?a.rhoView():static_cast<long double>(a.q[1])+a.q[2];
   long double dk=(static_cast<long double>(b.q[3])*b.q[3]-static_cast<long double>(a.q[3])*a.q[3])/(2*rho);
   long double heat=static_cast<long double>(b.q[6])-a.q[6]-dk;
   double roundoff=16*std::numeric_limits<double>::epsilon()*(std::abs(a.q[6])+std::abs(b.q[6])+double((static_cast<long double>(a.q[3])*a.q[3]+static_cast<long double>(b.q[3])*b.q[3])/rho));
   if(!std::isfinite(z.heating[i])||z.heating[i]<0||heat< -roundoff||std::abs(heat-z.heating[i])>roundoff+1e-14+1e-10*z.heating[i])throw RecoveryError("CRV3_ENERGY_WORK_OR_HEATING_BLOCKER");
  }
  guard(z.cells,old);return z;
 }
public:
 fiveEqCRV3TemporalDriver(const PrecisionOperator&o,const PrecisionReconstruction&r,double dx,double mu,TemporalPolicyJournal* journal=nullptr):op_(o),rec_(r),dx_(dx),mu_(mu),journal_(journal){
  if(o.model()!=MechanicalModel::NONZERO_K_PE_CRV3_v1||!(dx>0&&std::isfinite(dx)&&mu>=0&&std::isfinite(mu)))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_RUNTIME_IDENTITY_OR_COEFFICIENT");
 }
 CRV3Bounds bounds(const std::vector<PrecisionCell>& q)const {
  CRV3Bounds b;auto states=rec_.reconstruct(q,0);std::vector<PrecisionFace> faces;unsigned N=q.size();
  for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)faces.push_back(op_.face(states[i][0],states[(i+1)%N][1]));
  double minRho=INFINITY;
  for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){const auto&L=faces[(i+N-1)%N];const auto&R=faces[i];
   b.hyperbolic=std::min(b.hyperbolic,op_.acousticBound(q[i],dx_,L,R));b.joint=std::min(b.joint,op_.jointBound(q[i],dx_,L,R));
   if(q[i].representationV2){using namespace scaledV2;auto n=op_.totalFractionNumeratorScaled(q[i],L,R);SparseExponentAccumulator v;v.add(dx_);if(n.sign()!=0){auto amount=n.sign()<0?q[i].fraction.liquidExact():q[i].fraction.gasExact();auto out=n.sign()<0?n.negated():n;b.alphaSource=std::min(b.alphaSource,.95*rationalDoubleView(amount.multiplied(v),out).value);}}
   else {double n=ddValue(op_.totalFractionNumerator(q[i],L,R));if(n<0)b.alphaSource=std::min(b.alphaSource,positivePhaseTimeRatio(.95*q[i].fraction.liquidValue()*dx_,-n));if(n>0)b.alphaSource=std::min(b.alphaSource,positivePhaseTimeRatio(.95*q[i].fraction.gasValue()*dx_,n));}
   minRho=std::min(minRho,q[i].rhoView());
  }
  // L=diag(rho)^(-1) mu/dx^2 (2I-S-S^T), similar to a PSD matrix.
  // Gershgorin: lambdaMax<=4mu/(min(rho)dx^2). Each midpoint half-step
  // has g(lambda)=(1-dt*lambda/4)/(1+dt*lambda/4). Require g>=0:
  // dt<=4/lambdaUpper. Energy stability itself is unconditional.
  if(mu_>0){b.spectralUpper=4*mu_/minRho/dx_/dx_;b.viscous=4/b.spectralUpper;}
  b.joint=std::min(b.joint,b.viscous);return b;
 }
 CRV3StepResult advance(TemporalState& s,double request)const {
  if(!(request>0&&std::isfinite(request))||s.committed.size()<4||s.volume.size()!=s.committed.size())throw RecoveryError("CRV3_TEMPORAL_ARGUMENT");
  for(double V:s.volume)if(std::abs(V/dx_-1)>1e-12)throw RecoveryError("CRV3_UNIFORM_VOLUME_REQUIRED");
  const TemporalState anchor=s;guard(anchor.committed,anchor.committed);CRV3StepResult result;result.bounds=bounds(anchor.committed);
  double cap=std::min(request,result.bounds.joint),proposal=temporalGrowthRequest(cap,s.lastDt);
  // A viscous predictor can change the subsequent hyperbolic cap. Rebuild from
  // the same anchor on each adjustment; never accumulate rejected substeps.
  for(unsigned k=0;k<16;++k){try{auto pre=viscous(anchor.committed,.5*proposal);double fresh=std::min(cap,bounds(pre.cells).joint);if(proposal<=fresh)break;proposal=std::nextafter(fresh,0.);++result.capAdjustments;}catch(const std::exception&){break;}}
  struct Metrics {double linear=0,minHeat=INFINITY,heat=0,entropy=0;};
  std::map<double,TemporalState> legal;std::map<double,Metrics> diagnostics;double hyperHint=0;
  auto integrate=[&](const std::vector<PrecisionCell>& old,const design::KEWorkStep& z,Metrics& m){
   for(unsigned i=0;i<old.size();++i){auto a=op_.recover(old[i]),b=op_.recover(z.cells[i]);
    auto weight=[&](const fiveEqRecoveredState&r){if(old[i].representationV2){using namespace scaledV2;SparseExponentAccumulator J,W;for(unsigned j:{1u,2u})if(j==1?old[i].fraction.gasPresent():old[i].fraction.liquidPresent()){SparseExponentAccumulator beta,bt;beta.add(j==1?r.betaG:r.betaL);bt.add(j==1?r.betaG/r.TG:r.betaL/r.TL);J.add(old[i].mass(j).multiplied(beta));W.add(old[i].mass(j).multiplied(bt));}if(J.sign()<=0||W.sign()<=0)throw RecoveryError("CRV3_ENTROPY_WEIGHT_BLOCKER");return rationalDoubleView(W,J).value;}double J=0,W=0;if(old[i].q[1]>0){J+=old[i].q[1]*r.betaG;W+=old[i].q[1]*r.betaG/r.TG;}if(old[i].q[2]>0){J+=old[i].q[2]*r.betaL;W+=old[i].q[2]*r.betaL/r.TL;}if(!(J>0&&W>0&&std::isfinite(J)&&std::isfinite(W)))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_ENTROPY_WEIGHT_BLOCKER");return W/J;};
    m.heat+=dx_*z.heating[i];m.entropy+=dx_*z.heating[i]*.5*(weight(a)+weight(b));m.minHeat=std::min(m.minHeat,z.heating[i]);
   }
  };
  auto evaluate=[&](double dt){TemporalCandidateCheck c;try{
   if(!(dt>0&&std::isfinite(dt)&&dt<=cap))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_JOINT_BOUND");
   TemporalState trial=anchor;auto pre=viscous(anchor.committed,.5*dt);trial.committed=pre.cells;
   if(dt>bounds(pre.cells).joint)throw RecoveryError("CRV3_POST_VISCOUS_JOINT_BOUND");
   fiveEqTemporalDriver hyper(op_,rec_,journal_);auto h=hyper.advance(trial,dt);if(h.dt!=dt){if(h.dt>0&&h.dt<dt)hyperHint=h.dt;throw RecoveryError("CRV3_SPLIT_DT_MISMATCH");}
   guard(trial.committed,anchor.committed);auto postAnchor=trial.committed;auto post=viscous(postAnchor,.5*dt);trial.committed=post.cells;
   trial.old=anchor.committed;trial.oldVolume=anchor.volume;guard(trial.committed,anchor.committed);
   // The alpha/source bound was evaluated at the actual pre-viscous anchor.
   Metrics m;m.linear=std::max(pre.linearResidual,post.linearResidual);integrate(anchor.committed,pre,m);integrate(postAnchor,post,m);
   legal[dt]=trial;diagnostics[dt]=m;c.valid=true;c.reason="PASS";
  }catch(const std::exception&e){c.reason=e.what();}return c;};
  auto connected=[&](){
   // A nested TD1 selection supplies guidance, not a commit. Rebuild and fully
   // validate that dt with both viscous halves from the original anchor first.
   double hint=hyperHint;if(hint>0&&hint<=proposal&&evaluate(hint).valid)return hint;
   double lo=0,hi=proposal;bool exit=false;for(unsigned j=1;j<=32;++j){double x=proposal*j/32;if(!evaluate(x).valid){hi=x;exit=true;break;}lo=x;}if(exit)for(unsigned j=0;j<52;++j){double x=lo+(hi-lo)/2;if(x==lo||x==hi)break;if(evaluate(x).valid)lo=x;else hi=x;}return lo;};
  auto selection=selectTemporalCandidate(anchor.time,proposal,cap,evaluate,connected);
  if(!(selection.dt>0)){
   if(journal_){journal_->record(anchor.step,anchor.time,anchor.lastDt,result.bounds.joint,result.bounds.hyperbolic,request,cap,anchor.lastDt>0?2*anchor.lastDt:cap,proposal,selection);journal_->snapshot("crv3_blocked_full_anchor",anchor.step,anchor.time,anchor.lastDt,anchor.committed,anchor.volume);}
   throw RecoveryError(selection.blocker);
  }
  auto chosen=legal.find(selection.dt);if(chosen==legal.end())throw RecoveryError("CRV3_UNVALIDATED_COMMIT");
  if(!(chosen->second.time>anchor.time&&std::isfinite(chosen->second.time)))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_TIME_NONPROGRESS");
  s=std::move(chosen->second);result.dt=selection.dt;result.rejected=selection.rejected;const auto&m=diagnostics[selection.dt];result.linearResidual=m.linear;result.heatingMin=m.minHeat;result.heatingIntegral=m.heat;result.entropyProductionIntegral=m.entropy;if(!selection.tested.empty()&&!selection.tested.front().stateValid)result.firstRejection=selection.tested.front().reason;return result;
 }
};
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqPrecisionOperator.H"
#include <vector>
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
namespace fiveEq { namespace design {
struct KEWorkStep {std::vector<PrecisionCell> cells;std::vector<double> heating,stress;double linearResidual=0;};
inline KEWorkStep keWorkMidpointDense(const std::vector<PrecisionCell>&old,double dx,double dt,double mu){
 if(old.size()<4||!(dx>0)||!(dt>0)||!(mu>0)||!std::isfinite(dx)||!std::isfinite(dt)||!std::isfinite(mu))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_VISCOUS_STEP_ARGUMENT");
 const unsigned N=old.size();std::vector<double>rho(N),u(N),rhs(N),un(N),mid(N);std::vector<std::vector<double>>A(N,std::vector<double>(N,0));const double t=.5*dt*mu/(dx*dx);
 for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){rho[i]=old[i].rhoView();if(!(rho[i]>0))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_POSITIVE_MASS");u[i]=old[i].q[3]/rho[i];}
 for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){unsigned l=(i+N-1)%N,r=(i+1)%N;A[i][i]=rho[i]+2*t;A[i][l]-=t;A[i][r]-=t;rhs[i]=(rho[i]-2*t)*u[i]+t*(u[l]+u[r]);}
 auto original=A;auto originalRHS=rhs;
 // Verification prototype: dense solve. Replace with cyclic tridiagonal solve
 // before running large PDE grids; the equations and checks remain identical.
 for(unsigned k=0;k<N;++k){unsigned pivot=k;for(unsigned i=k+1;i<N;++i)if(std::abs(A[i][k])>std::abs(A[pivot][k]))pivot=i;if(!(std::abs(A[pivot][k])>0))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_LINEAR_SINGULAR");std::swap(A[k],A[pivot]);std::swap(rhs[k],rhs[pivot]);for(unsigned i=k+1;i<N;++i){double f=A[i][k]/A[k][k];A[i][k]=0;for(unsigned j=k+1;j<N;++j)A[i][j]-=f*A[k][j];rhs[i]-=f*rhs[k];}}
 for(int i=int(N)-1;i>=0;--i){double v=rhs[i];for(unsigned j=i+1;j<N;++j)v-=A[i][j]*un[j];un[i]=v/A[i][i];mid[i]=.5*(u[i]+un[i]);}
 KEWorkStep z;z.cells=old;z.heating.resize(N);z.stress.resize(N);std::vector<double>work(N);
 for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){unsigned r=(i+1)%N;z.stress[i]=mu*(mid[r]-mid[i])/dx;work[i]=.5*(mid[r]+mid[i])*z.stress[i];long double residual=-originalRHS[i],scale=std::abs(originalRHS[i]);for(unsigned j=0;j<N;++j){long double term=static_cast<long double>(original[i][j])*un[j];residual+=term;scale+=std::abs(term);}z.linearResidual=std::max(z.linearResidual,double(std::abs(residual)/std::max(scale,1.L)));}
 for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){unsigned l=(i+N-1)%N,r=(i+1)%N;z.cells[i].q[3]=rho[i]*un[i];z.cells[i].q[6]+=dt*(work[i]-work[l])/dx;z.heating[i]=dt*mu/(2*dx*dx)*((mid[r]-mid[i])*(mid[r]-mid[i])+(mid[i]-mid[l])*(mid[i]-mid[l]));if(!std::isfinite(z.cells[i].q[3])||!std::isfinite(z.cells[i].q[6]))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_NONFINITE_STEP");}
 return z;
}
inline KEWorkStep keWorkMidpoint(const std::vector<PrecisionCell>&old,double dx,double dt,double mu){
 if(old.size()<4||!(dx>0)||!(dt>0)||!(mu>0)||!std::isfinite(dx)||!std::isfinite(dt)||!std::isfinite(mu))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_VISCOUS_STEP_ARGUMENT");
 const unsigned N=old.size();const long double t=.5L*dt*mu/(static_cast<long double>(dx)*dx);std::vector<long double>rho(N),u(N),b(N),rhs(N);
 for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){rho[i]=old[i].rhoView();if(!(rho[i]>0))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_POSITIVE_MASS");u[i]=old[i].q[3]/rho[i];b[i]=rho[i]+2*t;}
 for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)rhs[i]=(rho[i]-2*t)*u[i]+t*(u[(i+N-1)%N]+u[(i+1)%N]);
 const long double gamma=-b[0],corner=-t;b[0]-=gamma;b[N-1]-=corner*corner/gamma;
 auto solve=[&](const std::vector<long double>&r){std::vector<long double>pivot=b,work=r,x(N);for(unsigned i=1;i<N;++i){if(!(pivot[i-1]>0))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_POSITIVE_LINEAR_PIVOT");long double f=-t/pivot[i-1];pivot[i]-=f*(-t);work[i]-=f*work[i-1];}x[N-1]=work[N-1]/pivot[N-1];for(int i=int(N)-2;i>=0;--i)x[i]=(work[i]+t*x[i+1])/pivot[i];return x;};
 auto un=solve(rhs);std::vector<long double>aux(N,0);aux[0]=gamma;aux[N-1]=corner;auto correction=solve(aux);long double factor=(un[0]+corner*un[N-1]/gamma)/(1+correction[0]+corner*correction[N-1]/gamma);std::vector<double>mid(N);for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){un[i]-=factor*correction[i];mid[i]=double(.5L*(u[i]+un[i]));}
 KEWorkStep z;z.cells=old;z.heating.resize(N);z.stress.resize(N);std::vector<double>work(N);
 for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){unsigned l=(i+N-1)%N,r=(i+1)%N;z.stress[i]=mu*(mid[r]-mid[i])/dx;work[i]=.5*(mid[r]+mid[i])*z.stress[i];long double residual=(rho[i]+2*t)*un[i]-t*un[l]-t*un[r]-rhs[i],scale=std::abs((rho[i]+2*t)*un[i])+std::abs(t*un[l])+std::abs(t*un[r])+std::abs(rhs[i]);z.linearResidual=std::max(z.linearResidual,double(std::abs(residual)/std::max(1.L,scale)));}
 for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){unsigned l=(i+N-1)%N,r=(i+1)%N;z.cells[i].q[3]=double(rho[i]*un[i]);z.cells[i].q[6]+=dt*(work[i]-work[l])/dx;z.heating[i]=dt*mu/(2*dx*dx)*((mid[r]-mid[i])*(mid[r]-mid[i])+(mid[i]-mid[l])*(mid[i]-mid[l]));if(!std::isfinite(z.cells[i].q[3])||!std::isfinite(z.cells[i].q[6]))throw RecoveryError("CRV3_NONFINITE_STEP");}
 return z;
}
}}

// Explicit archival schema conversion. Historical physics/EOS identities are
// retained. Historical archives cannot silently become active CRV3 restarts.
struct LegacySpeciesAudit {
 unsigned level,cell;std::string mG,mY0,mY1,sumIndependent,dependent,storedDependent,roundedDependent,difference,classification;
};
struct LegacyMigrationArchive {TemporalState state;std::string originalBytes,physics,eos,species,gasSHA;std::vector<LegacySpeciesAudit> speciesAudit;};

 static std::string dyadic(const scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator& x){scaledV2::Dyadic d;if(!x.compact(d))throw RecoveryError("LEGACY_AUDIT_DYADIC_RANGE");if(d.n==0)return "0*2^0";auto n=d.n<0?-d.n:d.n;unsigned z=boost::multiprecision::lsb(n);d.n>>=z;d.e=scaledV2::checkedAdd(d.e,z);std::ostringstream o;o<<d.n<<"*2^"<<d.e;return o.str();}
 static LegacySpeciesAudit audit(unsigned level,unsigned id,const PrecisionCell& c){using namespace scaledV2;SparseExponentAccumulator g,y0,y1,dep,old,rounded;g.add(c.q[1]);y0.add(c.q[7]);y1.add(c.q[8]);auto sum=y0;sum.add(y1);dep=g;dep.add(sum,-1);old.add(c.q[9]);rounded.add(c.q[1]-(c.q[7]+c.q[8]));auto diff=dep;diff.add(old,-1);return {level,id,dyadic(g),dyadic(y0),dyadic(y1),dyadic(sum),dyadic(dep),dyadic(old),dyadic(rounded),dyadic(diff),dep.sign()<0?"LEGACY_ROUNDING_ONLY_COMPATIBLE":"EXACT_COMPATIBLE"};}
 static std::string hexBytes(const std::string& s){const char* h="0123456789abcdef";std::string r;r.reserve(2*s.size());for(unsigned char c:s){r+=h[c>>4];r+=h[c&15];}return r;}

 static LegacyMigrationArchive parse(const std::string& all,const std::string& gasSHA){

 static std::string body(const LegacyMigrationArchive& a){
  // Origin bytes are immutable provenance, never recovery/flux authority. They
  // preserve every legacy Q9 view bit, including legacy dependent-rounding bits.
  std::ostringstream o;o<<"fiveEqLegacyArchive-v1 LEGACY_ARCHIVE_ONLY LF SHA256 "<<a.physics<<' '<<a.eos<<' '<<a.species<<' '<<a.gasSHA<<' '<<authoritySHA256(a.originalBytes)<<" EOS_NOT_REVALIDATED_ARCHIVE_ONLY\n";

  for(unsigned level=0;level<2;++level){bool known=level?s.oldClockKnown:true;o<<"level "<<level<<' '<<(known?(level?s.oldStep:s.step):0)<<' '<<hexDouble(known?(level?s.oldTime:s.time):0)<<' '<<hexDouble(known?(level?s.oldLastDt:s.lastDt):0)<<' '<<known<<'\n';const auto& cells=level?s.old:s.committed;const auto& volumes=level?s.oldVolume:s.volume;for(unsigned i=0;i<cells.size();++i){const auto& c=cells[i];auto f=c.fraction.scaledAuthority();o<<i<<' '<<hexDouble(volumes[i])<<' '<<int(f.side)<<' '<<hexDouble(f.q.hi)<<' '<<hexDouble(f.q.lo)<<' '<<f.q.exp2;for(const auto& x:c.authority){if(x.tag()!=scaledV2::AuthorityTag::NORMAL_DOUBLE)throw RecoveryError("MIGRATION_NOT_EXACT_BINARY64_SOURCE");o<<" NORMAL_DOUBLE "<<hexDouble(x.normalPayload());}for(unsigned j:{3u,4u,5u,6u})o<<' '<<hexDouble(c.q[j]);o<<'\n';}}
  return o.str();
 }
public:
