// Explicit binary64 payload, portable ASCII hex. No decoded-alpha transport.
inline std::uint64_t exactBits(double x){static_assert(sizeof(double)==8&&std::numeric_limits<double>::is_iec559&&std::numeric_limits<double>::digits==53,"IEEE binary64 required");std::uint64_t u;std::memcpy(&u,&x,8);return u;}
inline double fromExactBits(std::uint64_t u){double x;std::memcpy(&x,&u,8);return x;}
inline std::string hexDouble(double x){std::ostringstream s;s<<std::hex<<std::setfill('0')<<std::setw(16)<<exactBits(x);return s.str();}
inline double parseHexDouble(const std::string& s){
 if(s.size()!=16||s.find_first_not_of("0123456789abcdefABCDEF")!=std::string::npos)throw RecoveryError("BAD_EXACT_BITS");
 std::uint64_t u=0;std::istringstream in(s);in>>std::hex>>u;double x=fromExactBits(u);if(!std::isfinite(x))throw RecoveryError("NONFINITE_RESTART");return x;
}
inline PrecisionFraction p2Coordinate(double s){
 if(!std::isfinite(s)||std::abs(s)>.5)throw RecoveryError("BAD_P2_COORDINATE");
 return PrecisionFraction::minority(std::abs(s),std::signbit(s),FractionMode::P2);
}
inline bool exactCellEqual(const PrecisionCell& a,const PrecisionCell& b){
 if(a.representationV2!=b.representationV2)return false;
 if(a.representationV2){if(a.fraction.scaledAuthority().side!=b.fraction.scaledAuthority().side||a.fraction.scaledAuthority().q.wire()!=b.fraction.scaledAuthority().q.wire())return false;for(unsigned k=0;k<4;++k){if(a.authority[k].tag()!=b.authority[k].tag())return false;if(a.authority[k].tag()==scaledV2::AuthorityTag::NORMAL_DOUBLE){if(exactBits(a.authority[k].normalPayload())!=exactBits(b.authority[k].normalPayload()))return false;}else if(a.authority[k].scaled().wire()!=b.authority[k].scaled().wire())return false;}for(unsigned j:{3u,4u,5u,6u})if(exactBits(a.q[j])!=exactBits(b.q[j]))return false;return true;}
 if(exactBits(a.fraction.payload().hi)!=exactBits(b.fraction.payload().hi))return false;
 for(unsigned j=1;j<10;++j)if(exactBits(a.q[j])!=exactBits(b.q[j]))return false;
 return true;
}
inline std::vector<unsigned char> packP2(const PrecisionFraction& f){
 if(f.mode()!=FractionMode::P2)throw RecoveryError("P2_REQUIRED");
 auto u=exactBits(f.payload().hi);std::vector<unsigned char> b(8);for(unsigned j=0;j<8;++j)b[j]=(u>>(56-8*j))&255;return b;
}
inline PrecisionFraction unpackP2(const std::vector<unsigned char>& b){
 if(b.size()!=8){throw RecoveryError("BAD_P2_BUFFER_SIZE");}
 std::uint64_t u=0;for(auto x:b)u=(u<<8)|x;return p2Coordinate(fromExactBits(u));
}
struct TemporalState {std::vector<PrecisionCell> committed,old;std::vector<double> volume,oldVolume;double time=0,lastDt=0;unsigned step=0;double oldTime=0,oldLastDt=0;unsigned oldStep=0;bool oldClockKnown=true;};
inline std::uint64_t restartChecksum(const std::string& s){std::uint64_t h=14695981039346656037ull;for(unsigned char c:s){h^=c;h*=1099511628211ull;}return h;}
class fiveEqP2IO {
public:
 static void write(const std::string& path,const TemporalState& s,const std::string& species,MechanicalModel model=MechanicalModel::HISTORICAL_K0){
  if(s.committed.empty()||s.old.size()!=s.committed.size()||s.volume.size()!=s.committed.size()||s.oldVolume.size()!=s.committed.size())throw RecoveryError("INCOMPLETE_RESTART");
  std::ostringstream body;body<<(model==MechanicalModel::HISTORICAL_K0?"fiveEqRestart-v1":"fiveEqRestart-v2")<<" P2-v1 binary64-hex-msb "<<liquidEOS::productionLiquidEOSVersion()<<" "<<species;
  if(model!=MechanicalModel::HISTORICAL_K0)body<<' '<<modelTag(model);
  body<<'\n'<<s.step<<' '<<hexDouble(s.time)<<' '<<hexDouble(s.lastDt)<<' '<<s.committed.size()<<'\n';
  for(unsigned level=0;level<2;++level){const auto& q=level?s.old:s.committed;const auto& v=level?s.oldVolume:s.volume;
   for(unsigned i=0;i<q.size();++i){if(q[i].fraction.mode()!=FractionMode::P2)throw RecoveryError("P2_REQUIRED");body<<i<<' '<<hexDouble(v[i])<<' '<<hexDouble(q[i].fraction.payload().hi);for(unsigned j=1;j<10;++j)body<<' '<<hexDouble(q[i].q[j]);body<<'\n';}}
  std::ofstream out(path,std::ios::binary);if(!out)throw RecoveryError("RESTART_WRITE_FAILED");out<<body.str()<<"checksum "<<std::hex<<restartChecksum(body.str())<<'\n';out.close();if(!out)throw RecoveryError("RESTART_WRITE_FAILED");
 }
 static TemporalState read(const std::string& path,const std::string& species,const PrecisionOperator& op,const std::string& expectedEOS=liquidEOS::productionLiquidEOSVersion(),const std::string& expectedModel=""){
  std::ifstream file(path,std::ios::binary);if(!file)throw RecoveryError("RESTART_READ_FAILED");std::string all((std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(file)),{});auto k=all.rfind("checksum ");if(k==std::string::npos)throw RecoveryError("MISSING_RESTART_CHECKSUM");
  auto body=all.substr(0,k);std::istringstream tail(all.substr(k));std::string marker,extra;std::uint64_t sum;tail>>marker>>std::hex>>sum;if(!tail||tail>>extra||sum!=restartChecksum(body))throw RecoveryError("RESTART_CHECKSUM_FAILED");
  std::istringstream in(body);std::string schema,p2,arch,eos,sp,model="HISTORICAL_K0";in>>schema>>p2>>arch>>eos>>sp;if(schema=="fiveEqRestart-v2")in>>model;

  TemporalState s;std::string t,dt;std::size_t N;in>>s.step>>t>>dt>>N;if(!in||N==0||N>10000000)throw RecoveryError("BAD_RESTART_HEADER");s.time=parseHexDouble(t);s.lastDt=parseHexDouble(dt);if(s.time<0||s.lastDt<0)throw RecoveryError("BAD_RESTART_TIME");
  // Legacy schema stores only the common current clock. Missing historical
  // clock fields remain explicitly unavailable, never inferred by subtraction.
  s.oldClockKnown=s.step==0&&s.time==0&&s.lastDt==0;
  for(unsigned level=0;level<2;++level){auto& q=level?s.old:s.committed;auto& v=level?s.oldVolume:s.volume;
   for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){unsigned id;std::string vv,coord;in>>id>>vv>>coord;if(!in||id!=i)throw RecoveryError("BAD_RESTART_CELL_ID");double V=parseHexDouble(vv);if(!(V>0))throw RecoveryError("BAD_RESTART_VOLUME");PrecisionCell c;c.q.fill(0);c.fraction=p2Coordinate(parseHexDouble(coord));c.q[0]=c.fraction.liquidValue();for(unsigned j=1;j<10;++j){std::string x;in>>x;c.q[j]=parseHexDouble(x);}op.recover(c);q.push_back(c);v.push_back(V);}}
  if(in>>extra){throw RecoveryError("TRAILING_RESTART_DATA");}
  return s;

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic.H"
#include <cstdint>
#include <algorithm>
namespace fiveEq {
// Owned by caller/rank/thread. No query-level reductions or singleton counters.
struct fiveEqRecoveryCounters {
    std::uint64_t nPureGas=0,nMixed=0,nPureLiquid=0,nBufferLiquidStates=0,nInvalid=0;
    std::uint64_t nNewtonIterations=0,nFallbackSteps=0,noRootCount=0;
    unsigned maxIterations=0;
    void record(const fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic& d){
        if(d.status!=RecoveryStatus::SUCCESS)++nInvalid;
        else {if(d.recoveryMode==RecoveryMode::PURE_GAS)++nPureGas;
            if(d.recoveryMode==RecoveryMode::MIXED)++nMixed;
            if(d.recoveryMode==RecoveryMode::PURE_LIQUID)++nPureLiquid;}
        if(d.liquidValidity==PhaseValidity::BUFFER)++nBufferLiquidStates;
        nNewtonIterations+=d.iterations;nFallbackSteps+=d.fallbackCount;
        maxIterations=std::max(maxIterations,d.iterations);
        if(d.code==RecoveryCode::NO_PRESSURE_ROOT)++noRootCount;
    }
    void merge(const fiveEqRecoveryCounters& b){
        nPureGas+=b.nPureGas;nMixed+=b.nMixed;nPureLiquid+=b.nPureLiquid;
        nBufferLiquidStates+=b.nBufferLiquidStates;nInvalid+=b.nInvalid;
        nNewtonIterations+=b.nNewtonIterations;nFallbackSteps+=b.nFallbackSteps;
        noRootCount+=b.noRootCount;maxIterations=std::max(maxIterations,b.maxIterations);
    }
};
}

#pragma once
#include "../fiveEqThermo/fiveEqState.H"
#include <sstream>
namespace fiveEq {
enum class RecoveryStatus { SUCCESS, STATE_FAILURE, CONFIGURATION_FAILURE, INTERNAL_FAILURE };
enum class RecoveryCode { OK,INVALID_ALPHA,NEGATIVE_PARTIAL_MASS,INVALID_TOTAL_DENSITY,
    PHASE_PRESENCE_INCONSISTENT,GAS_EOS_INVALID,LIQUID_EOS_INVALID,
    NO_COMMON_PRESSURE_INTERVAL,NO_PRESSURE_ROOT,NONPOSITIVE_ROOT_DERIVATIVE,
    ROOT_NOT_CONVERGED,INVALID_SOUND_SPEED,NONFINITE_STATE,INTERNAL_PROGRAM_ERROR,
    INVALID_COMPOSITION,INVALID_DIMENSIONS,PHASE_PRECISION_UNRESOLVED };
enum class PhaseValidity { ABSENT, VALID, NORMAL, BUFFER, INVALID, NOT_EVALUATED };
enum class RecoveryContext { CELL,FACE };
struct fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic {
    RecoveryStatus status=RecoveryStatus::STATE_FAILURE;
    RecoveryCode code=RecoveryCode::INTERNAL_PROGRAM_ERROR;
    RecoveryContext context=RecoveryContext::CELL;
    RecoveryMode recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::INVALID;
    double alphaL=0,mG=0,mL=0,rhoMix=0,pBracketMin=0,pBracketMax=0,finalPressure=0,residual=0;
    unsigned iterations=0,fallbackCount=0;
    PhaseValidity gasValidity=PhaseValidity::NOT_EVALUATED,liquidValidity=PhaseValidity::NOT_EVALUATED;
    bool guessProvided=false,guessIgnored=false;
    // Explicit availability: absent values are zero placeholders, not valid pressure roots.
    bool bracketAvailable=false,pressureAvailable=false,residualAvailable=false;
};
const char* recoveryCodeName(RecoveryCode);
std::string formatDiagnostic(const fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic&);
}

#include "fiveEqRuntimeBridge.H"
#include <cmath>
#include <cstring>
namespace fiveEq {
fiveEqRuntimeBridge::fiveEqRuntimeBridge(const std::string& path,const Foam::wordList& names,const Foam::dictionary& metadata):
    gas_(path),liquid_(),speciesMap_(names,metadata,gas_),recovery_(gas_,liquid_){}
const char* recoveryCodeName(RecoveryCode code){
    switch(code){
#define CODE(x) case RecoveryCode::x:return #x
    CODE(OK);CODE(INVALID_ALPHA);CODE(NEGATIVE_PARTIAL_MASS);CODE(INVALID_TOTAL_DENSITY);
    CODE(PHASE_PRESENCE_INCONSISTENT);CODE(GAS_EOS_INVALID);CODE(LIQUID_EOS_INVALID);
    CODE(NO_COMMON_PRESSURE_INTERVAL);CODE(NO_PRESSURE_ROOT);CODE(NONPOSITIVE_ROOT_DERIVATIVE);
    CODE(ROOT_NOT_CONVERGED);CODE(INVALID_SOUND_SPEED);CODE(NONFINITE_STATE);
    CODE(INTERNAL_PROGRAM_ERROR);CODE(INVALID_COMPOSITION);CODE(INVALID_DIMENSIONS);CODE(PHASE_PRECISION_UNRESOLVED);
#undef CODE
    }return "INTERNAL_PROGRAM_ERROR";
}
std::string formatDiagnostic(const fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic& d){
    std::ostringstream out;out.precision(17);out<<recoveryCodeName(d.code)<<" alphaL="<<d.alphaL<<" mG="<<d.mG<<" mL="<<d.mL<<" rhoMix="<<d.rhoMix
      <<" bracketAvailable="<<d.bracketAvailable<<" bracket=["<<d.pBracketMin<<','<<d.pBracketMax<<"] p="<<d.finalPressure<<" residual="<<d.residual<<" iterations="<<d.iterations<<" fallback="<<d.fallbackCount;
    return out.str();
}
static RecoveryCode classify(const char* reason,bool liquid){
    if(std::strstr(reason,"SUBNORMAL_PRECISION_UNRESOLVED"))return RecoveryCode::PHASE_PRECISION_UNRESOLVED;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"NO_COMMON_VALID_PRESSURE_INTERVAL"))return RecoveryCode::NO_COMMON_PRESSURE_INTERVAL;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"NO_PHYSICAL_ENERGY_ROOT")||std::strstr(reason,"NO_GAS_ENERGY_ROOT")||std::strstr(reason,"energy not bracketed"))return RecoveryCode::NO_PRESSURE_ROOT;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"INVALID_THERMODYNAMIC_ROOT_DERIVATIVE"))return RecoveryCode::NONPOSITIVE_ROOT_DERIVATIVE;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"NOT_CONVERGED")||std::strstr(reason,"iterations"))return RecoveryCode::ROOT_NOT_CONVERGED;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"RECOVERED_STATE_VALIDATION_FAILED"))return RecoveryCode::ROOT_NOT_CONVERGED;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"INVALID_ALLAIRE_ACOUSTICS"))return RecoveryCode::INVALID_SOUND_SPEED;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"ABSENT_PHASE")||std::strstr(reason,"PRESENT_PHASE")||std::strstr(reason,"DISAPPEARING_PHASE"))return RecoveryCode::PHASE_PRESENCE_INCONSISTENT;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"INVALID_TOTAL_DENSITY"))return RecoveryCode::INVALID_TOTAL_DENSITY;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"INVALID_INTERNAL_ENERGY"))return RecoveryCode::NONFINITE_STATE;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"INVALID_GAS_COMPOSITION"))return RecoveryCode::INVALID_COMPOSITION;
    if(liquid||std::strstr(reason,"LIQUID_PRESSURE_INTERVAL")||std::strstr(reason,"INVALID_LIQUID"))return RecoveryCode::LIQUID_EOS_INVALID;
    if(std::strstr(reason,"INVALID_GAS"))return RecoveryCode::GAS_EOS_INVALID;
    return RecoveryCode::INTERNAL_PROGRAM_ERROR;
}
bool fiveEqRuntimeBridge::tryRecover(const fiveEqRuntimeInput& q,fiveEqRuntimeState& out,fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic& d,PressureGuess guess,RecoveryContext context) const {
    out=fiveEqRuntimeState();d=fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic();d.context=context;d.alphaL=q.alphaL.value();d.mG=q.mG.value();d.mL=q.mL.value();d.rhoMix=d.mG+d.mL;d.guessProvided=guess.supplied;
    auto fail=[&](RecoveryCode code){d.code=code;d.status=code==RecoveryCode::INTERNAL_PROGRAM_ERROR?RecoveryStatus::INTERNAL_FAILURE:code==RecoveryCode::INVALID_DIMENSIONS?RecoveryStatus::CONFIGURATION_FAILURE:RecoveryStatus::STATE_FAILURE;return false;};
    if(q.alphaL.dimensions()!=Foam::dimensionSet(0,0,0,0,0,0,0)||q.mG.dimensions()!=Foam::dimensionSet(1,-3,0,0,0,0,0)||q.mL.dimensions()!=Foam::dimensionSet(1,-3,0,0,0,0,0)||q.M.dimensions()!=Foam::dimensionSet(1,-2,-1,0,0,0,0)||q.Et.dimensions()!=Foam::dimensionSet(1,-1,-2,0,0,0,0))return fail(RecoveryCode::INVALID_DIMENSIONS);
    if(!std::isfinite(d.alphaL)||!std::isfinite(d.mG)||!std::isfinite(d.mL)||!std::isfinite(q.Et.value())||!std::isfinite(d.rhoMix))return fail(RecoveryCode::NONFINITE_STATE);
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)if(!std::isfinite(q.M.value()[j]))return fail(RecoveryCode::NONFINITE_STATE);
    if(d.alphaL<0||d.alphaL>1)return fail(RecoveryCode::INVALID_ALPHA);
    if(d.mG<0||d.mL<0)return fail(RecoveryCode::NEGATIVE_PARTIAL_MASS);
    if(d.rhoMix<=0)return fail(RecoveryCode::INVALID_TOTAL_DENSITY);
    fiveEqConservativeState state;state.alphaL=d.alphaL;state.mG=d.mG;state.mL=d.mL;state.Et=q.Et.value();
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)state.M[j]=q.M.value()[j];
    // Exact pure-liquid routing must never inspect undefined/absent gas composition.
    if(!(state.alphaL==1&&state.mG==0)&&!speciesMap_.map(q.YG,state.YG))return fail(RecoveryCode::INVALID_COMPOSITION);
    RecoveryOptions options;
    if(guess.supplied){options.initialGuess=InitialGuess::PROVIDED;options.knownPressure=guess.value;}
    fiveEqRecoveredState progress;RecoveryCode code=RecoveryCode::OK;
    try{out.thermo=recovery_.recover(state,options,&progress);}
    catch(const liquidEOS::EOSQueryError& e){code=classify(e.what(),true);}
    catch(const RecoveryError& e){code=classify(e.what(),false);}
    catch(const std::exception&){code=RecoveryCode::INTERNAL_PROGRAM_ERROR;}
    catch(...){code=RecoveryCode::INTERNAL_PROGRAM_ERROR;}
    d.recoveryMode=progress.recoveryMode;d.pBracketMin=progress.bracketMin;d.pBracketMax=progress.bracketMax;d.finalPressure=progress.p;d.residual=progress.energyResidual;d.iterations=progress.iterations;d.fallbackCount=progress.calls.fallback;
    d.bracketAvailable=progress.bracketMin>0&&progress.bracketMax>0;
    d.pressureAvailable=progress.p>0&&std::isfinite(progress.p);d.residualAvailable=progress.calls.residual>0||code==RecoveryCode::OK;
    d.gasValidity=progress.gasValidity=="VALID_JANAF"?PhaseValidity::VALID:(state.alphaL==1&&state.mG==0)?PhaseValidity::ABSENT:PhaseValidity::NOT_EVALUATED;
    d.liquidValidity=progress.liquidValidity=="NORMAL"?PhaseValidity::NORMAL:progress.liquidValidity=="BUFFER"?PhaseValidity::BUFFER:(state.alphaL==0&&state.mL==0)?PhaseValidity::ABSENT:PhaseValidity::NOT_EVALUATED;
    if(code==RecoveryCode::GAS_EOS_INVALID)d.gasValidity=PhaseValidity::INVALID;
    if(code==RecoveryCode::LIQUID_EOS_INVALID)d.liquidValidity=PhaseValidity::INVALID;
    d.guessIgnored=guess.supplied&&(!std::isfinite(guess.value)||!d.bracketAvailable||guess.value<=d.pBracketMin||guess.value>=d.pBracketMax||d.recoveryMode!=RecoveryMode::MIXED);
    if(code!=RecoveryCode::OK){out=fiveEqRuntimeState();return fail(code);}
    out.U=Foam::vector(out.thermo.U[0],out.thermo.U[1],out.thermo.U[2]);d.status=RecoveryStatus::SUCCESS;d.code=RecoveryCode::OK;return true;
}
fiveEqRuntimeState fiveEqRuntimeBridge::recoverOrFail(const fiveEqRuntimeInput& q,PressureGuess guess) const {
    fiveEqRuntimeState state;fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic diagnostic;
    if(!tryRecover(q,state,diagnostic,guess))throw RecoveryError(formatDiagnostic(diagnostic));
    return state; // Caller may choose controlled fatal; bridge never invokes it.
}
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqSpeciesMap.H"
#include "fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic.H"
#include "../fiveEqThermo/fiveEqStateRecovery.H"
#include "dimensionedScalar.H"
#include "dimensionedVector.H"
namespace fiveEq {
struct PressureGuess { bool supplied=false; Foam::scalar value=0; };
struct fiveEqRuntimeInput {
    Foam::dimensionedScalar alphaL,mG,mL,Et;
    Foam::dimensionedVector M;
    Foam::scalarField YG;
    // Test/helper construction; dimensioned inputs remain independently checkable.
    fiveEqRuntimeInput(Foam::scalar a,Foam::scalar g,Foam::scalar l,const Foam::vector& momentum,Foam::scalar energy,const Foam::scalarField& Y):
        alphaL("alphaL",Foam::dimensionSet(0,0,0,0,0,0,0),a),
        mG("mG",Foam::dimensionSet(1,-3,0,0,0,0,0),g),mL("mL",Foam::dimensionSet(1,-3,0,0,0,0,0),l),
        Et("Et",Foam::dimensionSet(1,-1,-2,0,0,0,0),energy),M("M",Foam::dimensionSet(1,-2,-1,0,0,0,0),momentum),YG(Y){}
};
struct fiveEqRuntimeState { fiveEqRecoveredState thermo;Foam::vector U=Foam::vector::zero; };
class fiveEqRuntimeBridge {
    const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator gas_;
    const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator liquid_;
    const fiveEqSpeciesMap speciesMap_;
    const fiveEqStateRecovery recovery_;
public:
    fiveEqRuntimeBridge(const std::string& gasDataPath,const Foam::wordList& runtimeNames,const Foam::dictionary& runtimeJANAF);
    fiveEqRuntimeBridge(const fiveEqRuntimeBridge&)=delete;
    fiveEqRuntimeBridge& operator=(const fiveEqRuntimeBridge&)=delete;
    bool tryRecover(const fiveEqRuntimeInput&,fiveEqRuntimeState&,fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic&,PressureGuess guess={},RecoveryContext context=RecoveryContext::CELL) const;
    bool recoverCellState(const fiveEqRuntimeInput& q,fiveEqRuntimeState& s,fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic& d,PressureGuess g={})const{return tryRecover(q,s,d,g,RecoveryContext::CELL);}
    bool recoverFaceState(const fiveEqRuntimeInput& q,fiveEqRuntimeState& s,fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic& d,PressureGuess g={})const{return tryRecover(q,s,d,g,RecoveryContext::FACE);}
    fiveEqRuntimeState recoverOrFail(const fiveEqRuntimeInput&,PressureGuess guess={}) const;
};
}

#include "fiveEqP2IO.H"

namespace fiveEq {
class fiveEqScaledP2IO {
 static const char* schema(){return "P2_SCALED_V2 TRACE_CONSERVATIVE_V2 Nminus1-scaled-v1 binary64-hex-msb int64-exp2 LF SHA256 clocks-v1 mesh-v1 migration-M1";}
 static std::string volumeIdentity(const std::vector<double>& volumes){std::ostringstream o;o<<volumes.size()<<'\n';for(unsigned i=0;i<volumes.size();++i){if(!(std::isfinite(volumes[i])&&volumes[i]>0))throw RecoveryError("BAD_RESTART_V3_VOLUME");o<<i<<' '<<hexDouble(volumes[i])<<'\n';}return authoritySHA256(o.str());}
 static void qualifiedMesh(const TemporalState& s){if(s.volume.empty()||s.oldVolume.size()!=s.volume.size())throw RecoveryError("RESTART_V3_INCOMPLETE_MESH");for(unsigned i=0;i<s.volume.size();++i)if(exactBits(s.volume[i])!=exactBits(s.volume[0])||exactBits(s.oldVolume[i])!=exactBits(s.volume[i]))throw RecoveryError("RESTART_V3_MESH_OUTSIDE_FIXED_UNIFORM_SCOPE");}

 static void scalar(std::ostream& out,const scaledV2::ScaledDD& x){if(!x.canonical())throw RecoveryError("RESTART_NONCANONICAL_SCALED");out<<hexDouble(x.hi)<<' '<<hexDouble(x.lo)<<' '<<x.exp2;}
 static scaledV2::ScaledDD scalar(std::istream& in){std::string h,l,e;in>>h>>l>>e;if(!in||e.empty()||e.find_first_not_of("-0123456789")!=std::string::npos)throw RecoveryError("BAD_SCALED_RESTART_RECORD");std::size_t pos;std::int64_t exp;try{exp=std::stoll(e,&pos);}catch(...){throw RecoveryError("REPRESENTATION_RANGE_BLOCKER");}if(pos!=e.size()||std::to_string(exp)!=e)throw RecoveryError("NONCANONICAL_RESTART_EXPONENT");scaledV2::ScaledDD s{parseHexDouble(h),parseHexDouble(l),exp};if(!s.canonical())throw RecoveryError("RESTART_NONCANONICAL_SCALED");return s;}

 static void write(const std::string& path,const TemporalState& s,const std::string& species,const std::string& gasSHA){
  if(s.committed.empty()||s.old.size()!=s.committed.size()||s.volume.size()!=s.committed.size()||s.oldVolume.size()!=s.committed.size())throw RecoveryError("INCOMPLETE_RESTART_V3");
  qualifiedMesh(s);

#include "fiveEqSpeciesMap.H"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
namespace fiveEq {
fiveEqSpeciesMap::fiveEqSpeciesMap(const Foam::wordList& names,const Foam::dictionary& data,const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator& evaluator){
    const auto expected=fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator::speciesNames();
    if(names.size()!=static_cast<Foam::label>(expected.size()))throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_UNSUPPORTED_MECHANISM_SIZE");
    for(unsigned i=0;i<expected.size();++i){
        unsigned found=0,index=0;
        for(Foam::label j=0;j<names.size();++j)if(names[j]==expected[i]){++found;index=j;}
        if(found!=1)throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_REQUIRED_NAME_MISSING_OR_DUPLICATE");
        if(!data.found(expected[i]))throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_JANAF_DATA_MISSING");
        const auto& d=data.subDict(expected[i]);
        if(!d.found("specie")||!d.found("thermodynamics"))throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_JANAF_METADATA_MISSING");
        const auto& sp=d.subDict("specie");const auto& th=d.subDict("thermodynamics");
        if(!sp.found("molWeight"))throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_MOLECULAR_WEIGHT_MISSING");
        for(const char* key:{"Tlow","Thigh","Tcommon","lowCpCoeffs","highCpCoeffs"})if(!th.found(key))throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_JANAF_METADATA_MISSING");
        const GasThermo actual(d);const auto& reference=evaluator.speciesData()[i];
        auto same=[](double a,double b){return std::isfinite(a)&&std::isfinite(b)&&std::abs(a-b)<=1e-12*std::max(1.,std::max(std::abs(a),std::abs(b)));};
        if(!same(actual.W(),reference.W()))throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_MOLECULAR_WEIGHT_MISMATCH");
        if(!same(actual.Tlow(),reference.Tlow())||!same(actual.Thigh(),reference.Thigh())||!same(actual.Tcommon(),reference.Tcommon()))throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_JANAF_RANGE_MISMATCH");
        for(unsigned j=0;j<7;++j)if(!same(actual.lowCpCoeffs()[j],reference.lowCpCoeffs()[j])||!same(actual.highCpCoeffs()[j],reference.highCpCoeffs()[j]))throw RecoveryError("SPECIES_MAP_JANAF_COEFFICIENT_MISMATCH");
        canonicalToRuntime_.push_back(index);
    }
}
bool fiveEqSpeciesMap::map(const Foam::scalarField& Y,std::vector<double>& canonical) const {
    if(Y.size()!=static_cast<Foam::label>(canonicalToRuntime_.size()))return false;
    canonical.resize(canonicalToRuntime_.size());double sum=0;
    for(unsigned i=0;i<canonical.size();++i){double y=Y[canonicalToRuntime_[i]];if(!std::isfinite(y)||y<0)return false;canonical[i]=y;sum+=y;}
    // Same explicit M6A2.1 budget. Accept tiny sum discrepancy as-is; no normalization.
    return std::isfinite(sum)&&std::abs(sum-1)<=1e-12;
}
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqP2IO.H"
#include "fiveEqPrecisionReconstruction.H"
#include "fiveEqTemporalPolicy.H"
#include <map>
namespace fiveEq {
struct TemporalStepResult {double dt=0;unsigned rejected=0;};
// Standalone fixed-mesh periodic Euler driver, no physical source activation.
class fiveEqTemporalDriver {
 const PrecisionOperator& op_;const PrecisionReconstruction& rec_;TemporalPolicyJournal* journal_;
public:
 fiveEqTemporalDriver(const PrecisionOperator& o,const PrecisionReconstruction& r,TemporalPolicyJournal* journal=nullptr):op_(o),rec_(r),journal_(journal){}
 TemporalStepResult advance(TemporalState& s,double requested,bool deliberateOversizedTrial=false)const {
  if(!(requested>0&&std::isfinite(requested)&&std::isfinite(s.time)&&s.lastDt>=0&&std::isfinite(s.lastDt))||s.volume.size()!=s.committed.size()||s.committed.empty())throw RecoveryError("BAD_TEMPORAL_STATE");
  if(journal_)journal_->snapshot("td1_last_anchor",s.step,s.time,s.lastDt,s.committed,s.volume);
  const auto anchor=s.committed;for(const auto& c:anchor)op_.recover(c);
  auto faces=rec_.reconstruct(anchor,op_.reconstructionStrategy());unsigned N=anchor.size();std::vector<PrecisionFace> F;
  for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)F.push_back(op_.face(faces[i][0],faces[(i+1)%N][1]));
  double joint=std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity(),acoustic=joint;
  for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){joint=std::min(joint,op_.jointBound(anchor[i],s.volume[i],F[(i+N-1)%N],F[i]));acoustic=std::min(acoustic,op_.acousticBound(anchor[i],s.volume[i],F[(i+N-1)%N],F[i]));}
  const double freshCap=std::min(requested,joint),growthCap=s.lastDt>0?2.0*s.lastDt:freshCap;
  if(!(freshCap>0&&std::isfinite(freshCap)))throw RecoveryError("NO_CONNECTED_DT_REQUIRES_DOMAIN_CLASSIFICATION");
  const double proposal=deliberateOversizedTrial?requested:temporalGrowthRequest(freshCap,s.lastDt);
  std::map<double,std::vector<PrecisionCell>> legal;std::vector<PrecisionCell> lastTrial;std::vector<unsigned char> lastBuilt;
  auto evaluate=[&](double dt){TemporalCandidateCheck check;check.valid=dt>0&&std::isfinite(dt);check.reason=check.valid?"PASS":"INVALID_TRIAL_DT";std::vector<PrecisionCell> trial;trial.reserve(N);std::vector<unsigned char> availability;availability.reserve(N);
   // Every trial starts at the same const anchor. No rejected update accumulates.
   for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){PrecisionCell c=anchor[i];bool built=false;try{c=op_.trial(anchor[i],s.volume[i],dt,F[(i+N-1)%N],F[i]);built=true;
     if(c.representationV2){if(c.fraction.mode()!=FractionMode::P2Scaled||!c.fraction.scaledAuthority().q.canonical())throw RecoveryError("TD1_SCALED_P2_INVARIANT_BLOCKER");for(unsigned j:{1u,2u})if(anchor[i].mass(j).sign()>0&&c.mass(j).sign()<=0)throw RecoveryError("TD1_PRESENT_INVARIANT_BLOCKER");}
     else {if(c.fraction.mode()!=FractionMode::P2||std::abs(c.fraction.payload().hi)>.5||c.fraction.payload().lo!=0||c.q[0]!=c.fraction.liquidValue())throw RecoveryError("TD1_P2_INVARIANT_BLOCKER");if((anchor[i].q[1]>0&&!(c.q[1]>0))||(anchor[i].q[2]>0&&!(c.q[2]>0)))throw RecoveryError("TD1_PRESENT_INVARIANT_BLOCKER");}op_.recover(c);
    }catch(const std::exception&e){if(check.valid){check.reason=e.what();check.cell=i;}check.valid=false;}
    if(built){
     // Exact original serial caller N-1 formulation, now validated before commit.
     if(c.representationV2)c.refreshViews();else c.q[9]=c.q[1]-(c.q[7]+c.q[8]);try{op_.recover(c);}catch(const std::exception&e){if(check.valid){check.reason=e.what();check.cell=i;}check.valid=false;}
    }trial.push_back(c);availability.push_back(built);
   }lastTrial=trial;lastBuilt=availability;if(check.valid)legal[dt]=trial;return check;};
  auto connected=[&](){double next=std::min(freshCap,proposal);for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)next=std::min(next,op_.connectedDt(anchor[i],s.volume[i],std::min(freshCap,proposal),F[(i+N-1)%N],F[i]));return next;};
  auto plan=selectTemporalCandidate(s.time,proposal,std::min(freshCap,proposal),evaluate,connected);
  if(journal_)journal_->record(s.step,s.time,s.lastDt>0?s.lastDt:requested,joint,acoustic,requested,freshCap,growthCap,proposal,plan);
  if(!(plan.dt>0)){if(journal_){journal_->snapshot("td1_blocked_anchor",s.step,s.time,s.lastDt,anchor,s.volume);journal_->snapshot("td1_blocked_trial",s.step,s.time,plan.tested.empty()?0:plan.tested.back().dt,lastTrial,s.volume);journal_->availability("td1_blocked_trial",lastBuilt);}throw RecoveryError(plan.blocker);}
  const double candidateTime=s.time+plan.dt;
  if(!(std::isfinite(candidateTime)&&candidateTime>s.time))throw RecoveryError("TD1_TIME_MONOTONICITY_BLOCKER");
  auto selected=legal.find(plan.dt);if(selected==legal.end())throw RecoveryError("TD1_UNVALIDATED_COMMIT_BLOCKER");
  s.old=anchor;s.oldVolume=s.volume;s.oldTime=s.time;s.oldLastDt=s.lastDt;s.oldStep=s.step;s.oldClockKnown=true;s.committed=selected->second;s.time=candidateTime;s.lastDt=plan.dt;++s.step;
  TemporalStepResult result;result.dt=plan.dt;result.rejected=plan.rejected;return result;
 }
};
}

#pragma once
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
#include <limits>
#include <string>
#include <vector>
#include <fstream>
#include <iomanip>
#include <sstream>
#include <cstring>
#include <cstdint>
namespace fiveEq {
// Project-local policy only. Evaluators retain the frozen physical operator.
struct TemporalCandidateCheck {bool valid=false;int cell=-1;std::string reason="UNTESTED";};
struct TemporalCandidateRecord {double dt=0;bool stateValid=false,progress=false;int cell=-1;std::string stage,reason;};
struct TemporalSelection {double dt=0,connected=0;unsigned rejected=0,rescueEvaluations=0;bool rescue=false,budgetExhausted=false;std::string blocker;std::vector<TemporalCandidateRecord> tested;};
inline double temporalGrowthRequest(double freshCap,double lastAccepted){return lastAccepted>0?std::min(freshCap,2.0*lastAccepted):freshCap;}
template<class Evaluate,class Connected>
TemporalSelection selectTemporalCandidate(double time,double requested,double rescueUpper,Evaluate evaluate,Connected connected){
 TemporalSelection out;
 auto test=[&](double dt,const std::string& stage){TemporalCandidateCheck c;try{c=evaluate(dt);}catch(const std::exception& e){c.reason=e.what();}
  TemporalCandidateRecord r;r.dt=dt;r.stage=stage;r.stateValid=c.valid;r.cell=c.cell;r.reason=c.reason;r.progress=std::isfinite(time+dt)&&time+dt>time;
  if(r.stateValid&&!r.progress)r.reason="STATE_VALID_TIME_NONPROGRESS";
  if(!(r.stateValid&&r.progress)){++out.rejected;}out.tested.push_back(r);return r;};
 auto committable=[](const TemporalCandidateRecord&r){return r.stateValid&&r.progress;};
 auto first=test(requested,"request");if(committable(first)){out.dt=requested;return out;}
 double seed=first.stateValid&&!first.progress?requested:0;
 out.connected=connected();double retry=out.connected;
 for(unsigned j=0;j<16&&retry>0&&std::isfinite(retry);++j){
  retry=std::min(retry,rescueUpper);auto r=test(retry,j==0?"connected":"retry_half");
  if(committable(r)){out.dt=retry;return out;}
  if(r.stateValid&&!r.progress){seed=retry;break;}
  retry*=.5;
 }
 out.rescue=true;
 // nextafter supplies guidance only; it is never assigned to the clock.
 const double ulp=std::nextafter(time,std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity())-time;
 if(!(seed>0))seed=std::min(rescueUpper,ulp);
 if(!(seed>0&&rescueUpper>0&&std::isfinite(rescueUpper))){out.blocker="TEMPORAL_RESOLUTION_SEARCH_BLOCKER";return out;}
 double best=0;
 auto rescueTest=[&](double dt,const std::string& stage){++out.rescueEvaluations;auto r=test(dt,stage);if(committable(r))best=std::max(best,dt);return r;};
 // Search does not stop on FAIL: later binary64 legal pockets may exist.
 double dt=seed;
 if(first.stateValid&&!first.progress&&requested==seed){} // already evaluated seed
 else if(seed!=out.connected&&out.rescueEvaluations<64)rescueTest(seed,"rescue_guidance");
 while(dt<rescueUpper&&out.rescueEvaluations<64){
  double next=std::min(rescueUpper,dt*2.0);if(!(next>dt))next=rescueUpper;
  rescueTest(next,"rescue_ladder");dt=next;
 }
 if(dt<rescueUpper)out.budgetExhausted=true;
 // Small bounded neighborhood audit, never a maximal-dt search.
 if(best>0&&out.rescueEvaluations<64){double d=std::nextafter(best,0.);if(d>0)rescueTest(d,"rescue_neighbor_down");}
 if(best>0&&out.rescueEvaluations<64){double d=std::nextafter(best,std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity());if(d<=rescueUpper)rescueTest(d,"rescue_neighbor_up");}
 if(best>0){out.dt=best;return out;}
 out.blocker="TEMPORAL_RESOLUTION_SEARCH_BLOCKER";return out;
}
class TemporalPolicyJournal {
 std::string path_;std::ofstream steps_,trials_;
public:
 explicit TemporalPolicyJournal(const std::string& path):path_(path),steps_(path+"/td1_steps.csv"),trials_(path+"/td1_candidates.csv"){
  steps_<<std::setprecision(17)<<"anchor,tOld,incomingDt,physicalJoint,acoustic,endCap,freshCap,growthCap,requestedDt,requestStateValid,connectedDt,rescue,rescueEvaluations,selectedDt,selectedOverULP,tNew,rejectedCandidates,retryAttempts,stateRejectedCandidates,nonprogressCandidates,limitingCell,limitingReason,status\n";
  trials_<<std::setprecision(17)<<"anchor,index,stage,dt,stateValid,timeProgress,limitingCell,reason\n";
 }
 template<class Cells>
 void snapshot(const std::string& name,unsigned step,double time,double dt,const Cells& cells,const std::vector<double>& volume){
  auto bits=[](double d){std::uint64_t u;std::memcpy(&u,&d,8);std::ostringstream o;o<<std::hex<<std::setfill('0')<<std::setw(16)<<u;return o.str();};

 void record(unsigned anchor,double old,double incoming,double joint,double acoustic,double endCap,double fresh,double growth,double request,const TemporalSelection& p){
  double ulp=std::nextafter(old,std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity())-old;
  unsigned retries=0,stateRejected=0,nonprogress=0;std::string firstReason="NONE";int firstCell=-1;
  for(const auto&r:p.tested){if(r.stage=="connected"||r.stage=="retry_half")++retries;if(!r.stateValid)++stateRejected;if(r.stateValid&&!r.progress)++nonprogress;if(firstReason=="NONE"&&(!r.stateValid||!r.progress)){firstReason=r.reason;firstCell=r.cell;}}
  steps_<<anchor<<','<<old<<','<<incoming<<','<<joint<<','<<acoustic<<','<<endCap<<','<<fresh<<','<<growth<<','<<request<<','<<(!p.tested.empty()&&p.tested.front().stateValid)<<','<<p.connected<<','<<p.rescue<<','<<p.rescueEvaluations<<','<<p.dt<<','<<(p.dt>0?static_cast<long double>(p.dt)/static_cast<long double>(ulp):0.L)<<','<<(p.dt>0?old+p.dt:old)<<','<<p.rejected<<','<<retries<<','<<stateRejected<<','<<nonprogress<<','<<firstCell<<','<<(p.blocker.empty()?firstReason:p.blocker)<<','<<(p.dt>0?"SELECTED":"BLOCKED")<<'\n';steps_.flush();
  for(unsigned i=0;i<p.tested.size();++i){const auto&r=p.tested[i];trials_<<anchor<<','<<i<<','<<r.stage<<','<<r.dt<<','<<r.stateValid<<','<<r.progress<<','<<r.cell<<','<<r.reason<<'\n';}trials_.flush();
 }
};

#pragma once
#include <cmath>
#include <stdexcept>
#include "fiveEqScaledAuthority.H"
namespace fiveEq {
struct DD {double hi=0,lo=0;DD(double h=0,double l=0):hi(h),lo(l){}};
inline DD twoSum(double a,double b){double s=a+b,z=s-a;return {s,(a-(s-z))+(b-z)};}
inline DD ddAdd(DD a,DD b){auto s=twoSum(a.hi,b.hi),t=twoSum(a.lo,b.lo);auto u=twoSum(s.hi,s.lo+t.hi);return twoSum(u.hi,u.lo+t.lo);}
inline DD ddNeg(DD a){return {-a.hi,-a.lo};}
inline DD ddSub(DD a,DD b){return ddAdd(a,ddNeg(b));}
inline DD ddScale(DD a,double b){double p=a.hi*b,e=std::fma(a.hi,b,-p);return ddAdd({p,e},{a.lo*b,0});}
inline double ddValue(DD a){return a.hi+a.lo;}
enum class FractionMode {P0,P1,P2,P2Scaled};
struct SignedMinorityStorage {double coordinate=0;};
struct CompensatedStorage {double high=0,low=0;};
// Mode is harness metadata. Selected P2 payload is ONE signed double;
// -0 is pure liquid, +0 pure gas. No second independent physical fraction.
class PrecisionFraction {
    FractionMode mode_;DD data_;scaledV2::P2Scaled scaled_;
public:
    PrecisionFraction(FractionMode m=FractionMode::P0):mode_(m){}
    static PrecisionFraction minority(double x,bool gas,FractionMode mode){
        if(!(std::isfinite(x)&&x>=0&&x<=.5))throw std::invalid_argument("Invalid minority fraction");
        PrecisionFraction f(mode);if(mode==FractionMode::P2Scaled){f.scaled_=scaledV2::P2Scaled::fromLegacy(gas?-x:x);return f;}if(mode==FractionMode::P0)f.data_={gas?1-x:x,0};
        else if(mode==FractionMode::P1)f.data_=gas?twoSum(1,-x):DD(x,0);
        else {f.data_={gas?-x:x,0};}return f;
    }
    static PrecisionFraction liquid(double a,FractionMode mode){return a<=.5?minority(a,false,mode):minority(1-a,true,mode);}
    static PrecisionFraction fromScaled(scaledV2::P2Scaled s){int side=int(s.side);if(side<0||side>3||!s.q.canonical()||(side<2?!s.q.exactZero():(s.q.sign()<=0||s.q.compare(scaledV2::ScaledDD::fromDoubleExact(.5))>0)))throw std::invalid_argument("INVALID_P2_SCALED_AUTHORITY");PrecisionFraction f(FractionMode::P2Scaled);f.scaled_=s;return f;}
    const scaledV2::P2Scaled& scaledAuthority()const{if(mode_!=FractionMode::P2Scaled)throw std::invalid_argument("NOT_P2_SCALED_V2");return scaled_;}
    scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator liquidExact()const{if(mode_==FractionMode::P2Scaled)return scaled_.liquid();auto d=liquidDD();scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator n;n.add(d.hi);n.add(d.lo);return n;}
    scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator gasExact()const{auto n=scaledV2::power(0);n.add(liquidExact(),-1);return n;}
    PrecisionFraction advancedScaled(const scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator& d)const{if(mode_!=FractionMode::P2Scaled)throw std::invalid_argument("NOT_P2_SCALED_V2");return fromScaled(scaled_.advanced(d));}
    DD liquidDD()const {if(mode_==FractionMode::P2Scaled)throw std::invalid_argument("SCALED_AUTHORITY_INVALID_DD_CONSUMER");return mode_==FractionMode::P2&&std::signbit(data_.hi)?twoSum(1,data_.hi):data_;}
    DD gasDD()const {if(mode_==FractionMode::P2&&std::signbit(data_.hi))return {-data_.hi,0};return ddSub({1,0},liquidDD());}
    double liquidValue()const{return mode_==FractionMode::P2Scaled?scaledV2::rationalDoubleView(liquidExact(),scaledV2::power(0)).value:ddValue(liquidDD());}
    double gasValue()const{return mode_==FractionMode::P2Scaled?scaledV2::rationalDoubleView(gasExact(),scaledV2::power(0)).value:ddValue(gasDD());}
    FractionMode mode()const{return mode_;}
    double minorityCoordinateView()const{if(mode_!=FractionMode::P2Scaled)return data_.hi;bool g=scaled_.side==scaledV2::Side::GAS_MINORITY||scaled_.side==scaledV2::Side::PURE_LIQUID;double v=scaled_.q.toDoubleView().value;return std::copysign(v,g?-1.:1.);}
    bool liquidPresent()const {
        if(mode_==FractionMode::P2Scaled)return scaled_.liquidPresent();
        return mode_==FractionMode::P2?!(data_.hi==0&&!std::signbit(data_.hi)):liquidValue()!=0;
    }
    bool gasPresent()const {
        if(mode_==FractionMode::P2Scaled)return scaled_.gasPresent();
        return mode_==FractionMode::P2?!(std::signbit(data_.hi)&&data_.hi==0):gasValue()!=0;
    }
    DD payload()const{if(mode_==FractionMode::P2Scaled)throw std::invalid_argument("SCALED_AUTHORITY_INVALID_LEGACY_PAYLOAD_CONSUMER");return data_;}
    PrecisionFraction advanced(DD deltaLiquid)const {
        if(mode_==FractionMode::P2Scaled){scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator n;n.add(deltaLiquid.hi);n.add(deltaLiquid.lo);return advancedScaled(n);}
        auto r=*this;if(mode_==FractionMode::P0){r.data_={data_.hi+ddValue(deltaLiquid),0};}
        else if(mode_==FractionMode::P1)r.data_=ddAdd(data_,deltaLiquid);
        else {bool gas=std::signbit(data_.hi);double x=ddValue(ddAdd({std::abs(data_.hi),0},gas?ddNeg(deltaLiquid):deltaLiquid));
            if(!(x>=0&&x<=1&&std::isfinite(x)))throw std::invalid_argument("Fraction update outside range");
            if(x>.5){x=1-x;gas=!gas;}r.data_={gas?-x:x,0};}
        if(!(r.gasValue()>=0&&r.liquidValue()>=0&&r.gasValue()<=1&&r.liquidValue()<=1)){throw std::invalid_argument("Fraction update outside range");}return r;
    }
};
inline DD fractionDifference(const PrecisionFraction& a,const PrecisionFraction& b){return ddSub(a.liquidDD(),b.liquidDD());}

namespace fiveEq {
enum class MechanicalModel { HISTORICAL_K0, NONZERO_K_PE_v1, NONZERO_K_PE_CRV3_v1 };
inline const char* modelTag(MechanicalModel m) {
 if(m==MechanicalModel::NONZERO_K_PE_CRV3_v1)return "NONZERO_K_PE_CRV3_v1";
 return m==MechanicalModel::NONZERO_K_PE_v1?"NONZERO_K_PE_v1":"HISTORICAL_K0";
}
inline bool usesNonzeroK(MechanicalModel m){return m==MechanicalModel::NONZERO_K_PE_v1||m==MechanicalModel::NONZERO_K_PE_CRV3_v1;}
struct PEAcoustics {double K=0,c=0,bulk=0,responseL=0,responseG=0;};
inline PEAcoustics pressureEquilibriumAcoustics(const fiveEqRecoveredState& s) {
 PEAcoustics z;
 if(s.recoveryMode==RecoveryMode::PURE_GAS){z.c=s.cG;z.bulk=s.rhoG*s.cG*s.cG;z.responseG=1;return z;}
 if(s.recoveryMode==RecoveryMode::PURE_LIQUID){z.c=s.cL;z.bulk=s.rhoL*s.cL*s.cL;z.responseL=1;return z;}
 const double a=s.alphaL,b=s.alphaG,AL=s.rhoL*s.cL*s.cL,AG=s.rhoG*s.cG*s.cG;
 if(!(a>0&&b>0&&AL>0&&AG>0&&s.rhoMix>0&&std::isfinite(a)&&std::isfinite(b)&&std::isfinite(s.rhoMix)&&std::isfinite(AL)&&std::isfinite(AG)))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_PE_MODULI");
 const double S=std::max(AL,AG),nL=a*(AG/S),nG=b*(AL/S),D=nG+nL;
 if(!(std::isfinite(S)&&std::isfinite(D)&&D>0))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_PE_RESPONSE_DENOMINATOR");
 z.responseL=nL/D;z.responseG=nG/D;
 z.K=(a*b)*(((AG-AL)/S)/D);
 z.bulk=AL<=AG?AL/(a+b*(AL/AG)):AG/(b+a*(AG/AL));
 z.c=std::sqrt(z.bulk/s.rhoMix);
 // Mathematical K bounds are certified by stable response factors. Rounded K
 // remains the frozen source coefficient, including outward rounding within
 // the separately qualified MC3 algebra budget; never clip it.
 if(!(std::isfinite(z.K)&&std::isfinite(z.c)&&z.c>0&&std::isfinite(z.responseL)&&std::isfinite(z.responseG)
      &&z.responseL>=0&&z.responseG>=0&&z.responseL<=1&&z.responseG<=1
      &&std::abs(z.responseL+z.responseG-1)<=5e-14+3e-20))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_PE_ACOUSTICS");
 return z;
}
struct ThreeLegPath {double uStar=0,IK=0,IaK=0,leftK=0,rightK=0,leftAK=0,rightAK=0;};
inline ThreeLegPath threeLegPath(const fiveEqRecoveredState& L,const fiveEqRecoveredState& R,double uStar) {
 ThreeLegPath z;z.uStar=uStar;const auto aL=pressureEquilibriumAcoustics(L),aR=pressureEquilibriumAcoustics(R);const double KL=aL.K,KR=aR.K;
 z.leftK=KL*(uStar-L.U[0]);z.rightK=KR*(R.U[0]-uStar);
 z.leftAK=aL.responseL*(uStar-L.U[0]);z.rightAK=aR.responseL*(R.U[0]-uStar);
 z.IK=z.leftK+z.rightK;z.IaK=z.leftAK+z.rightAK;return z;
}
}

static DD fractionNumerator(const PrecisionCell& c,const PrecisionFace& L,const PrecisionFace& R){
 auto n=ddAdd(ddScale(fractionDifference(L.left,c.fraction),L.flux.wave.Aplus),ddScale(fractionDifference(L.right,c.fraction),L.flux.wave.Aminus));
 n=ddSub(n,ddScale(fractionDifference(R.left,c.fraction),R.flux.wave.Aplus));return ddSub(n,ddScale(fractionDifference(R.right,c.fraction),R.flux.wave.Aminus));
}
PrecisionCell PrecisionOperator::trial(const PrecisionCell& c,double V,double dt,const PrecisionFace& L,const PrecisionFace& R,double ml,double mr)const {
 if(c.representationV2){using namespace scaledV2;if(ml!=0||mr!=0||!L.representationV2||!R.representationV2)throw RecoveryError("SCALED_STRATEGY0_FIXED_MESH_ONLY");if(dt==0)return c;auto t=c;SparseExponentAccumulator factor;factor.add(dt/V);
  for(unsigned j:{3u,4u,5u,6u}){double net=j==6?R.flux.energyPhi-L.flux.energyPhi:R.flux.momentumPhi[j-3]-L.flux.momentumPhi[j-3];t.q[j]=std::fma(-dt/V,net,c.q[j]);}
  for(unsigned k=0;k<4;++k){auto n=L.authorityFlux[k].exact();n.add(R.authorityFlux[k].exact(),-1);t.authority[k]=c.authority[k].updated(n.multiplied(factor));}
  t.fraction=c.fraction.advancedScaled(totalFractionNumeratorScaled(c,L,R).multiplied(factor));t.refreshViews();return t;
 }
 if(dt==0){return c;}auto q=explicitTrial(c.q,V,dt,L.flux,R.flux,ml,mr);PrecisionCell t=c;t.q=q.q;
 auto n=totalFractionNumerator(c,L,R);t.fraction=c.fraction.advanced(ddScale(n,dt/q.volume));t.q[0]=t.fraction.liquidValue();return t;
}
DD PrecisionOperator::totalFractionNumerator(const PrecisionCell& c,const PrecisionFace& L,const PrecisionFace& R)const {
 if(c.representationV2)throw RecoveryError("SCALED_AUTHORITY_INVALID_DD_NUMERATOR_CONSUMER");
 auto n=fractionNumerator(c,L,R);
 if(usesNonzeroK(model())){const double K=pressureEquilibriumAcoustics(recover(c)).K;
  n=ddAdd(n,ddScale(ddSub({R.flux.wave.phiPhysStar,0},{L.flux.wave.phiPhysStar,0}),K));}
 return n;
}
scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator PrecisionOperator::totalFractionNumeratorScaled(const PrecisionCell& c,const PrecisionFace& L,const PrecisionFace& R)const {
 using namespace scaledV2;if(!c.representationV2)throw RecoveryError("NOT_P2_SCALED_V2");SparseExponentAccumulator n;

 add(L.left,L.flux.wave.Aplus,1);add(L.right,L.flux.wave.Aminus,1);add(R.left,R.flux.wave.Aplus,-1);add(R.right,R.flux.wave.Aminus,-1);

}
double PrecisionOperator::jointBound(const PrecisionCell& c,double V,const PrecisionFace& L,const PrecisionFace& R,double ml,double mr)const {
 if(c.representationV2){using namespace scaledV2;if(ml!=0||mr!=0)throw RecoveryError("SCALED_STRATEGY0_FIXED_MESH_ONLY");auto n=totalFractionNumeratorScaled(c,L,R);SparseExponentAccumulator vol;vol.add(V);double cap=acousticBound(c,V,L,R);
  auto bound=[&](const SparseExponentAccumulator& amount,const SparseExponentAccumulator& outgoing){if(outgoing.sign()<=0)return;double d=rationalDoubleView(amount.multiplied(vol),outgoing).value;cap=std::min(cap,.95*d);};
  bound(c.fraction.liquidExact(),n.negated());bound(c.fraction.gasExact(),n);
  for(unsigned k=0;k<4;++k){unsigned j=k<2?k+1:k+5;auto out=R.authorityFlux[k].exact();out.add(L.authorityFlux[k].exact(),-1);bound(c.mass(j),out);}
  auto out=R.authorityFlux[0].exact();out.add(L.authorityFlux[0].exact(),-1);for(unsigned k=2;k<4;++k){out.add(R.authorityFlux[k].exact(),-1);out.add(L.authorityFlux[k].exact());}bound(c.mass(9),out);return cap;

 auto bound=[](double x,double out){return positivePhaseTimeRatio(x,out);};
 double a=std::min(bound(c.fraction.liquidValue()*V,-c.fraction.liquidValue()*D-n),bound(c.fraction.gasValue()*V,n-c.fraction.gasValue()*D));
 return std::min({b.acoustic,.95*a,.95*b.mG,.95*b.mL,.95*b.species,.95*b.volume});
}
double PrecisionOperator::connectedDt(const PrecisionCell& c,double V,double dt,const PrecisionFace& L,const PrecisionFace& R,double ml,double mr)const {
 if(!(dt>0)){return 0;}auto valid=[&](double x){try{recover(trial(c,V,x*dt,L,R,ml,mr));return true;}catch(const std::exception&){return false;}};
 if(!valid(0)){return 0;}double lo=0,hi=1;bool exit=false;for(unsigned j=1;j<=32;++j){double x=double(j)/32;if(!valid(x)){hi=x;exit=true;break;}lo=x;}
 if(exit){for(unsigned j=0;j<52;++j){double x=lo+(hi-lo)/2;if(x==lo||x==hi)break;if(valid(x))lo=x;else hi=x;}}return lo*dt;
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqPrecisionRecovery.H"

namespace fiveEq {
// When representationV2 is true, q[0,1,2,7,8,9] are certified/output views.
// The tagged slots below are the only phase/species conservative authorities.
struct PrecisionCell {
 MaterialCell q;PrecisionFraction fraction;bool representationV2=false;
 std::array<scaledV2::AuthorityScalar,4> authority; // mG,mL,mY0,mY1; mY2 residual
 scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator mass(unsigned j)const{if(representationV2){if(j==1||j==2)return authority[j-1].exact();if(j==7||j==8)return authority[j-5].exact();if(j==9){auto r=authority[0].exact();r.add(authority[2].exact(),-1);r.add(authority[3].exact(),-1);return r;}}scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator n;n.add(q[j]);return n;}
 double rhoView()const{if(!representationV2)return q[1]+q[2];auto n=mass(1);n.add(mass(2));return scaledV2::rationalDoubleView(n,scaledV2::power(0)).value;}
 void refreshViews(){if(!representationV2)return;q[0]=fraction.liquidValue();for(unsigned k=0;k<4;++k)q[k<2?k+1:k+5]=authority[k].view().value;q[9]=scaledV2::rationalDoubleView(mass(9),scaledV2::power(0)).value;}
 void migrateV2(){if(representationV2)return;if(fraction.mode()!=FractionMode::P2)throw RecoveryError("V2_MIGRATION_REQUIRES_LEGACY_P2");fraction=PrecisionFraction::fromScaled(scaledV2::P2Scaled::fromLegacy(fraction.payload().hi));for(unsigned j=0;j<4;++j)authority[j]=scaledV2::AuthorityScalar(q[j<2?j+1:j+5]);representationV2=true;refreshViews();}
};
struct PrecisionFace {fiveEqKTFaceFlux flux;PrecisionFraction left,right;ThreeLegPath path;bool representationV2=false;std::array<scaledV2::ScaledDD,4> authorityFlux;};
class PrecisionOperator {
 const fiveEqPrecisionRecovery& recovery_;std::array<unsigned,3> canonical_;
public:
 PrecisionOperator(const fiveEqPrecisionRecovery& r,std::array<unsigned,3> canonical):recovery_(r),canonical_(canonical){}
 MechanicalModel model()const{return recovery_.model();}
 unsigned reconstructionStrategy()const{return usesNonzeroK(model())?0:1;}
 DD totalFractionNumerator(const PrecisionCell&,const PrecisionFace&,const PrecisionFace&)const;
 scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator totalFractionNumeratorScaled(const PrecisionCell&,const PrecisionFace&,const PrecisionFace&)const;
 fiveEqRecoveredState recover(const PrecisionCell&)const;
 PrecisionFace face(const PrecisionCell&,const PrecisionCell&,double meshPhi=0,KTScheme scheme=KTScheme::Kurganov)const;

 double jointBound(const PrecisionCell&,double V,const PrecisionFace&,const PrecisionFace&,double meshL=0,double meshR=0)const;
 double acousticBound(const PrecisionCell& c,double V,const PrecisionFace& L,const PrecisionFace& R)const{if(!c.representationV2)return explicitTimeStepBounds(c.q,V,L.flux,R.flux,0,0).acoustic;double wl=std::max(std::abs(L.flux.wave.ap),std::abs(L.flux.wave.am)),wr=std::max(std::abs(R.flux.wave.ap),std::abs(R.flux.wave.am));return .15*2*V/(wl+wr);}
 double connectedDt(const PrecisionCell&,double V,double candidate,const PrecisionFace&,const PrecisionFace&,double meshL=0,double meshR=0)const;
};
}

#include "fiveEqPrecisionRecovery.H"
#include "fiveEqScaledRatio.H"
#include "fiveEqScaledAcoustics.H"

#include <cmath>
namespace fiveEq {
fiveEqConservativeState fiveEqPrecisionRecovery::generate(double a,double p,double TG,double TL,const std::array<double,3>& U,const std::vector<double>& Y) const {
    if(!std::isfinite(a)||a<0||a>1)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GENERATOR_ALPHA");
    fiveEqConservativeState q;q.alphaL=a;q.YG=Y;double internal=0;
    if(a<1){auto g=gas_.evaluatePT(p,TG,Y);q.mG=(1-a)*g.rho;internal+=q.mG*g.e;}
    if(a>0){auto l=liquid_.evaluatePT(p,TL);q.mL=a*l.rho;internal+=q.mL*l.e;}
    const double r=q.mG+q.mL;double v2=0;
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){if(!std::isfinite(U[j]))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GENERATOR_U");q.M[j]=r*U[j];v2+=U[j]*U[j];}
    q.Et=internal+0.5*r*v2;return q;
}
fiveEqRecoveredState fiveEqPrecisionRecovery::recover(const fiveEqConservativeState& q,const PrecisionFraction& fraction,const RecoveryOptions& o,fiveEqRecoveredState* progress) const {return recoverWithAuthority(q,fraction,o,progress,nullptr);}
fiveEqRecoveredState fiveEqPrecisionRecovery::recoverWithAuthority(const fiveEqConservativeState& q,const PrecisionFraction& fraction,const RecoveryOptions& o,fiveEqRecoveredState* progress,const RecoveryAuthority* authority) const {
    if(!std::isfinite(q.alphaL)||q.alphaL<0||q.alphaL>1||!std::isfinite(q.mG)||!std::isfinite(q.mL)||q.mG<0||q.mL<0||!std::isfinite(q.Et))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_CONSERVATIVE_STATE");
    fiveEqRecoveredState s;s.alphaL=fraction.liquidValue();s.alphaG=fraction.gasValue();s.mG=q.mG;s.mL=q.mL;s.rhoMix=q.mG+q.mL;
    scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator totalMass;
    if(authority){totalMass=authority->gasMass;totalMass.add(authority->liquidMass);s.rhoMix=scaledV2::rationalDoubleView(totalMass,scaledV2::power(0)).value;}
    auto intrinsic=[&](bool gas){if(authority)return scaledV2::rationalDoubleView(gas?authority->gasMass:authority->liquidMass,gas?fraction.gasExact():fraction.liquidExact()).value;return phaseIntrinsicRatio(gas?q.mG:q.mL,gas?s.alphaG:s.alphaL);};
    auto weighted=[&](double g,double l,double subtract=0){if(!authority)return q.mG*g+q.mL*l-subtract;scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator G,L;G.add(g);L.add(l);auto n=authority->gasMass.multiplied(G);n.add(authority->liquidMass.multiplied(L));n.add(-subtract);return scaledV2::rationalDoubleView(n,scaledV2::power(0)).value;};
    // Query-local snapshot on success or exception; does not alter root mathematics.
    struct Snapshot { fiveEqRecoveredState* dst; fiveEqRecoveredState& src;
        ~Snapshot(){if(dst)*dst=src;}
    } snapshot={progress,s};
    if(!(s.rhoMix>0&&std::isfinite(s.rhoMix)))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_TOTAL_DENSITY");
    if(!(o.pressureAbsTol>0&&o.pressureRelTol>0&&o.energyRelativeTol>0&&o.energyScalePerMass>0&&o.maxIterations>0))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_RECOVERY_OPTIONS");
    double kin=0;for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){if(!std::isfinite(q.M[j]))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_MOMENTUM");s.U[j]=q.M[j]/s.rhoMix;kin+=0.5*q.M[j]*s.U[j];}
    const double internal=q.Et-kin,scale=std::max(std::abs(internal),s.rhoMix*o.energyScalePerMass);
    if(!std::isfinite(internal)||!std::isfinite(scale))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_INTERNAL_ENERGY");
    // Presence BEFORE intrinsic-density divisions. Never discard trace mass.
    if(!(presence_.alphaPresenceTol>=0&&presence_.alphaPresenceTol<1&&presence_.massPresenceRelativeTol>=0&&presence_.massPresenceAbsoluteTol>=0))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_PHASE_PRESENCE_POLICY");
    // Contract v2: magnitude is diagnostic only; no phase deletion.
    auto presence=[&](bool gas,bool authoritativePresent,double alpha,double m,PhasePresence& state){
        state=PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT;
        if(authority){const auto& mass=gas?authority->gasMass:authority->liquidMass;if(!authoritativePresent){if(!mass.exactZero())throw RecoveryError("ABSENT_PHASE_HAS_MASS");state=PhasePresence::ABSENT;return false;}if(mass.sign()<=0)throw RecoveryError("PRESENT_PHASE_HAS_ZERO_OR_NEGATIVE_MASS");double rho=intrinsic(gas);if(!(std::isfinite(rho)&&rho>0))throw RecoveryError("PRESENT_PHASE_INVALID_INTRINSIC_DENSITY");state=alpha<=presence_.alphaPresenceTol?PhasePresence::TRACE_PRESENT:PhasePresence::PRESENT;return true;}
        if(!authoritativePresent){if(m!=0)throw RecoveryError("ABSENT_PHASE_HAS_MASS");state=PhasePresence::ABSENT;return false;}
        if(m==0)throw RecoveryError("PRESENT_PHASE_HAS_ZERO_MASS");
        const double rho=phaseIntrinsicRatio(m,alpha);
        if(!(std::isfinite(rho)&&rho>0))throw RecoveryError("PRESENT_PHASE_INVALID_INTRINSIC_DENSITY");
        state=alpha<=presence_.alphaPresenceTol?PhasePresence::TRACE_PRESENT:PhasePresence::PRESENT;
        return true;
    };
    bool pg=presence(true,fraction.gasPresent(),s.alphaG,q.mG,s.gasPresence),pl=presence(false,fraction.liquidPresent(),s.alphaL,q.mL,s.liquidPresence);
    auto putGas=[&](const GasState& g){s.p=g.p;s.rhoG=g.rho;s.TG=g.T;s.eG=g.e;s.cG=g.c;s.betaG=g.beta;s.gasValidity="VALID_JANAF";};
    auto putLiquid=[&](const liquidEOS::LiquidEOSState& l){s.p=l.p;s.rhoL=l.rho;s.TL=l.T;s.eL=l.e;s.cL=l.c;s.betaL=l.beta;s.liquidValidity=l.validity==liquidEOS::EOSValidity::NORMAL?"NORMAL":"BUFFER";};
    if(pg&&!pl){
        s.recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::PURE_GAS;++s.calls.gas;
        auto g=gas_.recoverRhoE(q.mG,internal/q.mG,q.YG);putGas(g);s.rhoG=q.mG;s.iterations=g.iterations;
        auto bracket=gas_.pressureInterval(q.mG,q.YG);s.bracketMin=bracket[0];s.bracketMax=bracket[1];
    }else if(pl&&!pg){
        s.recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::PURE_LIQUID;++s.calls.liquid;
        auto l=liquid_.recoverRhoE(q.mL,internal/q.mL);putLiquid(l);s.rhoL=q.mL;s.iterations=l.pressureIterations;
        // Pure liquid adapter performs its own strict bracket search.
        s.diagnosticFlags="PURE_LIQUID_FROZEN_EOS_BRACKET";
    }else{
        s.recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::MIXED;s.rhoG=intrinsic(true);s.rhoL=intrinsic(false);
        const double rg=s.rhoG,rl=s.rhoL;
        ++s.calls.gas;
        std::array<double,2> bg,bl;
        try{bg=gas_.pressureInterval(rg,q.YG);}catch(...){s.gasPresence=PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT;throw;}
        try{bl=liquid_.pressureInterval(rl,&s.calls.liquid);}catch(...){s.liquidPresence=PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT;throw;}
        double lo=std::max(bg[0],bl[0]),hi=std::min(bg[1],bl[1]);
        s.bracketMin=lo;s.bracketMax=hi;
        if(lo>hi)throw RecoveryError("NO_COMMON_VALID_PRESSURE_INTERVAL");
        // User-authorized exception: exact legal singleton, never a fuzzy interval.
        if(lo==hi)s.diagnosticFlags="EXACT_LEGAL_SINGLETON";
        auto residual=[&](double p){
            ++s.calls.residual;++s.calls.gas;++s.calls.liquid;
            const auto g=gas_.evaluateRhoP(rg,p,q.YG);const auto l=liquid_.evaluateRhoP(rl,p);
            putGas(g);putLiquid(l);s.rhoG=rg;s.rhoL=rl;
            const double f=weighted(g.e,l.e,internal),df=weighted(g.beta,l.beta);
            s.energyResidual=f;s.normalizedEnergyResidual=std::abs(f)/scale;
            if(!std::isfinite(f)||!std::isfinite(df)||df<=0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_THERMODYNAMIC_ROOT_DERIVATIVE");
            return std::array<double,2>{{f,df}};
        };
        auto left=residual(lo),right=residual(hi);const double etol=o.energyRelativeTol*scale;
        bool done=false;double p=(lo+hi)/2,last=hi-lo;
        if(std::abs(left[0])<=etol){p=lo;done=true;}
        else if(std::abs(right[0])<=etol){p=hi;done=true;}
        else if(!(left[0]<0&&right[0]>0))throw RecoveryError("NO_PHYSICAL_ENERGY_ROOT_IN_VALID_DOMAIN");
        if(!done){
            if(o.initialGuess==InitialGuess::PROVIDED&&std::isfinite(o.knownPressure)&&o.knownPressure>lo&&o.knownPressure<hi)p=o.knownPressure;
            if(o.initialGuess==InitialGuess::ENERGY_SECANT){
                // Energy-weighted endpoint estimate, not a gas/liquid pressure average.
                p=lo+(hi-lo)*(-left[0])/(right[0]-left[0]);
                if(!(p>lo&&p<hi))p=(lo+hi)/2;
            }
            for(unsigned n=1;n<=o.maxIterations;++n){
                std::array<double,2> f;
                try{f=residual(p);}catch(const liquidEOS::EOSQueryError& e){
                    if(std::string(e.what()).find("INVALID")==std::string::npos)throw;
                    p=(lo+hi)/2;++s.calls.fallback;f=residual(p);
                }catch(const RecoveryError& e){
                    if(std::string(e.what())!="INVALID_GAS_PT")throw;
                    p=(lo+hi)/2;++s.calls.fallback;f=residual(p);
                }
                s.iterations=n;double correction=f[0]/f[1];
                const double ptol=o.pressureAbsTol+o.pressureRelTol*std::abs(p);
                // Both energy and the implied Newton pressure increment must pass.
                if(std::abs(f[0])<=etol&&(std::abs(last)<=ptol||std::abs(correction)<=ptol)){done=true;s.pressureIncrement=std::abs(correction);break;}
                if(f[0]<0)lo=p;else hi=p;
                double next=p-correction;
                // Also safeguard excessive steps to exercise/bracket contraction.
                if(!(next>lo&&next<hi)||!std::isfinite(next)||std::abs(next-p)>.75*(hi-lo)){next=(lo+hi)/2;++s.calls.fallback;}
                last=next-p;p=next;
            }
        }
        if(!done)throw RecoveryError("PRESSURE_ROOT_NOT_CONVERGED_OR_JANAF_GAP");
        residual(p); // fresh state, never reuse stale endpoint/iterate properties.
    }
    s.cMix=authority?scaledAcoustics(s,fraction,totalMass).c:(usesNonzeroK(model_)?pressureEquilibriumAcoustics(s).c:allaireSoundSpeed(s));
    const double reconstructed=q.mG*s.eG+q.mL*s.eL+kin;
    s.energyResidual=authority?weighted(s.eG,s.eL,q.Et-kin):reconstructed-q.Et;s.normalizedEnergyResidual=std::abs(s.energyResidual)/scale;
    if(!(std::isfinite(s.p)&&s.p>0&&std::isfinite(s.cMix)&&s.cMix>0&&s.normalizedEnergyResidual<=4*o.energyRelativeTol))throw RecoveryError("RECOVERED_STATE_VALIDATION_FAILED");
    return s;
}
void fiveEqPrecisionRecovery::guardPath(const fiveEqConservativeState& qL,const fiveEqRecoveredState& L,const PrecisionFraction& fL,const fiveEqConservativeState& qR,const fiveEqRecoveredState& R,const PrecisionFraction& fR) const {
    const bool pg=fL.gasPresent()||fR.gasPresent(),pl=fL.liquidPresent()||fR.liquidPresent();
    const double TGL=fL.gasPresent()?L.TG:R.TG,TGR=fR.gasPresent()?R.TG:L.TG;
    const double TLL=fL.liquidPresent()?L.TL:R.TL,TLR=fR.liquidPresent()?R.TL:L.TL;
    const auto& YL=fL.gasPresent()?qL.YG:qR.YG;const auto& YR=fR.gasPresent()?qR.YG:qL.YG;
    if(pg){const auto ml=gas_.mixture(YL),mr=gas_.mixture(YR);
        if((TGL<ml.Tcommon())!=(TGR<mr.Tcommon()))throw RecoveryError("PATH_THERMO_BRANCH_OR_DOMAIN_BLOCKER");
        for(unsigned k=0;k<YL.size();++k)if(YL[k]>0||YR[k]>0){const double split=gas_.speciesData()[k].Tcommon();
            if((TGL<split)!=(TGR<split))throw RecoveryError("PATH_THERMO_BRANCH_OR_DOMAIN_BLOCKER");}}
    // Convex p/T/composition path, with inherited missing coordinates only here.
    // Native evaluations certify queried path nodes; no endpoint state is reset.
    for(unsigned i=0;i<=16;++i){const double t=double(i)/16,p=(1-t)*L.p+t*R.p;
        auto f=fL;
        if(i==16)f=fR;else if(fL.mode()==FractionMode::P2Scaled){auto d=fR.liquidExact();d.add(fL.liquidExact(),-1);scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator T;T.add(t);f=fL.advancedScaled(d.multiplied(T));}else f=fL.advanced(ddScale(fractionDifference(fR,fL),t));
        if(pg&&f.gasPresent()){std::vector<double> Y(YL.size());for(unsigned k=0;k<Y.size();++k)Y[k]=(1-t)*YL[k]+t*YR[k];gas_.evaluatePT(p,(1-t)*TGL+t*TGR,Y);}
        if(pl&&f.liquidPresent())liquid_.evaluatePT(p,(1-t)*TLL+t*TLR);
    }
}
double fiveEqPrecisionRecovery::dpDalpha(const fiveEqRecoveredState& s) const {
    if(s.recoveryMode!=RecoveryMode::MIXED)throw RecoveryError("ALPHA_DERIVATIVE_REQUIRES_TWO_PHASES");
    const double egRho=-s.betaG*s.p/s.rhoG;
    const double elRho=-s.betaL*(s.cL*s.cL-s.p/(s.rhoL*s.rhoL*s.betaL));
    const double ra=s.mG*egRho*s.rhoG/s.alphaG-s.mL*elRho*s.rhoL/s.alphaL;
    return -ra/(s.mG*s.betaG+s.mL*s.betaL);
}
}

#pragma once
#include "../fiveEqThermo/fiveEqStateRecovery.H"
#include "fiveEqFractionPrecision.H"
#include "fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator.H"
#include "fiveEqAcoustics.H"
#include "fiveEqNonzeroKModel.H"
namespace fiveEq {
struct RecoveryAuthority {scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator gasMass,liquidMass;};
class fiveEqPrecisionRecovery {
    const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator& gas_;
    const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& liquid_;
    const phasePresencePolicy presence_;
    const MechanicalModel model_;
public:
    fiveEqPrecisionRecovery(const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator& g,const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& l,phasePresencePolicy p={},MechanicalModel m=MechanicalModel::HISTORICAL_K0):gas_(g),liquid_(l),presence_(p),model_(m){}
    MechanicalModel model() const {return model_;}
    void guardPath(const fiveEqConservativeState&,const fiveEqRecoveredState&,const PrecisionFraction&,const fiveEqConservativeState&,const fiveEqRecoveredState&,const PrecisionFraction&) const;
    fiveEqRecoveredState recover(const fiveEqConservativeState&,const PrecisionFraction&,const RecoveryOptions& options={},fiveEqRecoveredState* progress=nullptr) const;
    fiveEqRecoveredState recoverWithAuthority(const fiveEqConservativeState&,const PrecisionFraction&,const RecoveryOptions&,fiveEqRecoveredState*,const RecoveryAuthority*) const;
    fiveEqRecoveredState tryRecover(const fiveEqConservativeState& q,const PrecisionFraction& f,const RecoveryOptions& options={}) const {
        try{return recover(q,f,options);}catch(const std::exception& e){
            fiveEqRecoveredState invalid;invalid.recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::INVALID;
            invalid.diagnosticFlags=e.what();return invalid;
        }
    }

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqScaledAuthority.H"
#include "fiveEqNonzeroKModel.H"
namespace fiveEq {
struct ScaledAcousticResponse {scaledV2::ScaledDD K;double c;ScaledAcousticResponse(scaledV2::ScaledDD k={},double v=0):K(k),c(v){}};
inline ScaledAcousticResponse scaledAcoustics(const fiveEqRecoveredState& s,const PrecisionFraction& f,const scaledV2::SparseExponentAccumulator& rho) {
 using namespace scaledV2;
 if(s.recoveryMode!=RecoveryMode::MIXED)return {ScaledDD{},pressureEquilibriumAcoustics(s).c};
 SparseExponentAccumulator AL,AG;AL.add(s.rhoL*s.cL*s.cL);AG.add(s.rhoG*s.cG*s.cG);
 auto D=f.gasExact().multiplied(AL);D.add(f.liquidExact().multiplied(AG));
 auto delta=AG;delta.add(AL,-1);auto K=ScaledDD::fromRatio(f.liquidExact().multiplied(f.gasExact()).multiplied(delta),D);
 auto c2=rationalDoubleView(AL.multiplied(AG),D.multiplied(rho));
 if(!(std::isfinite(c2.value)&&c2.value>0))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_SCALED_ACOUSTICS");
 return {K,std::sqrt(c2.value)};
}
}

inline std::int64_t checkedAdd(std::int64_t a,std::int64_t b) {
 if((b>0&&a>INT64_MAX-b)||(b<0&&a<INT64_MIN-b))throw std::range_error("REPRESENTATION_RANGE_BLOCKER");return a+b;
}
inline std::int64_t checkedNeg(std::int64_t a) {if(a==INT64_MIN)throw std::range_error("REPRESENTATION_RANGE_BLOCKER");return -a;}
inline std::uint64_t bits(double x){std::uint64_t u;std::memcpy(&u,&x,8);return u;}

struct Dyadic {Integer n;std::int64_t e;};
inline Dyadic decode(double x) {
 auto u=bits(x);unsigned p=(u>>52)&2047;std::uint64_t f=u&UINT64_C(0xfffffffffffff);
 if(p==2047)throw std::invalid_argument("NONFINITE_SCALED_INPUT");

inline unsigned bitLength(const Integer& n){return n==0?0:boost::multiprecision::msb(n<0?-n:n)+1;}
// Finite exact sparse dyadic polynomial. Widely separated exponents are NEVER
// aligned into a dense integer. Only overlapping leading clusters are merged.
class SparseExponentAccumulator {
 std::map<std::int64_t,Integer> terms_;
 static Dyadic canonical(Dyadic t){if(t.n==0)return {0,0};Integer a=t.n<0?-t.n:t.n;unsigned z=boost::multiprecision::lsb(a);t.n>>=z;t.e=checkedAdd(t.e,z);return t;}
 static std::int64_t top(const Dyadic& t){return checkedAdd(t.e,bitLength(t.n)-1);}
 std::vector<Dyadic> reduced()const {
  std::vector<Dyadic> v;for(const auto& t:terms_)if(t.second!=0)v.push_back(canonical({t.second,t.first}));
  while(v.size()>1){
   std::sort(v.begin(),v.end(),[](const Dyadic& a,const Dyadic& b){return top(a)>top(b);});
   unsigned log=0;for(std::size_t n=v.size()-1;n;n>>=1)++log;
   auto lowerTop=top(v[1]);if(lowerTop<=INT64_MAX-int(log)-1&&top(v[0])>lowerTop+log+1)break;
   auto a=v[0],b=v[1];auto e=std::min(a.e,b.e);
   // Overlap guarantees these shifts are bounded by stored significand sizes.
   Integer n=(a.n<<static_cast<std::uint64_t>(a.e-e))+(b.n<<static_cast<std::uint64_t>(b.e-e));
   v.erase(v.begin(),v.begin()+2);if(n!=0)v.push_back(canonical({n,e}));
  }return v;
 }
public:
 bool compact(Dyadic& out)const {if(terms_.empty()){out={0,0};return true;}auto e=terms_.begin()->first;auto last=terms_.rbegin()->first;if(e<=INT64_MAX-32768&&last>e+32768)return false;Integer n=0;for(const auto& t:terms_)n+=t.second<<static_cast<unsigned>(t.first-e);out={n,e};return true;}
 void add(Dyadic t){if(t.n==0)return;t=canonical(t);auto& n=terms_[t.e];n+=t.n;if(n==0)terms_.erase(t.e);}
 void add(double x,std::int64_t shift=0){auto t=decode(x);if(t.n!=0)t.e=checkedAdd(t.e,shift);add(t);}
 void add(const SparseExponentAccumulator& x,int sign=1){for(const auto& t:x.terms_)add({sign*t.second,t.first});}
 int sign()const {Dyadic d;if(compact(d))return d.n==0?0:d.n>0?1:-1;auto v=reduced();return v.empty()?0:(v[0].n>0?1:-1);}
 bool exactZero()const{return sign()==0;}
 std::int64_t leadingExponent()const {Dyadic d;if(compact(d))return d.n==0?0:top(d);auto v=reduced();if(v.empty())return 0;return top(v[0]);}
 SparseExponentAccumulator negated()const{SparseExponentAccumulator r;r.add(*this,-1);return r;}
 SparseExponentAccumulator shifted(std::int64_t e)const{SparseExponentAccumulator r;for(const auto& t:terms_)r.add({t.second,checkedAdd(t.first,e)});return r;}
 SparseExponentAccumulator multiplied(const SparseExponentAccumulator& b)const {
  SparseExponentAccumulator r;for(const auto& a:terms_)for(const auto& t:b.terms_)r.add({a.second*t.second,checkedAdd(a.first,t.first)});return r;
 }
 int compare(const SparseExponentAccumulator& b)const {auto r=*this;r.add(b,-1);return r.sign();}
 std::size_t bins()const{return terms_.size();}
};
inline SparseExponentAccumulator power(std::int64_t e,Integer n=1){SparseExponentAccumulator r;r.add({n,e});return r;}
// Exact rational comparisons certify rounding, including halfway cases. No
// floating primitive touches a raw tiny value, even when DAZ/FTZ are enabled.
struct RoundedInteger {Integer n;std::int64_t e;};
inline RoundedInteger roundRatio(const SparseExponentAccumulator& numerator,const SparseExponentAccumulator& denominator,unsigned precision=53) {
 int sn=numerator.sign(),sd=denominator.sign();if(sd<=0)throw std::invalid_argument("NONPOSITIVE_SCALED_DIVISOR");if(!sn)return {0,0};
 auto N=sn<0?numerator.negated():numerator;
 Dyadic nd,dd;if(N.compact(nd)&&denominator.compact(dd)){
  int k=int(bitLength(nd.n))-int(bitLength(dd.n));if(k>=0){if(nd.n<(dd.n<<k))--k;}else if((nd.n<<(-k))<dd.n)--k;
  int shift=int(precision)-1-k;Integer a=nd.n,b=dd.n;if(shift>=0)a<<=shift;else b<<=-shift;
  Integer q=a/b,rem=a%b;int c=(2*rem>b)?1:(2*rem==b)?0:-1;if(c>0||(c==0&&static_cast<bool>(q&1)))++q;
  auto e=checkedAdd(checkedAdd(nd.e,checkedNeg(dd.e)),k+1-int(precision));return {sn<0?-q:q,e};
 }
 auto cmp=[&](Integer n,std::int64_t e){auto r=N;r.add(denominator.multiplied(power(e,n)),-1);return r.sign();};
 auto k=checkedAdd(N.leadingExponent(),checkedNeg(denominator.leadingExponent()));
 while(cmp(1,k)<0)k=checkedAdd(k,-1);
 while(cmp(1,checkedAdd(k,1))>=0)k=checkedAdd(k,1);
 auto e=checkedAdd(k,1-int(precision));Integer lo=Integer(1)<<(precision-1),hi=Integer(1)<<precision;
 while(hi-lo>1){Integer mid=(hi+lo)>>1;if(cmp(mid,e)>=0)lo=mid;else hi=mid;}
 int c=cmp(2*lo+1,checkedAdd(e,-1));if(c>0||(c==0&&static_cast<bool>(lo&1)))++lo;
 return {sn<0?-lo:lo,e};
}
struct DoubleView {double value;bool exact,underflow,overflow;DoubleView(double v=0,bool e=true,bool u=false,bool o=false):value(v),exact(e),underflow(u),overflow(o){}};
inline DoubleView rationalDoubleView(const SparseExponentAccumulator& N,const SparseExponentAccumulator& D) {

struct ScaledDD {

 static ScaledDD fromRatio(const SparseExponentAccumulator& N,const SparseExponentAccumulator& D){

#pragma once
#include <cmath>
#include <limits>
namespace fiveEq {
// Calculation only: never changes the conservative mass or P2 payload.
// Preserve the original rounding for ordinary inputs; scale subnormal operands
// into normal mantissas before dividing, then restore the exponent difference.
inline double phaseIntrinsicRatio(double mass,double fraction) {
    if(std::fpclassify(mass)!=FP_SUBNORMAL&&std::fpclassify(fraction)!=FP_SUBNORMAL)
        return mass/fraction;
    int em=0,ea=0;
    const double mm=std::frexp(mass,&em),ma=std::frexp(fraction,&ea);
    return std::scalbn(mm/ma,em-ea);
}
// A bound above binary64 range is noncontrolling (+infinity), rather than an
// overflow trap. Ordinary representable quotients retain their original bits.
inline double positivePhaseTimeRatio(double integral,double outgoing) {
    if(!(outgoing>0))return std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
    if(static_cast<long double>(integral)/outgoing>std::numeric_limits<double>::max())
        return std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
    return integral/outgoing;
}
}

#include "fiveEqR1SharedTheta.H"
#include <algorithm>
namespace fiveEq {
R1Cell r1SharedCell(const MaterialCell& q,std::vector<MaterialCell> increments,const fiveEqRuntimeBridge& b){
    R1Cell r;r.theta=1;double linear=1;
    for(auto& d:increments){d[9]=d[1]-(d[7]+d[8]);ThetaResult bound;bound.linear=thetaLinearBound(q,d,bound.reason);linear=std::min(linear,bound.linear);r.bounds.push_back(bound);}
    // Continue the intersection at a COMMON theta. Independent face intervals
    // cannot safely be intersected by taking their last valid endpoints alone.
    auto valid=[&](double theta){bool all=true;for(unsigned i=0;i<increments.size();++i){auto f=sharedThetaFace(q,increments[i],theta);auto& bound=r.bounds[i];++bound.queries;
        bool present=!((q[0]>0&&(f[0]==0||f[2]==0))||(q[0]<1&&(f[0]==1||f[1]==0)));
        auto v=checkMaterialCell(f,b,true);bool ok=present&&v.issue==AdmissibilityIssue::OK;
        if(!ok){bound.reason=!present?"phase_presence":v.issue==AdmissibilityIssue::RECOVERY?recoveryCodeName(v.diagnostic.code):"linear_or_species";}all=all&&ok;}return all;
    };
    if(!valid(0))throw std::invalid_argument("R1 anchor invalid");
    double lo=0,hi=linear;bool exit=false;
    for(unsigned j=1;j<=32;++j){double t=linear*double(j)/32;if(!valid(t)){hi=t;exit=true;break;}lo=t;}
    if(exit)for(unsigned j=0;j<52;++j){double t=lo+(hi-lo)/2;if(t==lo||t==hi)break;if(valid(t))lo=t;else hi=t;}
    r.theta=lo;
    for(auto& bound:r.bounds){bound.valid=lo;bound.invalid=hi;}
    for(auto& d:increments){auto f=sharedThetaFace(q,d,r.theta);if(checkMaterialCell(f,b,true).issue!=AdmissibilityIssue::OK)throw std::runtime_error("Final shared theta face invalid");r.faces.push_back(f);}
    return r;
}
}

#include "fiveEqR2ThermoReconstruction.H"
namespace fiveEq {
R2Face r2ThermoFace(const std::array<MaterialCell,4>& cells,bool pos,const MaterialCell& fallback,
 const fiveEqRuntimeBridge& bridge,const fiveEqStateRecovery& eos,const Foam::wordList& names){
    R2Face r;r.q=fallback;std::array<fiveEqRecoveredState,4> s;
    bool gas=cells[pos?1:2][1]>0,liquid=cells[pos?1:2][2]>0;
    for(unsigned i=0;i<4;++i){auto v=checkMaterialCell(cells[i],bridge);if(v.issue!=AdmissibilityIssue::OK){r.route="INVALID_CELL";return r;}s[i]=v.recovered.thermo;
        if((cells[i][1]>0)!=gas||(cells[i][2]>0)!=liquid)return r;
    }
    auto scalar=[&](const std::array<double,4>& a){return of7MinmodFace(a[0],a[1],a[2],a[3],pos);};
    double alpha=scalar({{cells[0][0],cells[1][0],cells[2][0],cells[3][0]}});
    if((gas&&alpha==1)||(liquid&&alpha==0))return r;
    double p=scalar({{s[0].p,s[1].p,s[2].p,s[3].p}}),tg=0,tl=0;
    if(gas)tg=scalar({{s[0].TG,s[1].TG,s[2].TG,s[3].TG}});
    if(liquid)tl=scalar({{s[0].TL,s[1].TL,s[2].TL,s[3].TL}});
    auto U=of7MinmodVFace(Foam::vector(s[0].U[0],s[0].U[1],s[0].U[2]),Foam::vector(s[1].U[0],s[1].U[1],s[1].U[2]),Foam::vector(s[2].U[0],s[2].U[1],s[2].U[2]),Foam::vector(s[3].U[0],s[3].U[1],s[3].U[2]),pos);
    std::vector<double> Y(3,0);std::array<unsigned,3> index;
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){unsigned k=names[j]=="C10H22"?0:names[j]=="O2"?1:2;index[k]=j;}
    if(gas){for(unsigned k=0;k<2;++k){unsigned j=index[k];Y[k]=scalar({{cells[0][7+j]/cells[0][1],cells[1][7+j]/cells[1][1],cells[2][7+j]/cells[2][1],cells[3][7+j]/cells[3][1]}});}Y[2]=1-Y[0]-Y[1];}
    try{auto q=eos.generate(alpha,p,tg,tl,{{U.x(),U.y(),U.z()}},Y);
        MaterialCell f={{q.alphaL,q.mG,q.mL,q.M[0],q.M[1],q.M[2],q.Et,0,0,0}};
        if(gas)for(unsigned k=0;k<3;++k)f[7+index[k]]=q.mG*Y[k];
        if(checkMaterialCell(f,bridge,true).issue!=AdmissibilityIssue::OK){r.route="INVALID_CONSTRUCTION_FALLBACK_R1";return r;}
        r.q=f;r.route="DIRECT";return r;
    }catch(const std::exception&){r.route="INVALID_CONSTRUCTION_FALLBACK_R1";return r;}
}

#include "fiveEqReconstruction.H"
#include <cmath>
namespace fiveEq {
fiveEqRuntimeInput runtimeInput(const MaterialCell& q){
    const double sum=q[7]+q[8]+q[9];
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)if(!std::isfinite(q[7+j])||q[7+j]<0)
        throw std::invalid_argument("Invalid conservative species mass");
    if((q[1]==0&&sum!=0)||(q[1]>0&&std::abs(sum-q[1])/q[1]>1e-12))
        throw std::invalid_argument("Species masses do not close to mG");
    Foam::scalarField Y;
    if(q[1]>0){Y=Foam::scalarField(3,0);for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)Y[j]=q[7+j]/q[1];}
    return fiveEqRuntimeInput(q[0],q[1],q[2],Foam::vector(q[3],q[4],q[5]),q[6],Y);
}
CellAdmissibility checkMaterialCell(const MaterialCell& q,const fiveEqRuntimeBridge& bridge,bool face){
    CellAdmissibility d;
    for(double x:q)if(!std::isfinite(x)){d.issue=AdmissibilityIssue::NONFINITE;return d;}
    if(q[0]<0||q[0]>1){d.issue=AdmissibilityIssue::ALPHA;return d;}
    if(q[1]<0||q[2]<0||q[1]+q[2]<=0){d.issue=AdmissibilityIssue::MASS;return d;}
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)if(q[7+j]<0){d.issue=AdmissibilityIssue::SPECIES;return d;}
    const double sum=q[7]+q[8]+q[9];
    if((q[1]==0&&sum!=0)||(q[1]>0&&std::abs(sum-q[1])/q[1]>1e-12)){
        d.issue=AdmissibilityIssue::SPECIES;return d;
    }
    if((q[0]==0&&q[2]!=0)||(q[0]==1&&q[1]!=0)){
        d.issue=AdmissibilityIssue::PRESENCE;return d;
    }
    const auto in=runtimeInput(q);
    const bool ok=face?bridge.recoverFaceState(in,d.recovered,d.diagnostic):bridge.recoverCellState(in,d.recovered,d.diagnostic);
    d.issue=ok?AdmissibilityIssue::OK:AdmissibilityIssue::RECOVERY;return d;
}
fiveEqKTFaceFlux reconstructR0(const MaterialCell& L,const MaterialCell& R,
    const fiveEqKTFaceFluxKernel& flux,const Foam::vector& S,double meshPhi){
    // Caller validates cell-level species-mass closure; bridge validates Yi.
    return flux.evaluate(runtimeInput(L),runtimeInput(R),S,meshPhi);
}
}

#include "fiveEqKTFaceFlux.H"

namespace fiveEq {
// Authoritative local conservative state; species masses in runtime name order.
// alpha,mG,mL,Mx,My,Mz,Et,mY0,mY1,mY2. No p/T/phase-density owner.
using MaterialCell=std::array<double,10>;

struct CellAdmissibility {
    AdmissibilityIssue issue=AdmissibilityIssue::RECOVERY;
    fiveEqRuntimeState recovered;
    fiveEqRecoveryDiagnostic diagnostic;
};
