#include "fiveEqThermodynamicTimeStep.H"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
namespace fiveEq {
ThermodynamicStep connectedThermodynamicStep(const MaterialCell& q,double V,double dt,
 const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& L,const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& R,double ml,double mr,
 const fiveEqRuntimeBridge& bridge,
 const std::function<void(double,const ExplicitCellTrial&,const CellAdmissibility&)>& observer,unsigned subdivisions){
    ThermodynamicStep b;
    if(!(V>0&&dt>0&&std::isfinite(dt)&&subdivisions>0))throw std::invalid_argument("Invalid thermodynamic continuation configuration");
    auto test=[&](double x){auto t=explicitTrial(q,V,x*dt,L,R,ml,mr);auto a=checkMaterialCell(t.q,bridge);++b.queries;
        if(observer)observer(x,t,a);
        return a.issue==AdmissibilityIssue::OK;
    };
    b.initialValid=test(0);if(!b.initialValid){b.failure=R0FailureClass::NUMERICAL_THERMO_INADMISSIBILITY;return b;}
    double lo=0,hi=1;
    // Deterministic continuation, stop at FIRST sampled exit. Never search re-entry.
    // Finite sampling is not a mathematical certification between sample points.
    for(unsigned j=1;j<=subdivisions;++j){double x=double(j)/subdivisions;
        if(!test(x)){hi=x;b.exitFound=true;break;}lo=x;
    }
    if(b.exitFound){b.failure=R0FailureClass::NUMERICAL_THERMO_INADMISSIBILITY;
        for(unsigned j=0;j<52;++j){double mid=lo+(hi-lo)/2;if(mid==lo||mid==hi)break;
            if(test(mid))lo=mid;else hi=mid;
        }
    }
    b.lambdaValid=lo;b.lambdaInvalid=b.exitFound?hi:1;b.dtThermo=lo*dt;
    return b;
}
bool mixedOutwardBoundary(const MaterialCell& q,double V,const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& L,const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& R,
 double ml,double mr,const fiveEqRuntimeBridge& bridge,const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator& gas,
 const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& eos,const std::array<unsigned,3>& index){
    if(!(q[0]>0&&q[0]<1&&q[1]>0&&q[2]>0))return false;
    auto v=checkMaterialCell(q,bridge);if(v.issue!=AdmissibilityIssue::OK)return false;
    const auto& s=v.recovered.thermo;
    const auto li=eos.evaluateRhoP(s.rhoL,s.p);
    const double D=mr-ml;
    const double ad=(-(R.alphaPhiCandidate-L.alphaPhiCandidate)+q[0]*(R.wave.phiRelStar-L.wave.phiRelStar))/V;
    const double gd=(-(R.mGPhi-L.mGPhi)-q[1]*D)/V,ld=(-(R.mLPhi-L.mLPhi)-q[2]*D)/V;
    const double rgd=(gd+s.rhoG*ad)/(1-q[0]),rld=(ld-s.rhoL*ad)/q[0];
    const double Ed=(-(R.energyPhi-L.energyPhi)-q[6]*D)/V,r=s.rhoMix,rd=gd+ld;
    double kd=0;for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){const double Md=(-(R.momentumPhi[j]-L.momentumPhi[j])-q[3+j]*D)/V;
        kd+=q[3+j]*Md/r-.5*q[3+j]*q[3+j]*rd/(r*r);}
    std::vector<double> Y(3);double Rdot=0,eYdot=0;
    for(unsigned k=0;k<3;++k){unsigned j=index[k];Y[k]=q[7+j]/q[1];
        const double myd=(-(R.speciesPhi[j]-L.speciesPhi[j])-q[7+j]*D)/V;
        const double yd=(myd-Y[k]*gd)/q[1];
        Rdot+=yd*gas.speciesData()[k].R();eYdot+=yd*gas.speciesData()[k].Es(s.p,s.TG);
    }
    const auto g=gas.evaluateRhoP(s.rhoG,s.p,Y);eYdot-=g.Cv*s.TG*Rdot/g.R;
    const double egRho=-g.beta*s.p/s.rhoG,elRho=-li.beta*li.dpDrhoE;
    const double pd=(Ed-kd-gd*s.eG-ld*s.eL-q[1]*(egRho*rgd+eYdot)-q[2]*elRho*rld)/(q[1]*g.beta+q[2]*li.beta);
    const double eRT=(li.p-li.T*li.Cv/li.beta)/(li.rho*li.rho);
    const double td=(li.beta*pd+(elRho-eRT)*rld)/li.Cv;
    const double tgd=g.T*(pd/g.p-rgd/g.rho-Rdot/g.R);
    // Mixed recovery returns exact EOS endpoint temperature on root-boundary exhaustion.
    return (li.T==285&&td<0)||(li.T==320&&td>0)||(li.p==50000&&pd<0)||(li.p==12e6&&pd>0)
        ||(g.T==100&&tgd<0)||(g.T==6000&&tgd>0);
}
bool pureLiquidOutwardBoundary(const MaterialCell& q,double V,const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& L,
 const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& R,double ml,double mr,const fiveEqRuntimeBridge& bridge,const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& eos,double seedP,double seedT){
    if(q[0]!=1||q[1]!=0)return false;
    auto v=checkMaterialCell(q,bridge);if(v.issue!=AdmissibilityIssue::OK)return false;
    const auto s=eos.evaluateRhoP(q[2],v.recovered.thermo.p);
    const double D=mr-ml,rho=s.rho;
    const double rd=(-(R.mLPhi-L.mLPhi)-q[2]*D)/V;
    const double Ed=(-(R.energyPhi-L.energyPhi)-q[6]*D)/V;
    double kd=0;
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){const double Md=(-(R.momentumPhi[j]-L.momentumPhi[j])-q[3+j]*D)/V;
        kd+=q[3+j]*Md/rho-.5*q[3+j]*q[3+j]*rd/(rho*rho);}
    const double ed=(Ed-kd-s.e*rd)/rho;
    const double pd=s.dpDrhoE*rd+s.dpDeRho*ed;
    const double eRT=(s.p-s.T*s.Cv/s.beta)/(rho*rho),td=(ed-eRT*rd)/s.Cv;
    // Exact seed metadata is available only for user-defined synthetic boundary states.
    // Without such metadata, a merely near-boundary interior state is NOT domain escape.
    if(std::isfinite(seedP)&&std::abs(seedP-s.p)>1e-3)throw std::invalid_argument("Certified pressure seed mismatch");
    if(std::isfinite(seedT)&&std::abs(seedT-s.T)>1e-8)throw std::invalid_argument("Certified temperature seed mismatch");
    const double p=std::isfinite(seedP)?seedP:s.p,T=std::isfinite(seedT)?seedT:s.T;
    return (p==50000&&pd<0)||(p==12e6&&pd>0)||(T==285&&td<0)||(T==320&&td>0);
}
}

#include "fiveEqThetaAdmissibility.H"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
namespace fiveEq {
MaterialCell sharedThetaFace(const MaterialCell& q,const MaterialCell& delta,double t){
    if(t==0)return q; // exact R0 identity, including trace and stored species values
    MaterialCell f;for(unsigned j=0;j<10;++j)f[j]=std::fma(t,delta[j],q[j]);
    // N-1 independent runtime-ordered species. Dependent face closure, not normalization.
    f[9]=f[1]-(f[7]+f[8]);return f;
}
double thetaLinearBound(const MaterialCell& q,const MaterialCell& d,std::string& why){
    double t=1;auto bound=[&](double x,double dx,const char* name){if(dx<0){double b=-x/dx;if(b<t){t=std::max(0.,b);why=name;}}};
    bound(q[0],d[0],"alpha");bound(1-q[0],-d[0],"alpha");
    bound(q[1],d[1],"partial_mass");bound(q[2],d[2],"partial_mass");
    for(unsigned j=7;j<10;++j){bound(q[j],d[j],"species");}
    return t;
}
ThetaResult connectedFaceTheta(const MaterialCell& q,const MaterialCell& d,const fiveEqRuntimeBridge& bridge){
    ThetaResult r;r.linear=thetaLinearBound(q,d,r.reason);
    auto check=[&](double t){auto f=sharedThetaFace(q,d,t);++r.queries;
        // Nonzero cell phases must not be extinguished by a reconstruction limiter.
        if((q[0]>0&&(f[0]==0||f[2]==0))||(q[0]<1&&(f[0]==1||f[1]==0))){r.reason="phase_presence";return false;}
        auto v=checkMaterialCell(f,bridge,true);
        if(v.issue!=AdmissibilityIssue::OK){r.reason=v.issue==AdmissibilityIssue::RECOVERY?recoveryCodeName(v.diagnostic.code):"linear_or_species";return false;}return true;
    };
    if(!check(0))throw std::invalid_argument("R1 anchor invalid");
    double lo=0,hi=r.linear;bool exit=false;
    for(unsigned j=1;j<=32;++j){double t=r.linear*double(j)/32;if(!check(t)){hi=t;exit=true;break;}lo=t;}
    if(exit)for(unsigned j=0;j<52;++j){double t=lo+(hi-lo)/2;if(t==lo||t==hi)break;if(check(t))lo=t;else hi=t;}
    r.valid=lo;r.invalid=hi;return r;
}
}

#pragma once

#include "fiveEqTimeStepBounds.H"
#include "fiveEqAlphaDiscrete.H"
#include "../fiveEqPrecision/fiveEqScaledRatio.H"
#include <cmath>
#include <limits>
#include <algorithm>
namespace fiveEq {
namespace {
double positiveBound(double integral,double outgoing){return positivePhaseTimeRatio(integral,outgoing);}
void requireFace(const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& f){if(f.status!=FaceFluxStatus::OK)throw std::invalid_argument("Invalid flux passed to update");}
}
TimeStepBounds explicitTimeStepBounds(const MaterialCell& q,double V,
    const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& L,const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& R,double meshL,double meshR,double cfl,double safety){
    requireFace(L);requireFace(R);
    if(!(V>0&&cfl>0&&cfl<=1&&safety>0&&safety<=1))throw std::invalid_argument("Invalid timestep settings");
    const double D=meshR-meshL;
    const double N=-(R.alphaPhiCandidate-L.alphaPhiCandidate)+q[0]*(R.wave.phiRelStar-L.wave.phiRelStar);
    TimeStepBounds b;
    const double waveL=std::max(std::abs(L.wave.ap),std::abs(L.wave.am));
    const double waveR=std::max(std::abs(R.wave.ap),std::abs(R.wave.am));
    b.acoustic=cfl*2*V/(waveL+waveR);
    // alpha lower: a V+dt(a D+N)>=0; upper: (1-a)V+dt((1-a)D-N)>=0.
    b.alpha=std::min(positiveBound(q[0]*V,-q[0]*D-N),positiveBound((1-q[0])*V,N-(1-q[0])*D));
    b.mG=positiveBound(V*q[1],R.mGPhi-L.mGPhi);b.mL=positiveBound(V*q[2],R.mLPhi-L.mLPhi);
    b.species=std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)b.species=std::min(b.species,positiveBound(V*q[7+j],R.speciesPhi[j]-L.speciesPhi[j]));
    b.volume=D<0?V/(-D):std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity();
    b.joint=b.acoustic;b.controlling="acoustic";
    for(auto v:{std::make_pair(b.alpha,"alpha"),std::make_pair(b.mG,"mG"),std::make_pair(b.mL,"mL"),std::make_pair(b.species,"species"),std::make_pair(b.volume,"volume")}){
        if(safety*v.first<b.joint){b.joint=safety*v.first;b.controlling=v.second;}
    }
    return b;
}
ExplicitCellTrial explicitTrial(const MaterialCell& q,double V,double dt,
    const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& L,const fiveEqKTFaceFlux& R,double meshL,double meshR){
    requireFace(L);requireFace(R);
    auto a=alphaEulerStep(q[0],V,dt,L.alphaPhiCandidate,R.alphaPhiCandidate,L.wave,R.wave,meshL,meshR);
    ExplicitCellTrial s;s.volume=a.volume;s.alphaSourceIntegral=a.sourceIntegral;s.q=q;s.q[0]=a.alpha;
    s.q[1]=(V*q[1]-dt*(R.mGPhi-L.mGPhi))/s.volume;
    s.q[2]=(V*q[2]-dt*(R.mLPhi-L.mLPhi))/s.volume;
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){s.q[3+j]=(V*q[3+j]-dt*(R.momentumPhi[j]-L.momentumPhi[j]))/s.volume;
        s.q[7+j]=(V*q[7+j]-dt*(R.speciesPhi[j]-L.speciesPhi[j]))/s.volume;}
    s.q[6]=(V*q[6]-dt*(R.energyPhi-L.energyPhi))/s.volume;
    // Fixed-volume representation-safe evaluation of the same conservative
    // Euler update. Forming V*q first can quantize a coherent subnormal phase
    // even with zero net flux. FMA retains the stored mass without that loss.
    if(meshL==0&&meshR==0){
        const double rate=dt/V;
        s.q[1]=std::fma(-rate,R.mGPhi-L.mGPhi,q[1]);
        s.q[2]=std::fma(-rate,R.mLPhi-L.mLPhi,q[2]);
        for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){
            s.q[3+j]=std::fma(-rate,R.momentumPhi[j]-L.momentumPhi[j],q[3+j]);
            s.q[7+j]=std::fma(-rate,R.speciesPhi[j]-L.speciesPhi[j],q[7+j]);
        }
        s.q[6]=std::fma(-rate,R.energyPhi-L.energyPhi,q[6]);
    }
    // No repair, species normalization, energy positivity surrogate or EOS extrapolation.
    return s;
}
}

#pragma once
#include "fiveEqState.H"
#include <cmath>
namespace fiveEq {
inline double allaireSoundSpeed(const fiveEqRecoveredState& s) {
    if(s.recoveryMode==RecoveryMode::PURE_GAS)return s.cG;
    if(s.recoveryMode==RecoveryMode::PURE_LIQUID)return s.cL;
    const double xiG=s.rhoG*s.betaG,xiL=s.rhoL*s.betaL;
    const double numerator=(s.mG*xiG*s.cG*s.cG+s.mL*xiL*s.cL*s.cL)/s.rhoMix;
    const double denominator=s.alphaG*xiG+s.alphaL*xiL;
    if(!(numerator>0&&denominator>0&&std::isfinite(numerator)&&std::isfinite(denominator)))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_ALLAIRE_ACOUSTICS");
    return std::sqrt(numerator/denominator);
}
}

#include "fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator.H"
#include "IFstream.H"
#include "dictionary.H"
#include <cmath>
#include <algorithm>
namespace fiveEq {
std::vector<GasThermo> fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator::load(const std::string& path) {
    Foam::IFstream stream(path); Foam::dictionary d(stream);
    return {GasThermo(d.subDict("C10H22")),GasThermo(d.subDict("O2")),GasThermo(d.subDict("N2"))};
}
GasThermo fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator::mixture(const std::vector<double>& Y) const {
    if(Y.size()!=species_.size()) throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GAS_COMPOSITION_SIZE");
    double sum=0; for(double y:Y){if(!std::isfinite(y)||y<0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GAS_COMPOSITION");sum+=y;}
    if(std::abs(sum-1)>1e-12)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GAS_COMPOSITION_SUM");
    GasThermo mix=Y[0]*species_[0];
    for(unsigned i=1;i<Y.size();++i) mix+=Y[i]*species_[i];
    return mix; // OF7 local value: never changes global mixture_/thermo fields.
}
GasState fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator::evaluatePT(double p,double T,const std::vector<double>& Y) const {
    const GasThermo mix=mixture(Y);
    if(!std::isfinite(p)||p<=0||!std::isfinite(T)||T<mix.Tlow()||T>mix.Thigh())throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GAS_PT");
    GasState s; s.p=p;s.T=T;s.R=mix.R();s.rho=p/(s.R*T);
    s.Cp=mix.Cp(p,T);s.Cv=mix.Cv(p,T);s.e=mix.HE(p,T);
    s.c=std::sqrt(mix.gamma(p,T)/mix.psi(p,T));s.beta=s.Cv/(s.rho*s.R);
    s.dpDrhoE=p/s.rho; // e(T,Y) independent of density for perfectGas.
    if(!(s.R>0&&s.Cv>0&&s.Cp>0&&s.beta>0&&std::isfinite(s.c)&&std::isfinite(s.e)))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GAS_THERMO");
    return s;
}
GasState fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator::evaluateRhoP(double rho,double p,const std::vector<double>& Y) const {
    if(!std::isfinite(rho)||rho<=0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GAS_RHO");
    const auto mix=mixture(Y); return evaluatePT(p,p/(rho*mix.R()),Y);
}
std::array<double,2> fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator::pressureInterval(double rho,const std::vector<double>& Y) const {
    if(!std::isfinite(rho)||rho<=0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GAS_RHO");
    const auto mix=mixture(Y);const double factor=rho*mix.R();
    double lo=factor*mix.Tlow(),hi=factor*mix.Thigh();
    // Round computed interval endpoints inward, never expand temperature validity.
    while(lo/factor<mix.Tlow())lo=std::nextafter(lo,std::numeric_limits<double>::infinity());
    while(hi/factor>mix.Thigh())hi=std::nextafter(hi,0.);
    return {{lo,hi}};
}
GasState fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator::recoverRhoE(double rho,double target,const std::vector<double>& Y) const {
    if(!std::isfinite(rho)||rho<=0||!std::isfinite(target))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GAS_RHO_E");
    const auto mix=mixture(Y); const double R=mix.R();
    // Split JANAF branches: coefficients may have small discontinuities at Tcommon.
    // Never pretend a discontinuity gap is a physical root.
    const double edges[4]={mix.Tlow(),std::nextafter(mix.Tcommon(),mix.Tlow()),mix.Tcommon(),mix.Thigh()};
    double tol=2e-12*std::max(1e5,std::abs(target));
    for(unsigned branch=0;branch<2;++branch){
        double lo=edges[2*branch],hi=edges[2*branch+1];
        auto at=[&](double T){return evaluatePT(rho*R*T,T,Y);};
        auto sl=at(lo),sh=at(hi);double fl=sl.e-target,fh=sh.e-target;
        if(std::abs(fl)<=tol)return sl;
        if(std::abs(fh)<=tol)return sh;
        if(!(fl<0&&fh>0))continue;
        double T=(lo+hi)/2,delta=hi-lo;
        for(unsigned n=1;n<=100;++n){auto s=at(T);double f=s.e-target;
            if(std::abs(f)<=tol&&std::abs(delta)<=1e-9+2e-11*T){s.iterations=n;return s;}
            if(f<0)lo=T;else hi=T;
            double next=T-f/s.Cv;
            if(next==T&&std::abs(f)<=tol){s.iterations=n;return s;}
            if(!(next>lo&&next<hi))next=(lo+hi)/2;
            delta=next-T;T=next;
        }
        throw RecoveryError("GAS_INVERSION_NOT_CONVERGED");
    }
    throw RecoveryError("NO_GAS_ENERGY_ROOT_IN_JANAF_DOMAIN");
}
}

#include "fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator.H"
namespace fiveEq {
std::array<double,2> fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator::pressureInterval(double rho,unsigned* queryCalls) const {
    if(!std::isfinite(rho)||rho<=0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_LIQUID_RHO");
    double lo=50000,hi=12000000;
    auto density=[&](double p,double T){if(queryCalls)++*queryCalls;return eos_->evaluatePT(p,T).rho;};
    if(rho<density(lo,400)||rho>density(hi,265))throw RecoveryError("NO_LIQUID_PRESSURE_INTERVAL");
    auto crossing=[&](double T,bool lower){
        double a=50000,b=12000000;
        for(unsigned n=0;n<80&&b-a>1e-7;++n){double p=(a+b)/2;
            if(density(p,T)<rho)a=p;else b=p;
        }
        return lower?b:a; // inward admissible endpoints; never clamps query.
    };
    if(rho>density(lo,265))lo=crossing(265,true);
    if(rho<density(hi,400))hi=crossing(400,false);
    if(lo>hi)throw RecoveryError("EMPTY_LIQUID_PRESSURE_INTERVAL");
    return {{lo,hi}};
}
}

#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <vector>
#include <string>
#include <limits>
#include <stdexcept>
namespace fiveEq {
enum class PhasePresence { ABSENT, TRACE_PRESENT, PRESENT, INCONSISTENT };
constexpr unsigned phasePresenceContractVersion=2;
enum class RecoveryMode { PURE_GAS, MIXED, PURE_LIQUID, INVALID };
struct RecoveryError: std::runtime_error { using std::runtime_error::runtime_error; };
struct fiveEqConservativeState {
    double alphaL=0,mG=0,mL=0,Et=0;
    std::array<double,3> M={{0,0,0}};
    // Fixed species order from audited reactions: C10H22, O2, N2.
    std::vector<double> YG; // Caller-owned local composition; no default air.
};
struct QueryCounters { unsigned gas=0,liquid=0,residual=0,fallback=0; };
struct fiveEqRecoveredState {
    PhasePresence gasPresence=PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT, liquidPresence=PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT;
    double alphaG=0,alphaL=0,mG=0,mL=0,rhoMix=0;
    double rhoG=0,rhoL=0,p=0,TG=0,TL=0,eG=0,eL=0;
    double cG=0,cL=0,cMix=0,betaG=0,betaL=0,K=0;
    double energyResidual=0,normalizedEnergyResidual=0,pressureIncrement=0;
    double bracketMin=0,bracketMax=0;
    std::array<double,3> U={{0,0,0}};
    std::string gasValidity="ABSENT",liquidValidity="ABSENT",diagnosticFlags="OK";
    RecoveryMode recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::INVALID;
    unsigned iterations=0;
    QueryCounters calls;
};
struct phasePresencePolicy {
    // Dimensionless roundoff diagnostics, NOT an alpha transport floor.
    double alphaPresenceTol=64*std::numeric_limits<double>::epsilon();
    double massPresenceRelativeTol=64*std::numeric_limits<double>::epsilon();
    double massPresenceAbsoluteTol=0; // SI kg/m3; no arbitrary density floor.
};
enum class InitialGuess { MIDPOINT, PROVIDED, ENERGY_SECANT };
struct RecoveryOptions {
    InitialGuess initialGuess=InitialGuess::MIDPOINT;
    double knownPressure=std::numeric_limits<double>::quiet_NaN();
    double pressureAbsTol=1e-4,pressureRelTol=2e-11;
    double energyRelativeTol=2e-12,energyScalePerMass=1e5;
    unsigned maxIterations=100;
};
}

#include "fiveEqStateRecovery.H"
#include <algorithm>
#include <cmath>
namespace fiveEq {
fiveEqConservativeState fiveEqStateRecovery::generate(double a,double p,double TG,double TL,const std::array<double,3>& U,const std::vector<double>& Y) const {
    if(!std::isfinite(a)||a<0||a>1)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GENERATOR_ALPHA");
    fiveEqConservativeState q;q.alphaL=a;q.YG=Y;double internal=0;
    if(a<1){auto g=gas_.evaluatePT(p,TG,Y);q.mG=(1-a)*g.rho;internal+=q.mG*g.e;}
    if(a>0){auto l=liquid_.evaluatePT(p,TL);q.mL=a*l.rho;internal+=q.mL*l.e;}
    const double r=q.mG+q.mL;double v2=0;
    for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){if(!std::isfinite(U[j]))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_GENERATOR_U");q.M[j]=r*U[j];v2+=U[j]*U[j];}
    q.Et=internal+0.5*r*v2;return q;
}
fiveEqRecoveredState fiveEqStateRecovery::recover(const fiveEqConservativeState& q,const RecoveryOptions& o,fiveEqRecoveredState* progress) const {
    if(!std::isfinite(q.alphaL)||q.alphaL<0||q.alphaL>1||!std::isfinite(q.mG)||!std::isfinite(q.mL)||q.mG<0||q.mL<0||!std::isfinite(q.Et))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_CONSERVATIVE_STATE");
    fiveEqRecoveredState s;s.alphaL=q.alphaL;s.alphaG=1-q.alphaL;s.mG=q.mG;s.mL=q.mL;s.rhoMix=q.mG+q.mL;
    // Query-local snapshot on success or exception; does not alter root mathematics.
    struct Snapshot { fiveEqRecoveredState* dst; fiveEqRecoveredState& src;
        ~Snapshot(){if(dst)*dst=src;}
    } snapshot={progress,s};
    if(!(s.rhoMix>0&&std::isfinite(s.rhoMix)))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_TOTAL_DENSITY");
    if(!(o.pressureAbsTol>0&&o.pressureRelTol>0&&o.energyRelativeTol>0&&o.energyScalePerMass>0&&o.maxIterations>0))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_RECOVERY_OPTIONS");
    double kin=0;for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){if(!std::isfinite(q.M[j]))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_MOMENTUM");s.U[j]=q.M[j]/s.rhoMix;kin+=0.5*q.M[j]*s.U[j];}
    const double internal=q.Et-kin,scale=std::max(std::abs(internal),s.rhoMix*o.energyScalePerMass);
    if(!std::isfinite(internal)||!std::isfinite(scale))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_INTERNAL_ENERGY");
    // Presence BEFORE intrinsic-density divisions. Never discard trace mass.
    if(!(presence_.alphaPresenceTol>=0&&presence_.alphaPresenceTol<1&&presence_.massPresenceRelativeTol>=0&&presence_.massPresenceAbsoluteTol>=0))throw RecoveryError("INVALID_PHASE_PRESENCE_POLICY");
    // Contract v2: magnitude is diagnostic only; no phase deletion.
    auto presence=[&](double alpha,double m,PhasePresence& state){
        state=PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT;
        if(alpha==0){if(m!=0)throw RecoveryError("ABSENT_PHASE_HAS_MASS");state=PhasePresence::ABSENT;return false;}
        if(m==0)throw RecoveryError("PRESENT_PHASE_HAS_ZERO_MASS");
        if(std::fpclassify(alpha)==FP_SUBNORMAL||std::fpclassify(m)==FP_SUBNORMAL)
            throw RecoveryError("PRESENT_PHASE_SUBNORMAL_PRECISION_UNRESOLVED");
        const double rho=m/alpha;
        if(!(std::isfinite(rho)&&rho>0))throw RecoveryError("PRESENT_PHASE_INVALID_INTRINSIC_DENSITY");
        state=alpha<=presence_.alphaPresenceTol?PhasePresence::TRACE_PRESENT:PhasePresence::PRESENT;
        return true;
    };
    bool pg=presence(s.alphaG,q.mG,s.gasPresence),pl=presence(s.alphaL,q.mL,s.liquidPresence);
    auto putGas=[&](const GasState& g){s.p=g.p;s.rhoG=g.rho;s.TG=g.T;s.eG=g.e;s.cG=g.c;s.betaG=g.beta;s.gasValidity="VALID_JANAF";};
    auto putLiquid=[&](const liquidEOS::LiquidEOSState& l){s.p=l.p;s.rhoL=l.rho;s.TL=l.T;s.eL=l.e;s.cL=l.c;s.betaL=l.beta;s.liquidValidity=l.validity==liquidEOS::EOSValidity::NORMAL?"NORMAL":"BUFFER";};
    if(pg&&!pl){
        s.recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::PURE_GAS;++s.calls.gas;
        auto g=gas_.recoverRhoE(q.mG,internal/q.mG,q.YG);putGas(g);s.rhoG=q.mG;s.iterations=g.iterations;
        auto bracket=gas_.pressureInterval(q.mG,q.YG);s.bracketMin=bracket[0];s.bracketMax=bracket[1];
    }else if(pl&&!pg){
        s.recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::PURE_LIQUID;++s.calls.liquid;
        auto l=liquid_.recoverRhoE(q.mL,internal/q.mL);putLiquid(l);s.rhoL=q.mL;s.iterations=l.pressureIterations;
        // Pure liquid adapter performs its own strict bracket search.
        s.diagnosticFlags="PURE_LIQUID_FROZEN_EOS_BRACKET";
    }else{
        s.recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::MIXED;s.rhoG=q.mG/s.alphaG;s.rhoL=q.mL/s.alphaL;
        const double rg=s.rhoG,rl=s.rhoL;
        ++s.calls.gas;
        std::array<double,2> bg,bl;
        try{bg=gas_.pressureInterval(rg,q.YG);}catch(...){s.gasPresence=PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT;throw;}
        try{bl=liquid_.pressureInterval(rl,&s.calls.liquid);}catch(...){s.liquidPresence=PhasePresence::INCONSISTENT;throw;}
        double lo=std::max(bg[0],bl[0]),hi=std::min(bg[1],bl[1]);
        s.bracketMin=lo;s.bracketMax=hi;
        if(lo>hi)throw RecoveryError("NO_COMMON_VALID_PRESSURE_INTERVAL");
        // User-authorized exception: exact legal singleton, never a fuzzy interval.
        if(lo==hi)s.diagnosticFlags="EXACT_LEGAL_SINGLETON";
        auto residual=[&](double p){
            ++s.calls.residual;++s.calls.gas;++s.calls.liquid;
            const auto g=gas_.evaluateRhoP(rg,p,q.YG);const auto l=liquid_.evaluateRhoP(rl,p);
            putGas(g);putLiquid(l);s.rhoG=rg;s.rhoL=rl;
            const double f=q.mG*g.e+q.mL*l.e-internal,df=q.mG*g.beta+q.mL*l.beta;
            s.energyResidual=f;s.normalizedEnergyResidual=std::abs(f)/scale;
            if(!std::isfinite(f)||!std::isfinite(df)||df<=0)throw RecoveryError("INVALID_THERMODYNAMIC_ROOT_DERIVATIVE");
            return std::array<double,2>{{f,df}};
        };
        auto left=residual(lo),right=residual(hi);const double etol=o.energyRelativeTol*scale;
        bool done=false;double p=(lo+hi)/2,last=hi-lo;
        if(std::abs(left[0])<=etol){p=lo;done=true;}
        else if(std::abs(right[0])<=etol){p=hi;done=true;}
        else if(!(left[0]<0&&right[0]>0))throw RecoveryError("NO_PHYSICAL_ENERGY_ROOT_IN_VALID_DOMAIN");
        if(!done){
            if(o.initialGuess==InitialGuess::PROVIDED&&std::isfinite(o.knownPressure)&&o.knownPressure>lo&&o.knownPressure<hi)p=o.knownPressure;
            if(o.initialGuess==InitialGuess::ENERGY_SECANT){
                // Energy-weighted endpoint estimate, not a gas/liquid pressure average.
                p=lo+(hi-lo)*(-left[0])/(right[0]-left[0]);
                if(!(p>lo&&p<hi))p=(lo+hi)/2;
            }
            for(unsigned n=1;n<=o.maxIterations;++n){
                std::array<double,2> f;
                try{f=residual(p);}catch(const liquidEOS::EOSQueryError& e){
                    if(std::string(e.what()).find("INVALID")==std::string::npos)throw;
                    p=(lo+hi)/2;++s.calls.fallback;f=residual(p);
                }catch(const RecoveryError& e){
                    if(std::string(e.what())!="INVALID_GAS_PT")throw;
                    p=(lo+hi)/2;++s.calls.fallback;f=residual(p);
                }
                s.iterations=n;double correction=f[0]/f[1];
                const double ptol=o.pressureAbsTol+o.pressureRelTol*std::abs(p);
                // Both energy and the implied Newton pressure increment must pass.
                if(std::abs(f[0])<=etol&&(std::abs(last)<=ptol||std::abs(correction)<=ptol)){done=true;s.pressureIncrement=std::abs(correction);break;}
                if(f[0]<0)lo=p;else hi=p;
                double next=p-correction;
                // Also safeguard excessive steps to exercise/bracket contraction.
                if(!(next>lo&&next<hi)||!std::isfinite(next)||std::abs(next-p)>.75*(hi-lo)){next=(lo+hi)/2;++s.calls.fallback;}
                last=next-p;p=next;
            }
        }
        if(!done)throw RecoveryError("PRESSURE_ROOT_NOT_CONVERGED_OR_JANAF_GAP");
        residual(p); // fresh state, never reuse stale endpoint/iterate properties.
    }
    s.cMix=allaireSoundSpeed(s);
    const double reconstructed=q.mG*s.eG+q.mL*s.eL+kin;
    s.energyResidual=reconstructed-q.Et;s.normalizedEnergyResidual=std::abs(s.energyResidual)/scale;
    if(!(std::isfinite(s.p)&&s.p>0&&std::isfinite(s.cMix)&&s.cMix>0&&s.normalizedEnergyResidual<=4*o.energyRelativeTol))throw RecoveryError("RECOVERED_STATE_VALIDATION_FAILED");
    return s;
}
double fiveEqStateRecovery::dpDalpha(const fiveEqRecoveredState& s) const {
    if(s.recoveryMode!=RecoveryMode::MIXED)throw RecoveryError("ALPHA_DERIVATIVE_REQUIRES_TWO_PHASES");
    const double egRho=-s.betaG*s.p/s.rhoG;
    const double elRho=-s.betaL*(s.cL*s.cL-s.p/(s.rhoL*s.rhoL*s.betaL));
    const double ra=s.mG*egRho*s.rhoG/s.alphaG-s.mL*elRho*s.rhoL/s.alphaL;
    return -ra/(s.mG*s.betaG+s.mL*s.betaL);
}
}

namespace fiveEq {
class fiveEqStateRecovery {
    const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator& gas_;
    const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& liquid_;
    const phasePresencePolicy presence_;
public:
    fiveEqStateRecovery(const fiveEqGasThermoEvaluator& g,const fiveEqLiquidThermoEvaluator& l,phasePresencePolicy p={}):gas_(g),liquid_(l),presence_(p){}
    fiveEqRecoveredState recover(const fiveEqConservativeState&,const RecoveryOptions& options={},fiveEqRecoveredState* progress=nullptr) const;
    fiveEqRecoveredState tryRecover(const fiveEqConservativeState& q,const RecoveryOptions& options={}) const {
        try{return recover(q,options);}catch(const std::exception& e){
            fiveEqRecoveredState invalid;invalid.recoveryMode=RecoveryMode::INVALID;
            invalid.diagnosticFlags=e.what();return invalid;
        }
    }
    fiveEqConservativeState generate(double alphaL,double p,double TG,double TL,const std::array<double,3>& U,const std::vector<double>& Y={}) const;
    double dpDalpha(const fiveEqRecoveredState&) const;
};

enum class EOSValidity { NORMAL, BUFFER, INVALID };
struct LiquidEOSState {
    double p,T,rho,e,h,Cp,Cv,c,beta,dpDrhoE,dpDeRho;

        std::runtime_error("GibbsPotentialLiquidEOS: "+why),queryRho(r),queryP(p),queryT(T){}

class GibbsPotentialLiquidEOS: public rhosprayLiquidEOS {
    const double offset_;
    static void basis(double x,double a[9]) {a[0]=1;a[1]=x;for(int i=2;i<9;++i)a[i]=2*x*a[i-1]-a[i-2];}
    static std::array<double,6> potential(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);
        std::array<double,6> g={{0,0,0,0,0,0}};
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i)for(int j=0;j<9;++j){double v=a[i]*b[j];for(int k=0;k<6;++k)g[k]+=coefficients[k][i][j]*v;}
        return g;
    }
    static double volume(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);double s=0;
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i){for(int j=0;j<9;++j){s+=coefficients[1][i][j]*(a[i]*b[j]);}}
        return s;
    }
    static std::array<double,2> volumeDerivative(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);std::array<double,2>s={{0,0}};
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i){for(int j=0;j<9;++j){double v=a[i]*b[j];s[0]+=coefficients[1][i][j]*v;s[1]+=coefficients[4][i][j]*v;}}
        return s;
    }
    static EOSValidity ptClass(double p,double T) {
        if(!std::isfinite(p)||!std::isfinite(T)||p<50000||p>12000000||T<285||T>320)return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        return p>=100000&&p<=8000000&&T>=295&&T<=305?EOSValidity::NORMAL:EOSValidity::BUFFER;
    }
    double crossing(double rho,double T,bool lower) const {
        double lo=50000,hi=12000000;
        for(int n=0;n<70&&hi-lo>1e-7;++n){double p=(lo+hi)/2;double r=1/volume(p,T);if(r<rho)lo=p;else hi=p;}
        // Admissible search endpoint, not a returned-state clamp.
        return lower?hi:lo;
    }
public:
    static constexpr double productionEnergyOffset=369229.95372342807;
    explicit GibbsPotentialLiquidEOS(double energyOffset=productionEnergyOffset):offset_(energyOffset){
        if(!std::isfinite(offset_))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID energy offset",0,0,0);
    }
    double energyOffset() const {return offset_;}
    LiquidEOSState evaluatePT(double p,double T) const override {
        EOSValidity q=ptClass(p,T);if(q==EOSValidity::INVALID)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID PT",0,p,T);
        const auto g=potential(p,T);const double v=g[1];
        LiquidEOSState s;s.p=p;s.T=T;s.rho=1/v;s.Cp=-T*g[5];s.Cv=s.Cp+T*g[4]*g[4]/g[3];
        const double c2=-v*v/(g[3]-g[4]*g[4]/g[5]);s.c=std::sqrt(c2);
        s.beta=T*(-g[4]+g[5]*g[3]/g[4]);s.h=g[0]-T*g[2]+offset_;s.e=s.h-p*v;
        s.dpDeRho=1/s.beta;s.dpDrhoE=c2-p/(s.rho*s.rho*s.beta);s.validity=q;
        if(!(v>0&&s.Cp>0&&s.Cv>0&&c2>0&&s.beta>0&&std::isfinite(s.e)&&std::isfinite(s.c)))
            throw EOSQueryError("FAIL_THERMO",s.rho,p,T);
        return s;
    }
    EOSValidity classifyRhoP(double r,double p) const override {
        if(!std::isfinite(r)||r<=0||!std::isfinite(p)||p<50000||p>12000000)return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        if(r<1/volume(p,320)||r>1/volume(p,285))return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        if(p>=100000&&p<=8000000&&r>=1/volume(p,305)&&r<=1/volume(p,295))return EOSValidity::NORMAL;
        return EOSValidity::BUFFER;
    }
    LiquidEOSState evaluateRhoP(double r,double p) const override {
        const EOSValidity q=classifyRhoP(r,p);if(q==EOSValidity::INVALID)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,p",r,p,0);
        double lo=285,hi=320,T=300,target=1/r;unsigned it=0;bool found=false;
        for(double edge:{285.,295.,305.,320.})if(r==1/volume(p,edge)){T=edge;found=true;break;}
        if(!found){
            const double fl=volume(p,lo)-target,fh=volume(p,hi)-target;
            if(fl>0||fh<0)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL temperature bracket",r,p,T);
            for(double edge:{295.,305.,std::nextafter(295.,285.),std::nextafter(295.,305.),std::nextafter(305.,295.),std::nextafter(305.,320.)})
                if(ptClass(p,edge)==q&&std::abs(volume(p,edge)-target)<=2e-18){T=edge;found=true;break;}
            if(!found){
                for(it=1;it<=80;++it){const auto vd=volumeDerivative(p,T);double f=vd[0]-target;
                    if(std::abs(f)<=2e-18&&ptClass(p,T)==q){found=true;break;}
                    if(f<0)lo=T;else hi=T;
                    const double next=T-f/vd[1];T=next>lo&&next<hi?next:(lo+hi)/2;
                }
            }
        }
        if(!found)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL T iterations",r,p,T);
        LiquidEOSState s=evaluatePT(p,T);if(s.validity!=q)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL class disagreement",r,p,T);
        s.inversionIterations=it;s.volumeResidual=volume(p,T)-target;return s;
    }
    LiquidEOSState recoverRhoE(double r,double e) const override {
        if(!std::isfinite(r)||r<=0||!std::isfinite(e))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,e",r,0,0);
        double lo=50000,hi=12000000;
        if(1/volume(hi,285)<r||1/volume(lo,320)>r)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,e pressure domain",r,0,0);
        if(1/volume(lo,285)<r)lo=crossing(r,285,true);
        if(1/volume(hi,320)>r)hi=crossing(r,320,false);
        const double width=hi-lo;
        LiquidEOSState sl=evaluateRhoP(r,lo),sh=evaluateRhoP(r,hi);
        double fl=sl.e-e,fh=sh.e-e;
        const double etol=64*std::numeric_limits<double>::epsilon()*std::max(100000.,std::max(std::abs(e-offset_),std::abs(offset_)));
        auto finish=[&](LiquidEOSState s,unsigned n){s.pressureIterations=n;s.energyResidual=s.e-e;s.pressureBracketWidth=width;return s;};
        if(std::abs(fl)<=etol)return finish(sl,0);
        if(std::abs(fh)<=etol)return finish(sh,0);
        if(!(fl<0&&fh>0))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID energy not bracketed in HARD",r,0,0);
        double p=(lo+hi)/2;
        for(unsigned n=1;n<=80;++n){LiquidEOSState s=evaluateRhoP(r,p);const double f=s.e-e;
            if(std::abs(f)<=etol)return finish(s,n);
            if(f<0)lo=p;else hi=p;
            const double next=p-f/s.beta;p=next>lo&&next<hi?next:(lo+hi)/2;
        }
        throw EOSQueryError("FAIL pressure iterations",r,p,0);
    }
};
}

class GibbsPotentialLiquidEOSV2: public rhosprayLiquidEOS {
    const double offset_;
    static void basis(double x,double a[9]) {a[0]=1;a[1]=x;for(int i=2;i<9;++i)a[i]=2*x*a[i-1]-a[i-2];}
    static std::array<double,6> potential(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);
        std::array<double,6> g={{0,0,0,0,0,0}};
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i)for(int j=0;j<9;++j){double v=a[i]*b[j];for(int k=0;k<6;++k)g[k]+=v2Coefficients[k][i][j]*v;}
        return g;
    }
    static double volume(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);double s=0;
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i){for(int j=0;j<9;++j){s+=v2Coefficients[1][i][j]*(a[i]*b[j]);}}
        return s;
    }
    static std::array<double,2> volumeDerivative(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);std::array<double,2>s={{0,0}};
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i){for(int j=0;j<9;++j){double v=a[i]*b[j];s[0]+=v2Coefficients[1][i][j]*v;s[1]+=v2Coefficients[4][i][j]*v;}}
        return s;
    }
    static EOSValidity ptClass(double p,double T) {
        if(!std::isfinite(p)||!std::isfinite(T)||p<50000||p>12000000||T<285||T>330)return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        return p>=100000&&p<=8000000&&T>=295&&T<=305?EOSValidity::NORMAL:EOSValidity::BUFFER;
    }
    double crossing(double rho,double T,bool lower) const {
        double lo=50000,hi=12000000;
        for(int n=0;n<70&&hi-lo>1e-7;++n){double p=(lo+hi)/2;double r=1/volume(p,T);if(r<rho)lo=p;else hi=p;}
        // Admissible search endpoint, not a returned-state clamp.
        return lower?hi:lo;
    }
public:
    static constexpr double productionEnergyOffset=369229.95372342807;
    explicit GibbsPotentialLiquidEOSV2(double energyOffset=productionEnergyOffset):offset_(energyOffset){
        if(!std::isfinite(offset_))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID energy offset",0,0,0);
    }
    double energyOffset() const {return offset_;}
    LiquidEOSState evaluatePT(double p,double T) const override {
        EOSValidity q=ptClass(p,T);if(q==EOSValidity::INVALID)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID PT",0,p,T);
        const auto g=potential(p,T);const double v=g[1];
        LiquidEOSState s;s.p=p;s.T=T;s.rho=1/v;s.Cp=-T*g[5];s.Cv=s.Cp+T*g[4]*g[4]/g[3];
        const double c2=-v*v/(g[3]-g[4]*g[4]/g[5]);s.c=std::sqrt(c2);
        s.beta=T*(-g[4]+g[5]*g[3]/g[4]);s.h=g[0]-T*g[2]+offset_;s.e=s.h-p*v;
        s.dpDeRho=1/s.beta;s.dpDrhoE=c2-p/(s.rho*s.rho*s.beta);s.validity=q;
        if(!(v>0&&s.Cp>0&&s.Cv>0&&c2>0&&s.beta>0&&std::isfinite(s.e)&&std::isfinite(s.c)))
            throw EOSQueryError("FAIL_THERMO",s.rho,p,T);
        return s;
    }
    EOSValidity classifyRhoP(double r,double p) const override {
        if(!std::isfinite(r)||r<=0||!std::isfinite(p)||p<50000||p>12000000)return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        if(r<1/volume(p,330)||r>1/volume(p,285))return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        if(p>=100000&&p<=8000000&&r>=1/volume(p,305)&&r<=1/volume(p,295))return EOSValidity::NORMAL;
        return EOSValidity::BUFFER;
    }
    LiquidEOSState evaluateRhoP(double r,double p) const override {
        const EOSValidity q=classifyRhoP(r,p);if(q==EOSValidity::INVALID)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,p",r,p,0);
        double lo=285,hi=330,T=300,target=1/r;unsigned it=0;bool found=false;
        for(double edge:{285.,295.,305.,330.})if(r==1/volume(p,edge)){T=edge;found=true;break;}
        if(!found){
            const double fl=volume(p,lo)-target,fh=volume(p,hi)-target;
            if(fl>0||fh<0)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL temperature bracket",r,p,T);
            for(double edge:{295.,305.,std::nextafter(295.,285.),std::nextafter(295.,305.),std::nextafter(305.,295.),std::nextafter(305.,330.)})
                if(ptClass(p,edge)==q&&std::abs(volume(p,edge)-target)<=2e-18){T=edge;found=true;break;}
            if(!found){
                for(it=1;it<=80;++it){const auto vd=volumeDerivative(p,T);double f=vd[0]-target;
                    if(std::abs(f)<=2e-18&&ptClass(p,T)==q){found=true;break;}
                    if(f<0)lo=T;else hi=T;
                    const double next=T-f/vd[1];T=next>lo&&next<hi?next:(lo+hi)/2;
                }
            }
        }
        if(!found)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL T iterations",r,p,T);
        LiquidEOSState s=evaluatePT(p,T);if(s.validity!=q)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL class disagreement",r,p,T);
        s.inversionIterations=it;s.volumeResidual=volume(p,T)-target;return s;
    }
    LiquidEOSState recoverRhoE(double r,double e) const override {
        if(!std::isfinite(r)||r<=0||!std::isfinite(e))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,e",r,0,0);
        double lo=50000,hi=12000000;
        if(1/volume(hi,285)<r||1/volume(lo,330)>r)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,e pressure domain",r,0,0);
        if(1/volume(lo,285)<r)lo=crossing(r,285,true);
        if(1/volume(hi,330)>r)hi=crossing(r,330,false);
        const double width=hi-lo;
        LiquidEOSState sl=evaluateRhoP(r,lo),sh=evaluateRhoP(r,hi);
        double fl=sl.e-e,fh=sh.e-e;
        const double etol=64*std::numeric_limits<double>::epsilon()*std::max(100000.,std::max(std::abs(e-offset_),std::abs(offset_)));
        auto finish=[&](LiquidEOSState s,unsigned n){s.pressureIterations=n;s.energyResidual=s.e-e;s.pressureBracketWidth=width;return s;};
        if(std::abs(fl)<=etol)return finish(sl,0);
        if(std::abs(fh)<=etol)return finish(sh,0);
        if(!(fl<0&&fh>0))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID energy not bracketed in HARD",r,0,0);
        double p=(lo+hi)/2;
        for(unsigned n=1;n<=80;++n){LiquidEOSState s=evaluateRhoP(r,p);const double f=s.e-e;
            if(std::abs(f)<=etol)return finish(s,n);
            if(f<0)lo=p;else hi=p;
            const double next=p-f/s.beta;p=next>lo&&next<hi?next:(lo+hi)/2;
        }
        throw EOSQueryError("FAIL pressure iterations",r,p,0);
    }
};
}

class GibbsPotentialLiquidEOSV3: public rhosprayLiquidEOS {
    const double offset_;
    static void basis(double x,double a[9]) {a[0]=1;a[1]=x;for(int i=2;i<9;++i)a[i]=2*x*a[i-1]-a[i-2];}
    static std::array<double,6> potential(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);
        std::array<double,6> g={{0,0,0,0,0,0}};
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i)for(int j=0;j<9;++j){double v=a[i]*b[j];for(int k=0;k<6;++k)g[k]+=v3Coefficients[k][i][j]*v;}
        return g;
    }
    static double volume(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);double s=0;
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i){for(int j=0;j<9;++j){s+=v3Coefficients[1][i][j]*(a[i]*b[j]);}}
        return s;
    }
    static std::array<double,2> volumeDerivative(double p,double T) {
        double a[9],b[9];basis((p-6025000.)/5975000.,a);basis((T-302.5)/17.5,b);std::array<double,2>s={{0,0}};
        for(int i=0;i<9;++i){for(int j=0;j<9;++j){double v=a[i]*b[j];s[0]+=v3Coefficients[1][i][j]*v;s[1]+=v3Coefficients[4][i][j]*v;}}
        return s;
    }
    static EOSValidity ptClass(double p,double T) {
        if(!std::isfinite(p)||!std::isfinite(T)||p<50000||p>12000000||T<265||T>400)return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        return p>=100000&&p<=8000000&&T>=295&&T<=305?EOSValidity::NORMAL:EOSValidity::BUFFER;
    }
    double crossing(double rho,double T,bool lower) const {
        double lo=50000,hi=12000000;
        for(int n=0;n<70&&hi-lo>1e-7;++n){double p=(lo+hi)/2;double r=1/volume(p,T);if(r<rho)lo=p;else hi=p;}
        // Admissible search endpoint, not a returned-state clamp.
        return lower?hi:lo;
    }
public:
    static constexpr double productionEnergyOffset=369229.95372342807;
    explicit GibbsPotentialLiquidEOSV3(double energyOffset=productionEnergyOffset):offset_(energyOffset){
        if(!std::isfinite(offset_))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID energy offset",0,0,0);
    }
    double energyOffset() const {return offset_;}
    LiquidEOSState evaluatePT(double p,double T) const override {
        EOSValidity q=ptClass(p,T);if(q==EOSValidity::INVALID)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID PT",0,p,T);
        const auto g=potential(p,T);const double v=g[1];
        LiquidEOSState s;s.p=p;s.T=T;s.rho=1/v;s.Cp=-T*g[5];s.Cv=s.Cp+T*g[4]*g[4]/g[3];
        const double c2=-v*v/(g[3]-g[4]*g[4]/g[5]);s.c=std::sqrt(c2);
        s.beta=T*(-g[4]+g[5]*g[3]/g[4]);s.h=g[0]-T*g[2]+offset_;s.e=s.h-p*v;
        s.dpDeRho=1/s.beta;s.dpDrhoE=c2-p/(s.rho*s.rho*s.beta);s.validity=q;
        if(!(v>0&&s.Cp>0&&s.Cv>0&&c2>0&&s.beta>0&&std::isfinite(s.e)&&std::isfinite(s.c)))
            throw EOSQueryError("FAIL_THERMO",s.rho,p,T);
        return s;
    }
    EOSValidity classifyRhoP(double r,double p) const override {
        if(!std::isfinite(r)||r<=0||!std::isfinite(p)||p<50000||p>12000000)return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        if(r<1/volume(p,400)||r>1/volume(p,265))return EOSValidity::INVALID;
        if(p>=100000&&p<=8000000&&r>=1/volume(p,305)&&r<=1/volume(p,295))return EOSValidity::NORMAL;
        return EOSValidity::BUFFER;
    }
    LiquidEOSState evaluateRhoP(double r,double p) const override {
        const EOSValidity q=classifyRhoP(r,p);if(q==EOSValidity::INVALID)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,p",r,p,0);
        double lo=265,hi=400,T=300,target=1/r;unsigned it=0;bool found=false;
        for(double edge:{265.,295.,305.,400.})if(r==1/volume(p,edge)){T=edge;found=true;break;}
        if(!found){
            const double fl=volume(p,lo)-target,fh=volume(p,hi)-target;
            if(fl>0||fh<0)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL temperature bracket",r,p,T);
            for(double edge:{295.,305.,std::nextafter(295.,265.),std::nextafter(295.,305.),std::nextafter(305.,295.),std::nextafter(305.,400.)})
                if(ptClass(p,edge)==q&&std::abs(volume(p,edge)-target)<=2e-18){T=edge;found=true;break;}
            if(!found){
                for(it=1;it<=80;++it){const auto vd=volumeDerivative(p,T);double f=vd[0]-target;
                    if(std::abs(f)<=2e-18&&ptClass(p,T)==q){found=true;break;}
                    if(f<0)lo=T;else hi=T;
                    const double next=T-f/vd[1];T=next>lo&&next<hi?next:(lo+hi)/2;
                }
            }
        }
        if(!found)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL T iterations",r,p,T);
        LiquidEOSState s=evaluatePT(p,T);if(s.validity!=q)throw EOSQueryError("FAIL class disagreement",r,p,T);
        s.inversionIterations=it;s.volumeResidual=volume(p,T)-target;return s;
    }
    LiquidEOSState recoverRhoE(double r,double e) const override {
        if(!std::isfinite(r)||r<=0||!std::isfinite(e))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,e",r,0,0);
        double lo=50000,hi=12000000;
        if(1/volume(hi,265)<r||1/volume(lo,400)>r)throw EOSQueryError("INVALID rho,e pressure domain",r,0,0);
        if(1/volume(lo,265)<r)lo=crossing(r,265,true);
        if(1/volume(hi,400)>r)hi=crossing(r,400,false);
        const double width=hi-lo;
        LiquidEOSState sl=evaluateRhoP(r,lo),sh=evaluateRhoP(r,hi);
        double fl=sl.e-e,fh=sh.e-e;
        const double etol=64*std::numeric_limits<double>::epsilon()*std::max(100000.,std::max(std::abs(e-offset_),std::abs(offset_)));
        auto finish=[&](LiquidEOSState s,unsigned n){s.pressureIterations=n;s.energyResidual=s.e-e;s.pressureBracketWidth=width;return s;};
        if(std::abs(fl)<=etol)return finish(sl,0);
        if(std::abs(fh)<=etol)return finish(sh,0);
        if(!(fl<0&&fh>0))throw EOSQueryError("INVALID energy not bracketed in HARD",r,0,0);
        double p=(lo+hi)/2;
        for(unsigned n=1;n<=80;++n){LiquidEOSState s=evaluateRhoP(r,p);const double f=s.e-e;
            if(std::abs(f)<=etol)return finish(s,n);
            if(f<0)lo=p;else hi=p;
            const double next=p-f/s.beta;p=next>lo&&next<hi?next:(lo+hi)/2;
        }
        throw EOSQueryError("FAIL pressure iterations",r,p,0);
    }
};
}

class DynamicObservation {
 std::string path_;double dx_,L_;bool constantP2_;unsigned snapshotEvery_;
 std::ofstream history_;std::array<long double,9> sums0_{};bool started_=false;

 std::array<double,9> maxRaw_{},maxScaled_{};
public:
 DynamicObservation(const std::string& path,double dx,unsigned N,bool constantP2,unsigned snapshots):path_(path),dx_(dx),L_(N*dx),constantP2_(constantP2),snapshotEvery_(snapshots),history_(path+"/dynamic_history.csv") {
  history_<<std::setprecision(17)<<"step,time,dt,pMin,pMax,rhoMin,TGmin,TGmax,TLmin,TLmax,EintMin,marginMin,closure,CFL,pRe,pIm,alphaRe,alphaIm,fuelRe,fuelIm,uRe,uIm";

 void record(const fiveEq::TemporalState& q,const std::vector<fiveEq::fiveEqRecoveredState>& ss){
  if(!started_){p2_=q.committed[0].fraction.minorityCoordinateView();snapshot(q,ss,"initial");}
  std::array<long double,9> sums{};std::complex<long double> p{},a{},fuel{},vel{};
  double plo=1e300,phi=-1e300,rlo=1e300,tglo=1e300,tghi=-1e300,tllo=1e300,tlhi=-1e300,elo=1e300,margin=1e300,cfl=0,closure=0;

  if(runTime.writeTime()){runTime.write();if(scaledRepresentation){fiveEqScaledP2IO::write((runTime.path()/runTime.timeName()/"fiveEqRestart").c_str(),state,"C10H22:O2:N2",gasSHA);writeScaledAuthorityExport((runTime.path()/runTime.timeName()/"fiveEqAuthority.csv").c_str(),state);}else fiveEqP2IO::write((runTime.path()/runTime.timeName()/"fiveEqRestart").c_str(),state,"C10H22:O2:N2",model);}
 }
 // Final exact conservative/P2 contract. Production restart remains uncertified.
 if(scaledRepresentation)fiveEqScaledP2IO::write((evidence/"final_fiveEqRestart").c_str(),state,"C10H22:O2:N2",gasSHA);else fiveEqP2IO::write((evidence/"final_fiveEqRestart").c_str(),state,"C10H22:O2:N2",model);
 if(scaledRepresentation)writeScaledAuthorityExport((evidence/"final_authority.csv").c_str(),state);
 const auto obs=m6b0::observations();auto sums=integrals(state);
 std::ofstream diagnostic((evidence/"primitive_diagnostics.json").c_str());diagnostic<<std::setprecision(17)<<"{\"maxUniformPrimitiveDrift\":"<<maxUniformPrimitive<<",\"maxAlphaChange\":"<<maxAlphaChange<<",\"registryChecked\":true}\n";
 std::ofstream result((evidence/"result.json").c_str());result<<std::setprecision(17);
 result<<"{\"profile\":\""<<profile<<"\",\"steps\":"<<state.step<<",\"np\":1,\"registeredFields\":20,\"maxPressureRelative\":"<<maxP<<",\"maxUniformRelative\":"<<maxUniform<<",\"maxTraceRelative\":"<<maxTrace<<",\"maxAlphaAdvectionAbsolute\":"<<maxAlphaError<<",\"maxSpeciesClosureRelative\":"<<maxClosure<<",\"maxConservationRelative\":"<<maxConservation<<",\"branchChanges\":"<<signChanges<<",\"rejectedTrials\":"<<rejected<<",\"acceptedRecoveryCalls\":"<<acceptedCalls<<",\"acceptedRecoveryFailures\":"<<acceptedFailures<<",\"acceptedRecoveryIterations\":"<<acceptedIterations<<",\"allRecoveryCalls\":"<<obs.calls<<",\"allRecoveryFailuresIncludingProbes\":"<<obs.failures<<",\"allRecoveryIterations\":"<<obs.iterations<<",\"pureGasCalls\":"<<obs.gas<<",\"pureLiquidCalls\":"<<obs.liquid<<",\"mixedCalls\":"<<obs.mixed<<",\"gasQueries\":"<<obs.gasQueries<<",\"liquidQueries\":"<<obs.liquidQueries<<",\"conservationByComponent\":[";
 for(unsigned j=0;j<9;++j){if(j)result<<',';result<<static_cast<double>(std::abs(sums[j]-sums0[j])/std::max(std::abs(sums0[j]),static_cast<long double>(1.)));}result<<"]}\n";
 Info<<"M6B0 normal exit; serial verification micro-case only"<<nl;return 0;
 }catch(const std::exception& e){FatalErrorInFunction<<"M6B0 STOP: "<<e.what()<<exit(FatalError);return 1;}
}

# MFC Newtonian transport adoption — independent implementation stage

User authorization 2026-10-07: adopt MFC viscous stress/transport parameters, retain existing EOS. Transport material assumptions: dry air and liquid C10H22, interpreted as n-decane. User authorized selection/fitting of conditions. This supersedes self-designed constant-mu transport as the intended production direction; it does not rewrite the prior frozen CRV3 qualification or authorize CRV3-3.

Pinned MFC master commit: 3dc5b2f2a1b1b9c25672aa09d4fbfabee21a91e8. Upstream viscous source and MIT license are preserved here. Independent C++ tensor law: tau = eta*(gradU+gradU^T) + (zeta-2*eta/3)*divU*I. Mixture eta/zeta are volume-fraction weighted. One-dimensional longitudinal coefficient is 4*eta/3+zeta. No clipping, fraction normalization or phase/species diffusion is introduced.

Transport-only surrogate uses CoolProp 8.0.0 published correlations: Air (Lemmon/Jacobsen 2004) and n-Decane (Huber/Laesecke/Xiang 2004). No new experimental fit or physical uncertainty claim. The library EOS is used offline to evaluate reference transport at T,p; solver pressure/energy/recovery EOS remains untouched. Air viscosity is an explicit dry-air approximation, not validated for fuel-bearing/reacting gas composition. Bulk viscosity is set to zero as the MFC default/Stokes approximation, not fitted from shear data.

Declared fitting windows selected to cover existing qualification temperatures/pressures with margin: air 600–1200 K, 0.1–10 MPa; liquid 280–450 K, 1–10 MPa. The temperature bounds exclude critical liquid behavior. Pressure above critical with temperature below critical is accepted as compressed liquid (CoolProp supercritical_liquid label). No extrapolation or silent clamping is permitted. Wider production envelopes require additional transport qualification.

Files: MFCNewtonianTransport.H (constitutive law), TransportViscosityFit.H (generated positive viscosity evaluators), fit_transport.py, transport_fit_gate.json, verify_transport.C. Validation covers analytical longitudinal compression, simple shear, rigid rotation, isotropic compression, two-phase mixing, invalid inputs, 1000 random stress/dissipation identities and 4008 separate reference evaluation points through compiled C++.

Status: constitutive/transport prototype only. Not connected to the active solver, not a new PDE/physical qualification PASS. Next work: separate variable-coefficient conservative viscous momentum/energy work operator, common-EOS heat/entropy check and native authority preservation, independent shear/longitudinal periodic checks, then a separately identified driver/case. Do not retrofit existing frozen executable or label existing mu3 results as material viscosity results. A true material-viscosity shock can be much thinner; mesh feasibility must be assessed before launching the existing shock-resolution ladder with physical transport.

Current legacy N4096 run and controller are preserved. Before any controller transition, check process/lock identity and retain all completed/unfinished evidence. New-model integration must use a distinct model identity and restart metadata; existing production restart remains NOT CERTIFIED.

Sources:
- https://github.com/MFlowCode/MFC/tree/3dc5b2f2a1b1b9c25672aa09d4fbfabee21a91e8
- https://mflowcode.github.io/documentation/equations.html
- https://coolprop.org/fluid_properties/fluids/Air.html
- https://coolprop.org/fluid_properties/fluids/n-Decane.html


## Variable-coefficient work kernel checkpoint

PeriodicViscousWork.H now evolves all three velocity components with spatially variable positive face shear/bulk coefficients. Coefficients are frozen at the substep anchor; this is a linearized verification operator, not a nonlinear time-accuracy qualification. Midpoint traction and shared energy work flux satisfy nonnegative cell heating and periodic momentum/total-energy conservation. 100 randomized variable-density/coefficient cases and 3 independent Fourier eigenmodes PASS; max cell work error4.33681e-19, conservation8.13152e-20, linear residual1.688e-19. Strict compile warning was corrected with braces; numerical law unchanged. Dense solve is explicitly limited to N<=256 small fixtures, not production. Native retained-EOS recovery, entropy and both-level authority integration are next and have NOT yet run.

material_resolution_screen_v1.json estimates scales using the old constant-mu reference only; nominal mixture longitudinal coefficient approximately3.22505e-4Pa.s, indicative thickness approximately1e-6m and order8million cells per1m for8-cell pressure thickness. This is a feasibility warning/diagnostic, not a new variable-coefficient BVP result or instruction to launch such a grid. Existing oldmu3 controller untouched.

#pragma once
#include <array>
#include <cmath>
#include <stdexcept>

// Newtonian constitutive law and volume-fraction mixing from MFlowCode/MFC
// commit 3dc5b2f2a1b1b9c25672aa09d4fbfabee21a91e8, m_viscous.fpp.
// Independent C++ implementation. No EOS, state repair or discretization.
namespace mfcTransport {
using Tensor = std::array<std::array<long double,3>,3>;
struct Coefficients {
    long double shear, bulk; // Pa.s in an SI dimensional case
    long double longitudinal() const { return 4.L*shear/3.L+bulk; }
};
inline void validate(Coefficients c) {
    if (!std::isfinite(c.shear)||!std::isfinite(c.bulk)||c.shear<0||c.bulk<0)
        throw std::invalid_argument("INVALID_TRANSPORT_COEFFICIENT");
}
// Inputs are explicit dimensional viscosities, not inverse-Re sentinels.
// alphaG and alphaL must come from the existing authority representation.
// The adapter never clips or normalizes fractions.
inline Coefficients mixture(long double alphaG,long double alphaL,
                            Coefficients gas,Coefficients liquid) {
    validate(gas);validate(liquid);
    if (!std::isfinite(alphaG)||!std::isfinite(alphaL)||alphaG<0||alphaL<0||
        alphaG>1||alphaL>1||std::abs(alphaG+alphaL-1)>1e-15L)
        throw std::invalid_argument("INVALID_TRANSPORT_FRACTIONS");
    return {alphaG*gas.shear+alphaL*liquid.shear,
            alphaG*gas.bulk+alphaL*liquid.bulk};
}
inline long double dimensionalFromInverseRe(long double stored,long double scale=1) {
    if (!std::isfinite(stored)||stored<=0||!std::isfinite(scale)||scale<=0)
        throw std::invalid_argument("INVALID_INVERSE_RE_INPUT");
    return scale/stored; // scale=1 dimensional; rho0*U0*L0 nondimensional
}
inline Tensor stress(const Tensor& grad,Coefficients c) {
    validate(c);long double div=0;Tensor tau{};
    for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i) {
        for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)
            if(!std::isfinite(grad[i][j]))throw std::invalid_argument("INVALID_GRADIENT");
        div+=grad[i][i];
    }
    for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i)for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)
        tau[i][j]=c.shear*(grad[i][j]+grad[j][i])+
                    (i==j?(c.bulk-2.L*c.shear/3.L)*div:0.L);
    return tau;
}
inline long double dissipation(const Tensor& grad,Coefficients c) {
    const auto tau=stress(grad,c);long double phi=0;
    for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i)for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)phi+=tau[i][j]*grad[i][j];
    return phi;
}
}

#pragma once
#include "MFCNewtonianTransport.H"
#include <vector>
#include <algorithm>
#include <limits>
namespace mfcTransport {
using Velocity=std::array<long double,3>;
struct WorkState { std::vector<long double> rho,energy;std::vector<Velocity> velocity; };
struct WorkResult { WorkState state;std::vector<long double> heat;long double relativeResidual=0; };
// Periodic, uniform fixed-grid verification operator, with coefficients frozen
// at the substep anchor. Spatially variable positive face coefficients.
// Uses midpoint velocities in both traction and total-energy work flux.
// Dense solve is restricted to small verification fixtures, not production.
inline WorkResult periodicWork(const WorkState& old,const std::vector<Coefficients>& face,
                               long double dx,long double dt) {
    const auto N=old.rho.size();
    if(N<4||N>256||old.energy.size()!=N||old.velocity.size()!=N||face.size()!=N||
       !std::isfinite(dx)||!std::isfinite(dt)||dx<=0||dt<=0)
        throw std::invalid_argument("VISCOUS_FIXTURE_ARGUMENT");
    for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){validate(face[i]);
        if(!std::isfinite(old.rho[i])||old.rho[i]<=0||!std::isfinite(old.energy[i]))throw std::invalid_argument("INVALID_WORK_STATE");
        for(auto u:old.velocity[i])if(!std::isfinite(u))throw std::invalid_argument("INVALID_WORK_VELOCITY");
    }
    WorkResult out;out.state=old;out.heat.assign(N,0);
    std::vector<Velocity> midpoint(N),traction(N);std::vector<long double> work(N,0);
    for(unsigned component=0;component<3;++component){
        std::vector<std::vector<long double>> A(N,std::vector<long double>(N,0));
        std::vector<long double> rhs(N),cf(N);
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)cf[i]=component==0?face[i].longitudinal():face[i].shear;
        const auto h=dt/(2*dx*dx);
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){const auto l=(i+N-1)%N,r=(i+1)%N;
            A[i][i]=old.rho[i]+h*(cf[l]+cf[i]);A[i][l]=-h*cf[l];A[i][r]=-h*cf[i];
            rhs[i]=(old.rho[i]-h*(cf[l]+cf[i]))*old.velocity[i][component]+
                    h*(cf[l]*old.velocity[l][component]+cf[i]*old.velocity[r][component]);
        }
        const auto original=A;const auto originalRhs=rhs;
        for(unsigned k=0;k<N;++k){
            if(!(A[k][k]>0))throw std::runtime_error("INVALID_WORK_PIVOT");
            for(unsigned i=k+1;i<N;++i){auto f=A[i][k]/A[k][k];
                for(unsigned j=k;j<N;++j){A[i][j]-=f*A[k][j];}
                rhs[i]-=f*rhs[k];}
        }
        std::vector<long double> un(N);
        for(int i=int(N)-1;i>=0;--i){auto value=rhs[i];for(unsigned j=i+1;j<N;++j)value-=A[i][j]*un[j];un[i]=value/A[i][i];}
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){long double residual=-originalRhs[i],scale=std::abs(originalRhs[i]);
            for(unsigned j=0;j<N;++j){auto term=original[i][j]*un[j];residual+=term;scale+=std::abs(term);}
            out.relativeResidual=std::max(out.relativeResidual,std::abs(residual)/std::max(1.L,scale));
            out.state.velocity[i][component]=un[i];midpoint[i][component]=(un[i]+old.velocity[i][component])/2;
        }
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){auto r=(i+1)%N;
            traction[i][component]=cf[i]*(midpoint[r][component]-midpoint[i][component])/dx;
            work[i]+=traction[i][component]*(midpoint[r][component]+midpoint[i][component])/2;
        }
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){auto l=(i+N-1)%N,r=(i+1)%N;
            auto dl=midpoint[i][component]-midpoint[l][component],dr=midpoint[r][component]-midpoint[i][component];
            out.heat[i]+=h*(cf[l]*dl*dl+cf[i]*dr*dr);
        }
    }
    for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)out.state.energy[i]+=dt*(work[i]-work[(i+N-1)%N])/dx;
    return out;
}
}

inline double liquid(double T,double P) {
if(!std::isfinite(T)||!std::isfinite(P)||T<280||T>450||P<1000000||P>10000000)throw std::out_of_range("TRANSPORT_FIT_ENVELOPE");

"""Offline transport-only surrogate. Never substitutes the solver EOS."""
from pathlib import Path
import sys,json,hashlib,csv
import numpy as np
from numpy.polynomial import chebyshev as ch
S=Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0,str(S/'property_backend'))
import CoolProp
from CoolProp.CoolProp import PropsSI,PhaseSI,get_global_param_string
configs={'air':('Air',(600.,1200.),(1e5,1e7)),
         'liquid':('n-Decane',(280.,450.),(1e6,1e7))}
def viscos(fluid,T,P):
    return np.array([PropsSI('V','T',float(t),'P',float(p),fluid) for t,p in zip(np.ravel(T),np.ravel(P))]).reshape(np.shape(T))
def mapped(x,b):return 2*(x-b[0])/(b[1]-b[0])-1
output={'scope':'shear viscosity only; retained solver EOS; no PDE qualification claim',
        'CoolPropVersion':CoolProp.__version__,'CoolPropGitRevision':get_global_param_string('gitrevision'),
        'reference':'published CoolProp transport correlations; model-data surrogate, not new experimental calibration',
        'gasAssumption':'dry Air transport independent of retained species EOS; not a reacting-mixture viscosity model',
        'liquidAssumption':'C10H22 interpreted as n-decane',
        'bulkViscosity':{'value':0,'meaning':'explicit Stokes approximation; not inferred from shear data'},
        'acceptanceMaxRelativeSurrogateError':.001,'families':{}}
header=['#pragma once','#include <cmath>','#include <stdexcept>','namespace mfcTransportFit {']
validation=[]
rng=np.random.default_rng(20261007)
for name,(fluid,tb,pb) in configs.items():
    degree=8;nodes=np.cos(np.pi*(np.arange(20)+.5)/20)
    x,y=np.meshgrid(nodes,nodes,indexing='ij');T=tb[0]+(x+1)*(tb[1]-tb[0])/2;P=pb[0]+(y+1)*(pb[1]-pb[0])/2
    truth=viscos(fluid,T,P)
    # CoolProp calls compressed liquid at P>Pc,T<Tc 'supercritical_liquid'.
    if fluid=='n-Decane':assert all(PhaseSI('T',float(t),'P',float(p),fluid) in ('liquid','supercritical_liquid') for t,p in zip(T.ravel(),P.ravel()))
    design=ch.chebvander2d(x.ravel(),y.ravel(),[degree,degree]);coef=np.linalg.lstsq(design,np.log(truth).ravel(),rcond=None)[0].reshape(degree+1,degree+1)
    Tv=np.r_[rng.uniform(*tb,2000),tb[0],tb[0],tb[1],tb[1]];Pv=np.r_[rng.uniform(*pb,2000),pb[0],pb[1],pb[0],pb[1]]
    reference=viscos(fluid,Tv,Pv);fitted=np.exp(ch.chebval2d(mapped(Tv,tb),mapped(Pv,pb),coef))
    error=float(np.max(np.abs(fitted/reference-1)));assert error<=.001,(name,error)
    validation.extend((name,float(t),float(p),float(eta)) for t,p,eta in zip(Tv,Pv,reference))
    output['families'][name]={'fluid':fluid,'temperatureK':tb,'pressurePa':pb,'degree':[degree,degree],
      'logViscosityChebyshevCoefficients':coef.tolist(),'independentValidationPoints':len(Tv),
      'maxRelativeSurrogateError':error,'rangePaS':[float(reference.min()),float(reference.max())],
      'nominal':{'T':800 if name=='air' else 300,'P':3e6,'etaPaS':PropsSI('V','T',800 if name=='air' else 300,'P',3e6,fluid)},'status':'PASS_SURROGATE_ONLY'}
    header+=['inline double '+name+'(double T,double P) {',
      f'if(!std::isfinite(T)||!std::isfinite(P)||T<{tb[0]:.17g}||T>{tb[1]:.17g}||P<{pb[0]:.17g}||P>{pb[1]:.17g})throw std::out_of_range("TRANSPORT_FIT_ENVELOPE");',
      f'const double x=2*(T-{tb[0]:.17g})/{tb[1]-tb[0]:.17g}-1,y=2*(P-{pb[0]:.17g})/{pb[1]-pb[0]:.17g}-1;',
      'double a[9]={1,x},b[9]={1,y};for(int i=2;i<9;++i){a[i]=2*x*a[i-1]-a[i-2];b[i]=2*y*b[i-1]-b[i-2];}',
      'static const double c[9][9]={'+','.join('{'+','.join(format(v,'.17g') for v in row)+'}' for row in coef)+'};',
      'double sum=0;for(int i=0;i<9;++i)for(int j=0;j<9;++j)sum+=c[i][j]*a[i]*b[j];return std::exp(sum);','}']
header+=['}']
(S/'TransportViscosityFit.H').write_text('\n'.join(header)+'\n')
with (S/'transport_validation.csv').open('w',newline='') as f:
    w=csv.writer(f,lineterminator='\n');w.writerow(['family','T','P','referenceEta']);w.writerows(validation)
output['status']='PASS_TRANSPORT_SURROGATE_ONLY'
output['generatedHeaderSHA256']=hashlib.sha256((S/'TransportViscosityFit.H').read_bytes()).hexdigest()
(S/'transport_fit_gate.json').write_text(json.dumps(output,indent=2)+'\n')
print(json.dumps({name:{k:v for k,v in z.items() if k in ('maxRelativeSurrogateError','nominal')} for name,z in output['families'].items()}))

#include "MFCNewtonianTransport.H"
#include "TransportViscosityFit.H"
#include <iostream>
#include <random>
#include <algorithm>
#include <fstream>
#include <sstream>
using namespace mfcTransport;
void check(bool ok){if(!ok)throw std::runtime_error("TRANSPORT_TEST_FAILED");}
bool near(long double a,long double b){return std::abs(a-b)<=1e-14L*std::max(1.L,std::max(std::abs(a),std::abs(b)));}
int main(){
    Coefficients c{2.25L,0};Tensor g{};g[0][0]=2;
    check(near(stress(g,c)[0][0],6));check(near(dissipation(g,c),12));
    g={};g[0][1]=2;check(near(stress(g,c)[0][1],4.5L));check(near(dissipation(g,c),9));
    g[1][0]=-2;check(near(dissipation(g,c),0)); // rigid rotation
    g={};for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i)g[i][i]=2;
    check(near(dissipation(g,{2.25L,0}),0));
    check(near(dissipation(g,{2.25L,1}),36)); // pure bulk compression
    auto mix=mixture(.75L,.25L,{1,2},{5,6});
    check(near(mix.shear,2)&&near(mix.bulk,3));
    check(near(mixture(1,0,{1,2},{5,6}).shear,1));
    check(near(dimensionalFromInverseRe(4),.25L));
    check(near(dimensionalFromInverseRe(4,8),2));
    bool rejected=false;try{mixture(.8L,.3L,{1,0},{1,0});}catch(const std::invalid_argument&){rejected=true;}check(rejected);
    rejected=false;try{stress(g,{-1,0});}catch(const std::invalid_argument&){rejected=true;}check(rejected);
    std::mt19937 gen(73);std::uniform_real_distribution<double>d(-10,10);
    for(unsigned n=0;n<1000;++n){
        for(auto&row:g)for(auto&x:row)x=d(gen);
        c={std::abs(d(gen)),std::abs(d(gen))};auto tau=stress(g,c);
        long double div=g[0][0]+g[1][1]+g[2][2],norm=0;
        for(unsigned i=0;i<3;++i)for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j){
            check(near(tau[i][j],tau[j][i]));
            auto dev=(g[i][j]+g[j][i])/2-(i==j?div/3:0);norm+=dev*dev;
        }
        const auto phi=dissipation(g,c);
        check(phi>=-1e-14L);check(near(phi,2*c.shear*norm+c.bulk*div*div));
    }
    std::ifstream input("transport_validation.csv");check(bool(input));
    std::string line;std::getline(input,line);unsigned count=0;double maxError=0;
    while(std::getline(input,line)){
        std::stringstream stream(line);std::string family,a,b,c0;
        std::getline(stream,family,',');std::getline(stream,a,',');std::getline(stream,b,',');std::getline(stream,c0,',');
        const auto T=std::stod(a),P=std::stod(b),truth=std::stod(c0);
        const auto fit=family=="air"?mfcTransportFit::air(T,P):mfcTransportFit::liquid(T,P);
        maxError=std::max(maxError,std::abs(fit/truth-1));++count;
    }
    check(count==4008&&maxError<=.001);
    rejected=false;try{mfcTransportFit::liquid(1000,3e6);}catch(const std::out_of_range&){rejected=true;}check(rejected);
    std::cout<<"PASS constitutive tests; 1000 independent dissipation identities; "<<count<<" transport validation points; max relative error "<<maxError<<"\n";
}

#include "PeriodicViscousWork.H"
#include <iostream>
#include <random>
using namespace mfcTransport;
void require(bool ok){if(!ok)throw std::runtime_error("VARIABLE_WORK_GATE_FAILED");}
long double kinetic(long double rho,Velocity u){long double k=0;for(auto x:u)k+=rho*x*x/2;return k;}
int main(){
    constexpr unsigned N=16;const long double dx=1.L/N,dt=.002L,pi=std::acos(-1.L);
    std::mt19937 random(471);std::uniform_real_distribution<double>d(.1,2);
    long double worstIdentity=0,worstConservation=0,worstLinear=0;
    for(unsigned test=0;test<100;++test){
        WorkState a;a.rho.resize(N);a.energy.resize(N);a.velocity.resize(N);std::vector<Coefficients> face(N);
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){a.rho[i]=d(random);face[i]={d(random),d(random)};
            for(auto&u:a.velocity[i])u=d(random)-1;
            a.energy[i]=5+kinetic(a.rho[i],a.velocity[i]);}
        const auto z=periodicWork(a,face,dx,dt);Velocity dm{};long double de=0;
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i){require(a.rho[i]==z.state.rho[i]);require(z.heat[i]>=0);
            const auto heat=z.state.energy[i]-a.energy[i]-kinetic(a.rho[i],z.state.velocity[i])+kinetic(a.rho[i],a.velocity[i]);
            worstIdentity=std::max(worstIdentity,std::abs(heat-z.heat[i]));
            require(std::abs(heat-z.heat[i])<1e-13L);
            de+=dx*(z.state.energy[i]-a.energy[i]);
            for(unsigned j=0;j<3;++j)dm[j]+=dx*a.rho[i]*(z.state.velocity[i][j]-a.velocity[i][j]);
        }
        worstConservation=std::max(worstConservation,std::abs(de));for(auto x:dm){require(std::abs(x)<1e-13L);worstConservation=std::max(worstConservation,std::abs(x));}
        require(std::abs(de)<1e-13L);worstLinear=std::max(worstLinear,z.relativeResidual);
    }
    // Independent Fourier eigenmode: compare normal and transverse diffusion.
    for(unsigned component=0;component<3;++component){
        WorkState a;a.rho.assign(N,2);a.energy.assign(N,10);a.velocity.resize(N);
        std::vector<Coefficients> face(N,{.3L,.2L});
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)a.velocity[i][component]=std::sin(2*pi*i/N);
        auto z=periodicWork(a,face,dx,dt);auto coefficient=component==0?.6L:.3L;
        auto lambda=4*coefficient/2/(dx*dx)*std::pow(std::sin(pi/N),2);
        auto amplification=(1-dt*lambda/2)/(1+dt*lambda/2);
        for(unsigned i=0;i<N;++i)require(std::abs(z.state.velocity[i][component]-amplification*a.velocity[i][component])<1e-13L);
    }
    std::cout<<"PASS 100 variable-coefficient vector work fixtures; 3 analytic Fourier modes; max cell work identity "<<double(worstIdentity)<<"; max conservation "<<double(worstConservation)<<"; max linear residual "<<double(worstLinear)<<"\n";
}
